#!/usr/bin/env node

/**
 * Alma CLI — manage Alma app settings and status from the command line.
 */
import { execSync as _execSync } from 'child_process';
import _os from 'os';
import _path from 'path';
import _fs from 'fs';
import { fileURLToPath as _fileURLToPath } from 'url';
const BASE_URL = process.env.ALMA_API_URL || 'http://localhost:23001';
const __alma_filename = _fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __alma_dirname = _path.dirname(__alma_filename);

/** Get the best package runner: npx, bunx, or bundled bun */
let _cachedRunner;
function getPackageRunner() {
  if (_cachedRunner !== undefined) return _cachedRunner;
  function tryCmd(cmd) {
    try {
      _execSync(`${cmd} --version`, {
        stdio: 'ignore'
      });
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }
  if (tryCmd('npx')) {
    _cachedRunner = 'npx';
    return _cachedRunner;
  }
  if (tryCmd('bunx')) {
    _cachedRunner = 'bunx';
    return _cachedRunner;
  }
  // Try Alma's bundled bun, then a user-installed one.
  const bunExe = process.platform === 'win32' ? 'bun.exe' : 'bun';
  const bundledPaths = [
  // Packaged app on every platform: this file is <resources>/cli/alma and
  // electron-builder puts bun at <resources>/bun/ (see extraResources).
  _path.join(__alma_dirname, '..', 'bun', bunExe),
  // Source checkout: bin/alma next to vendor/bun/<platform>-<arch>/.
  _path.join(__alma_dirname, '..', 'vendor', 'bun', `${process.platform}-${process.arch}`, bunExe), _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.bun', 'bin', bunExe)];
  for (const p of bundledPaths) {
    if (_fs.existsSync(p)) {
      _cachedRunner = `"${p}" x`;
      return _cachedRunner;
    }
  }
  _cachedRunner = null;
  return _cachedRunner;
}
async function api(method, path, body) {
  const opts = {
    method,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json'
    }
  };
  if (body !== undefined) opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${path}`, opts);
    if (!resp.ok) {
      const text = await resp.text().catch(() => '');
      console.error(`Error: HTTP ${resp.status} ${resp.statusText}${text ? ' — ' + text : ''}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const ct = resp.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (ct.includes('application/json')) return await resp.json();
    return await resp.text();
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`Error: Cannot connect to Alma at ${BASE_URL}`);
    console.error('Is Alma running? Start it with: npm run dev (in the alma directory)');
    process.exit(1);
  }
}
async function apiRaw(method, path) {
  const opts = {
    method
  };
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${path}`, opts);
    if (!resp.ok) {
      console.error(`Error: HTTP ${resp.status} ${resp.statusText}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    return resp;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`Error: Cannot connect to Alma at ${BASE_URL}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

/**
 * Split a config path into segments, expanding `[n]` array indices:
 * "agents.profiles[5].model" → ['agents', 'profiles', 5, 'model'].
 * Without this, "profiles[5]" used to become a literal object key.
 */
function parsePathSegments(path) {
  const segments = [];
  for (const part of path.split('.')) {
    const m = part.match(/^([^[\]]+)((?:\[\d+\])+)$/);
    if (m) {
      segments.push(m[1]);
      for (const idx of m[2].match(/\d+/g)) {
        segments.push(parseInt(idx, 10));
      }
    } else {
      segments.push(part);
    }
  }
  return segments;
}
function getNestedValue(obj, path) {
  if (!path) return obj;
  let val = obj;
  for (const part of parsePathSegments(path)) {
    if (val && typeof val === 'object') {
      val = val[part];
    } else {
      return undefined;
    }
  }
  return val;
}
function setNestedValue(obj, path, value) {
  const parts = parsePathSegments(path);
  let target = obj;
  for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) {
    const key = parts[i];
    const nextIsIndex = typeof parts[i + 1] === 'number';
    if (typeof key === 'number' && !Array.isArray(target)) {
      throw new Error(`Path segment [${key}] expects an array, but found ${typeof target}`);
    }
    if (!target[key] || typeof target[key] !== 'object') {
      target[key] = nextIsIndex ? [] : {};
    }
    target = target[key];
  }
  const last = parts[parts.length - 1];
  if (typeof last === 'number' && !Array.isArray(target)) {
    throw new Error(`Path segment [${last}] expects an array, but found ${typeof target}`);
  }
  target[last] = value;
  return obj;
}
function parseValue(str) {
  if (str === 'true') return true;
  if (str === 'false') return false;
  if (str === 'null') return null;
  if (/^-?\d+$/.test(str)) return parseInt(str, 10);
  if (/^-?\d+\.\d+$/.test(str)) return parseFloat(str);
  // JSON objects/arrays: parse so `alma config set agents "$json"` stores a real
  // object instead of a string (which silently corrupted the settings tree).
  if (/^[[{]/.test(str.trim())) {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(str);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`❌ Value looks like JSON but failed to parse: ${err.message}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  }
  return str;
}
function prettyPrint(val) {
  if (val === undefined) {
    console.log('(not set)');
  } else if (typeof val === 'object') {
    console.log(JSON.stringify(val, null, 2));
  } else {
    console.log(String(val));
  }
}
function formatDate(d) {
  if (!d) return '';
  return new Date(d).toLocaleString();
}
function truncate(str, len = 60) {
  if (!str) return '';
  return str.length > len ? str.slice(0, len - 1) + '…' : str;
}

/**
 * `--deliver-to` names an EXTERNAL chat (Telegram chat/group id — what
 * ALMA_CHAT_ID carries when a Telegram message triggered the turn), never an
 * Alma thread. Agents kept passing ALMA_THREAD_ID here and the job silently
 * "delivered" into the void, so refuse a value that resolves to a thread and
 * warn on anything that does not look like a Telegram target.
 */
async function validateDeliverTo(value, platform = 'telegram') {
  if (platform !== 'telegram' && platform !== 'discord') {
    console.error('❌ --delivery-platform must be telegram or discord');
    process.exit(1);
  }
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/threads/${encodeURIComponent(value)}`);
    if (resp.ok) {
      console.error(`❌ --deliver-to ${value} is an Alma thread id, not a chat id.`);
      console.error('   --deliver-to takes an external chat/channel id, never ALMA_THREAD_ID. Use --delivery-platform discord only for an explicitly authorized Discord target.');
      console.error('   To run the job inside that thread instead, use: --mode main --thread-id ' + value);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  } catch {
    // Cannot reach Alma here; api() below will report that properly.
  }
  if (platform === 'discord' && !/^\d+$/.test(value)) {
    console.error('❌ Discord --deliver-to must be a channel id');
    process.exit(1);
  }
  if (platform === 'telegram' && !/^-?\d+$/.test(value) && !/^@[A-Za-z0-9_]{4,}$/.test(value)) {
    console.error(`⚠️  --deliver-to ${value} does not look like a Telegram chat id (numeric, negative for groups) or @channel — delivery may fail.`);
  }
}
function resolveCronJob(jobs, id) {
  const exactMatch = jobs.find(j => j.id === id);
  if (exactMatch) return exactMatch;
  const prefixMatches = jobs.filter(j => j.id.startsWith(id));
  if (prefixMatches.length === 1) return prefixMatches[0];
  if (prefixMatches.length > 1) {
    const matches = prefixMatches.map(j => `${j.id} (${j.name})`).join(', ');
    console.error(`Ambiguous job id: ${id}. Matches: ${matches}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  console.error(`Job not found: ${id}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

// Gemini-only ranking: this list is applied to ids fetched from Google's own
// /models endpoint (the `alma image-model` command and the last-resort Google
// path). The cross-provider "Auto" order — which is what `alma image generate`
// and the settings picker actually use — lives in
// electron/image-model-selection.ts (AUTO_IMAGE_MODEL_PRIORITY).
const IMAGE_MODEL_PRIORITY = ['nano-banana-2', 'nano-banana-pro', 'nano-banana', 'gemini-3-pro-image', 'gemini-2.5-flash-image', 'gemini-2.0-flash'];
async function getEnabledGoogleProvider() {
  const providers = await api('GET', '/api/providers');
  return (providers || []).find(p => p.type === 'google' && p.apiKey && p.enabled !== false);
}
async function fetchGeminiImageModelIds(provider) {
  const baseUrl = (provider.baseURL || 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com').replace(/\/+$/, '');
  const apiPath = baseUrl.endsWith('/v1beta') ? '' : '/v1beta';
  const resp = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${apiPath}/models?key=${provider.apiKey}`);
  if (!resp.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Failed to fetch Gemini models: HTTP ${resp.status}`);
  }
  const data = await resp.json();
  return (data.models || []).filter(m => m.supportedGenerationMethods?.includes('generateContent') && (m.name.includes('image') || m.name.includes('nano-banana') || m.name.includes('imagen')) && !m.name.includes('imagen')).map(m => m.name.replace(/^models\//, ''));
}
function pickBestImageModel(modelIds) {
  for (const prefix of IMAGE_MODEL_PRIORITY) {
    const match = modelIds.find(id => id.includes(prefix));
    if (match) return match;
  }
  return modelIds[0] || null;
}

// A skill run is a Bash call: the prompt that would justify `--model` is only
// visible in the conversation, never in argv. So when an explicit `--model`
// contradicts the user's configured image model, ask the conversation whether
// the user actually named a model. If their own words say nothing about models,
// the agent volunteered the flag and the configured model wins — that is exactly
// the complaint in alma-releases#65 ("use my configured model unless I specify
// one"). Fails OPEN (override honored) when there is no thread to ask, so a
// human running the CLI by hand is never second-guessed.
const MODEL_TOKEN_STOPWORDS = new Set(['image', 'images', 'img', 'model', 'models', 'preview', 'latest', 'stable', 'beta', 'exp', 'gen', 'generate', 'generation', 'free', 'plugin', 'auto']);

// "Compare them all", "试试别的模型" — the user is talking about model choice
// even without naming one, so an override is theirs, not volunteered.
const MODEL_INTENT_PATTERNS = [/\bmodels?\b/, /模型/, /\bcompare\b/, /对比/, /比较/];
function modelMatchTokens(modelId) {
  return modelId.toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9.]+/).filter(token => token.length >= 3 && !MODEL_TOKEN_STOPWORDS.has(token) && !/^[0-9.]+$/.test(token));
}
function textNamesModel(text, modelId) {
  const haystack = (text || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!haystack) return false;
  if (MODEL_INTENT_PATTERNS.some(re => re.test(haystack))) return true;
  const tokens = modelMatchTokens(modelId);
  // A bare vendor prefix ("openrouter") is not the user naming a model, so the
  // tokens that count are the ones after the provider id.
  const modelPart = modelId.includes(':') ? modelId.slice(modelId.indexOf(':') + 1) : modelId;
  const modelTokens = modelMatchTokens(modelPart);
  const candidates = modelTokens.length > 0 ? modelTokens : tokens;
  return candidates.some(token => haystack.includes(token));
}
async function latestUserMessageText() {
  const threadId = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
  if (!threadId) return null;
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/threads/${encodeURIComponent(threadId)}/messages?limit=10`);
    if (!resp.ok) return null;
    const rows = await resp.json();
    if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return null;
    for (let i = rows.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const row = rows[i];
      let msg = row;
      try {
        msg = typeof row.message === 'string' ? JSON.parse(row.message) : row.message || row;
      } catch {
        msg = row;
      }
      const role = msg && msg.role || row.role;
      if (role !== 'user') continue;
      let text = '';
      if (msg && Array.isArray(msg.parts)) {
        text = msg.parts.filter(part => part && part.type === 'text' && typeof part.text === 'string').map(part => part.text).join(' ');
      } else if (msg && typeof msg.content === 'string') {
        text = msg.content;
      }
      if (text.trim()) return text;
    }
    return null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
function mimeForImagePath(p) {
  const ext = p.toLowerCase();
  if (ext.endsWith('.png')) return 'image/png';
  if (ext.endsWith('.gif')) return 'image/gif';
  if (ext.endsWith('.webp')) return 'image/webp';
  return 'image/jpeg';
}

// Generate an image via a non-Google provider. OpenRouter has its own unified
// /images JSON endpoint (including input_references); the other compatible
// providers use /images/edits or /images/generations. Writes the result to a
// temp file, prints its path on stdout, and exits the process.
// Map an aspect-ratio string to an OpenAI /v1/images `size` value. gpt-image-1 only accepts
// 1024x1024 / 1536x1024 / 1024x1536 / auto, so we bucket by orientation. Empty/"auto" → auto.
function aspectToOpenAISize(aspect) {
  if (!aspect || aspect === 'auto') return 'auto';
  const m = String(aspect).match(/^(\d+)\s*[:x]\s*(\d+)$/);
  if (!m) return 'auto';
  const w = +m[1];
  const h = +m[2];
  if (w === h) return '1024x1024';
  return w > h ? '1536x1024' : '1024x1536';
}

// Generate/edit an image via a plugin provider (e.g. grok-imagine through the
// SuperGrok OAuth plugin). The plugin's authenticated fetch only lives inside
// the app process, so this delegates to the server's /api/image/generate
// endpoint instead of reading credentials itself. Writes the result to a temp
// file, prints its path on stdout, and exits the process.
async function generateImageViaPlugin(model, {
  sub,
  prompt,
  editPath,
  referencePaths,
  aspect
}) {
  const inputPaths = sub === 'edit' ? [editPath] : (referencePaths || []).filter(p => _fs.existsSync(p));
  const images = inputPaths.map(p => {
    const mime = mimeForImagePath(p);
    return `data:${mime};base64,${_fs.readFileSync(p).toString('base64')}`;
  });
  console.error(`[Image] Using plugin model: ${model}${images.length ? ` with ${images.length} input image(s)` : ''}`);
  const resp = await api('POST', '/api/image/generate', {
    model,
    prompt,
    images,
    ...(aspect && aspect !== 'auto' ? {
      aspectRatio: aspect
    } : {})
  });
  const dataUrl = resp && resp.dataUrl;
  const comma = typeof dataUrl === 'string' ? dataUrl.indexOf(',') : -1;
  if (comma < 0) {
    console.error('❌ Image generation failed: empty response from app');
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const mime = (dataUrl.match(/^data:([^;,]+)/) || [])[1] || 'image/png';
  const ext = (mime.split('/')[1] || 'png').replace('jpeg', 'jpg');
  const outPath = _path.join(_os.tmpdir(), `alma-gen-${Date.now()}-0.${ext}`);
  _fs.writeFileSync(outPath, Buffer.from(dataUrl.slice(comma + 1), 'base64'));
  console.log(outPath);
  process.exit(0);
}
async function generateImageViaOpenAI(provider, modelId, {
  sub,
  prompt,
  editPath,
  referencePaths,
  size,
  aspect
}) {
  const defaultBaseURL = provider.type === 'openrouter' ? 'https://openrouter.ai/api/v1' : 'https://api.openai.com/v1';
  const baseURL = (provider.baseURL || defaultBaseURL).replace(/\/+$/, '');
  // Edit mode: the source image is the input. Generate mode: any --reference images.
  const inputPaths = sub === 'edit' ? [editPath] : (referencePaths || []).filter(p => _fs.existsSync(p));
  const headers = {};
  if (provider.apiKey) headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${provider.apiKey}`;
  if (provider.customHeaders) {
    try {
      Object.assign(headers, JSON.parse(provider.customHeaders));
    } catch {
      console.error('[Image] Ignoring invalid provider custom headers');
    }
  }
  console.error(`[Image] Using ${provider.name || provider.type} model: ${modelId} (OpenAI-compatible)`);
  let resp;
  try {
    if (provider.type === 'openrouter') {
      const inputReferences = inputPaths.map(p => {
        const mime = mimeForImagePath(p);
        return {
          type: 'image_url',
          image_url: {
            url: `data:${mime};base64,${_fs.readFileSync(p).toString('base64')}`
          }
        };
      });
      console.error(`Generating via ${baseURL}/images${inputReferences.length ? ` with ${inputReferences.length} input image(s)` : ''}...`);
      resp = await fetch(`${baseURL}/images`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          ...headers,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: modelId,
          prompt,
          ...(inputReferences.length ? {
            input_references: inputReferences
          } : {}),
          ...(aspect && aspect !== 'auto' ? {
            aspect_ratio: aspect
          } : {})
        })
      });
    } else if (inputPaths.length > 0) {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('model', modelId);
      formData.append('prompt', prompt);
      if (size && size !== 'auto') formData.append('size', size);
      // OpenAI requires `image[]` array syntax for multiple images; a bare
      // repeated `image` field returns 400 "Duplicate parameter".
      const imageField = inputPaths.length > 1 ? 'image[]' : 'image';
      for (let i = 0; i < inputPaths.length; i++) {
        const p = inputPaths[i];
        const mime = mimeForImagePath(p);
        const ext = mime.split('/')[1] || 'png';
        const blob = new Blob([_fs.readFileSync(p)], {
          type: mime
        });
        formData.append(imageField, blob, `input-${i}.${ext}`);
      }
      console.error(`Generating (edit) via ${baseURL}/images/edits with ${inputPaths.length} input image(s)...`);
      resp = await fetch(`${baseURL}/images/edits`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers,
        body: formData
      });
    } else {
      console.error(`Generating via ${baseURL}/images/generations...`);
      resp = await fetch(`${baseURL}/images/generations`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          ...headers,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: modelId,
          prompt,
          ...(size && size !== 'auto' ? {
            size
          } : {})
        })
      });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`❌ Image request failed: ${err.message || err}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  if (!resp.ok) {
    const errorBody = await resp.text().catch(() => resp.statusText);
    console.error(`❌ API error: ${resp.status} ${errorBody}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const data = await resp.json();
  const item = data.data?.[0];
  if (!item) {
    console.error('❌ No image returned from API');
    process.exit(1);
  }
  let buffer;
  let mediaType = item.media_type || 'image/png';
  if (item.b64_json) {
    buffer = Buffer.from(item.b64_json, 'base64');
  } else if (item.url) {
    const imgResp = await fetch(item.url);
    if (!imgResp.ok) {
      console.error(`❌ Failed to download generated image: HTTP ${imgResp.status}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    buffer = Buffer.from(await imgResp.arrayBuffer());
    mediaType = imgResp.headers.get('content-type') || mediaType;
  } else {
    console.error('❌ API returned no image data (no url or b64_json)');
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const ext = (mediaType.split('/')[1] || 'png').split(';')[0].replace('jpeg', 'jpg');
  const outPath = _path.join(_os.tmpdir(), `alma-gen-${Date.now()}-0.${ext}`);
  _fs.writeFileSync(outPath, buffer);
  console.log(outPath);
  process.exit(0);
}
async function main() {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const cmd = args[0];
  if (!cmd || cmd === 'help' || cmd === '--help' || cmd === '-h') {
    console.log(`Alma CLI — manage your Alma app

Usage:
  alma status                          Check if Alma is running
  alma health                          Detailed health check

  alma config get [path]               Read settings (e.g., "tts.auto")
  alma config set <path> <value>       Update a setting
  alma config list                     List all settings (JSON)

  alma providers                       List configured providers
  alma provider add <name> <type>      Add a new provider (flags: --api-key, --base-url, --models)
  alma provider delete <id>            Remove a provider
  alma providers <id> models           List models for a provider
  alma models                          List all available models
  alma model set <provider:model>      Set default model

  alma threads [limit|--all] [--project id] [--json]  List visible thread metadata (source discovery only)
  alma thread create <title> [--project <id>]  Create a new thread (optionally in a project)
  alma thread delete <id>              Delete a thread
  alma thread rename [--thread <id>] <title>  Rename a thread (default: this thread, $ALMA_THREAD_ID)
  alma thread search <query> [--project <id>] [--json]  Discover threads with project metadata
  alma thread compact <id>             Compact a thread's context
  alma thread messages <id> [limit] [--full] [--offset n] [--json]  Read message page (default: 80-char previews)
  alma thread switch <id>              Switch current chat to a different thread

  alma project list                    List projects (workspaces) with thread counts
  alma project create <name> [--path <dir>]  Create a new project (path defaults to ~/AlmaProjects/<slug>)
  alma project show <id>               Show project details (path, thread count, etc.)
  alma project delete <id>             Delete an empty project
  alma project current                 Show current thread's project (uses $ALMA_THREAD_ID)
  alma project card <id|current>       Show the project card (brief/focus/resume + config)
  alma project card propose <id|current> --focus ".." --reason ".."  Suggest a card update (user confirms in UI)
  alma project card draft <id|current> [--hint "一两句话"]  Draft brief/focus via the tool model (lands as a proposal)
  alma project card set <id> ...       Confirmed card write (user only; refused from model shells)
  alma project config <id|current>     Show project run configuration (defaults & boundary)
  alma project config set <id> --json '{...}'  Set run configuration (user only)
  alma project switch <id> [title]     Create a new thread in <id> and switch the current chat to it
  alma project assign <threadId> <id>  Move a thread into a project (no new thread created)
                                       Tip: in an uncategorized thread, run
                                         alma project create <name>
                                         alma project assign $ALMA_THREAD_ID <id>

  alma plan template                   Print an example plan.json (task graph format)
  alma plan set --file <f|->           Create the workspace plan from JSON (--force to replace)
  alma plan status [--json]            Show the task graph with block statuses
  alma plan claim                      Claim the next work item, print its work packet
  alma plan show <taskId:blockId>      Inspect one block (status, artifacts)
  alma plan submit <ref> --report <f|->   Submit an implementation report, mark block done
  alma plan review <ref> --verdict <passed|needs_changes> --notes <text|f|->  Close a review gate
  alma plan resolve --report <f|->     Resolve open review feedback (rework report)
  alma plan block <ref> --reason <t>   Mark a block externally blocked (unblock to undo)
  alma plan reset|archive|delete       Reset state / archive plan / delete plan

  alma ref kinds                       List reference kinds (what can be @-mentioned)
  alma ref search <query> [--kind k]   Find objects → alma:// URIs (--json for machine output)
  alma ref resolve <uri> [--full]      Describe an object (card JSON; --full adds body/transcript/file text)
  alma ref backlinks <uri>             Messages that mention an object (双链 反向链接)
  alma ref outlinks <uri>              What a message mentions
  alma ref related <uri>               Objects manually/body-linked to this one (双链 sibling edges)
  alma ref link <fromUri> <toUri> [label]   Create an object↔object relation (e.g. skill→prompt)
  alma ref unlink <fromUri> <toUri>    Remove a manual relation
  alma ref graph <uri> [--depth n]     Neighbourhood graph (nodes + edges)
  alma ref snippet <text|-> [--title]  Capture text as a snippet object in this thread → URI
  alma ref open <uri>                  Navigate the app to an object

  alma workspace pins <id>             List a project's pinned references (置顶)
  alma workspace pin <id> <uri> [label]   Pin an object to a project (user only)
  alma workspace unpin <id> <uri>      Remove a project pin (user only)
  alma theme list|current              Color schemes (74 bundled base46 themes)
  alma theme set <id> [--light]        Apply a theme (dark slot by default)
  alma theme random [--light]          Switch to a random theme
  alma host list                       Registered SSH remote hosts
  alma host test <id|name|all>         Test connectivity (one host or all)
  alma mail accounts                   Connected email accounts + pending counts
  alma mail list [--status pending]    Inbox items (原料仓)
  alma mail show <id>                  Full email: headers, body, attachment paths
  alma mail draft <id> --to <a> --subject <s> --body-file -   Save a reply/forward draft
  alma mail send <id>                  Send the stored draft (user only — the model drafts, you confirm)
  alma mail dismiss|done|reopen <id>   Move an inbox item between states
  alma mail check <account-id>         Fetch new mail now
  alma ref context [--thread <id>]     Show the <referenced_objects> block the model receives for a thread
  alma ref token <uri> [label]         Print the inline mention token @[label](uri)

  alma agent list                      List managed agent profiles
  alma agent create <name> --prompt <f|-> [--summary <t>] [--focus a,b] [--model <provider:model>] [--execution-mode <lane>]
                                       Create a custom managed agent (usable via Task agent_id / @agent)
  alma agent delete <id>               Delete a custom managed agent profile
  alma skill create <name> --file <f|->  Install a personal skill from a full SKILL.md (frontmatter required)

  alma memory list                     List all memories
  alma memory search <query>           Search memories
  alma memory add <content>            Add a new memory
  alma memory delete <id>              Delete a memory
  alma memory stats                    Memory statistics

  alma voices                          List available TTS voices

  alma image models                    List available image generation models
  alma image generate ...              Generate an image (supports --model, --reference, --aspect)
  alma image edit ...                  Edit an image (supports --model, --aspect)

  alma skill list                      List installed skills
  alma skill search <query>            Search skills (via skills.sh)
  alma skill find <query>              Alias for search
  alma skill install <source>          Install a skill (skills.sh or GitHub)
  alma skill update                    Check and update installed skills
  alma skill uninstall <name>          Remove an installed skill

  alma tool list                       List invocable tools (name, description, JSON schema)
  alma tool call <name> [jsonArgs]     Invoke a tool directly, e.g. alma tool call Read '{"file_path":"/tmp/x"}'

  alma heartbeat [status]               Heartbeat agent status
  alma heartbeat config                 Show heartbeat config
  alma heartbeat enable                 Enable heartbeat
  alma heartbeat disable                Disable heartbeat
  alma heartbeat interval <minutes>       Set heartbeat interval in minutes
  alma heartbeat patrol <enable|disable|config>  Group chat proactive patrol

  alma activity status                  Recorder status + storage usage
  alma activity start                   Start recording (captures + OCR + input)
  alma activity stop                    Stop recording
  alma activity config [get|set K V]    Read / update recorder config
  alma activity sessions [limit]        List recent activity sessions
  alma activity show <id>               Show full session detail (events, snapshots, OCR)
  alma activity search <query>          Keyword search across OCR text
  alma activity search semantic <q>     Semantic (embedding) search across OCR text
  alma activity summary <daily|weekly> [date] [--narrative]  Generate summary
  alma activity analyze <sessionId>     Trigger LLM analysis for one session
  alma activity digest [--lookback MIN] [--max-analyzed N]  Rich markdown digest with OCR excerpts (for AI use)
  alma activity report [date|today|yesterday] [--force] [--skeleton] [--json]  Thematic day-level work journal (PRs, people, decisions)

  alma cron list                        List cron jobs
  alma cron add <name> <type> <sched>   Add a cron job (type: at|every|cron)
  alma cron remove <id>                 Remove a cron job (its run history is kept)
  alma cron run <id>                    Run a cron job now
  alma cron enable <id>                 Enable a cron job
  alma cron disable <id>                Disable a cron job
  alma cron history <id>                Show job run history (start, duration, result; ⏳ = still running)

  alma secret list                      List stored secrets (names only — values are write-only)
  alma secret set <NAME> [--note ".."] [--no-env]  Store a secret (value from stdin); env:on injects $NAME into agent shells
  alma secret env <NAME> on|off         Toggle env injection for a secret
  alma secret rm <NAME>                 Delete a secret

  alma usage                           Usage statistics
  alma export                          Export all data to file
  alma import <file>                   Import data from file

  alma workspace list                  List workspaces
  alma workspace set <id> <path>       Update workspace path

  alma soul                            Show SOUL.md content
  alma soul edit                       Open SOUL.md path for editing
  alma soul set <content>              Replace SOUL.md content
  alma soul append-trait <desc>        Add an evolved personality trait

  alma version                         Show Alma version
  alma update [check|download|install]  Check/download/install updates
  alma dm <userId> <message>            Send a private DM to a Telegram user
  alma msg delete <chatId> <messageId>  Delete (retract) a message
  alma sing generate "lyrics/desc"      Generate a song (Suno via PiAPI)
  alma sing config <piapi-api-key>      Configure PiAPI API key
  alma emotion status                   Show current emotion state
  alma emotion set-base <mood> <energy> <valence> <desc>
  alma emotion set-context <chatId> <mood> <valence> <trigger>
  alma emotion get [chatId]             Get blended emotion (JSON)

  alma browser status                   Chrome Relay connection status
  alma browser tabs                     List open Chrome tabs
  alma browser open [url]               Open a new tab (optionally with URL)
  alma browser goto <tabId> <url>       Navigate tab to URL
  alma browser click <tabId> <selector> Click element by CSS selector
  alma browser type <tabId> <sel> <text> [--enter]  Type into input field
  alma browser screenshot [tabId]       Take screenshot (prints file path)

  alma capture [options]                Screenshot the Alma window itself (prints file path)
    --no-redact                         Keep sidebar thread titles readable (default: redacted)
    --bg <preset|color|transparent|wallpaper>  Background: bundled photo (sea/canyon/mist/highland/ocean) or color field (silver/azure/indigo/ember/graphite),
                                        CSS color, transparent, or the saved custom wallpaper
    --padding <px>                      Padding around the window (0-160)
    --no-shadow                         Skip the window drop shadow
    -o, --output <path>                 Write PNG to this path (default: temp file)
  alma browser read <tabId>             Read page content as markdown
  alma browser read-dom <tabId>         List interactive DOM elements (JSON)
  alma browser eval <tabId> <code>      Execute JavaScript in page
  alma browser scroll <tabId> <up|down> [amount]  Scroll page
  alma browser back <tabId>             Go back in history
  alma browser forward <tabId>          Go forward in history

  alma cu <subcommand>                  macOS desktop automation (AX-based, no cursor movement)
  alma cu help                          Show computer-use help

  alma calendar [events] [options]      Query calendar events (macOS only)
  alma calendar add [options]           Create a calendar event
  alma calendar calendars [--json]      List available calendars
  alma calendar help                    Show calendar help

  alma tui                              Launch interactive TUI (Terminal UI)
  alma run [options] [prompt]           AI completion via full chat pipeline (memory, tools, SOUL, plugins)
    -m, --model <provider:model>        Override default model (provider can be name, type, or ID)
    -s, --system <prompt>               Prepend a system instruction to message
    -t, --temperature <n>               Set temperature (0-2)
    --raw                               Strip markdown from output
    --no-stream                         Buffer output, print at end
    -l, --list-models                   List available models and exit
    -v, --verbose                       Show thinking and tool calls (auto-enabled in TTY)
    --tools <list|auto|none>            Tools offered to the model. Default: the coding set
                                        (Bash,BashOutput,KillShell,Read,Write,Edit,Glob,Grep);
                                        "auto" lets the tool model pick, "none" sends no selection

  alma goal <condition>                Keep this thread working until a condition holds
  alma goal                            Show the goal, its turns and the last verdict
  alma goal clear                      Stop before the condition is met
  alma loop <interval> <prompt>        Re-run a prompt on this thread ("5m check the deploy")
  alma loop                            Show the loop, its interval and iteration count
  alma loop stop                       Stop it

  alma help                            Show this help

Environment:
  ALMA_API_URL    API base URL (default: http://localhost:23001)`);
    return;
  }

  // ── Tools (Programmatic Tool Calling) ───────────────────
  // Inside a run_script sandbox, ALMA_PTC_SESSION/ALMA_PTC_TOKEN scope the call
  // to that session's tools; outside, calls resolve against all available tools.
  if (cmd === 'tool') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    const ptcSession = process.env.ALMA_PTC_SESSION;
    const ptcToken = process.env.ALMA_PTC_TOKEN;
    if (subcmd === 'list') {
      const qs = ptcSession ? `?session=${encodeURIComponent(ptcSession)}&token=${encodeURIComponent(ptcToken || '')}` : '';
      const data = await api('GET', `/api/tools/list${qs}`);
      prettyPrint(data.tools ?? data);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'call') {
      const name = args[2];
      if (!name) {
        console.error('Usage: alma tool call <name> [jsonArgs]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let toolArgs = {};
      if (args[3] !== undefined) {
        try {
          toolArgs = JSON.parse(args[3]);
        } catch {
          console.error(`Error: args must be valid JSON. Got: ${args[3]}`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
      }
      const body = {
        name,
        args: toolArgs
      };
      if (ptcSession) {
        body.session = ptcSession;
        body.token = ptcToken;
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/tools/invoke', body);
      if (data && data.error) {
        console.error(`Error: ${data.error}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const result = data && 'result' in data ? data.result : data;
      if (typeof result === 'string') console.log(result);else console.log(JSON.stringify(result));
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma tool <list|call>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Status ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'status') {
    try {
      await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/settings`);
      console.log(`✅ Alma is running at ${BASE_URL}`);
    } catch {
      console.log(`❌ Alma is not running at ${BASE_URL}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    // If running inside a thread, also surface current thread + project
    const tid = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
    if (tid) {
      try {
        const thread = await api('GET', `/api/threads/${tid}`);
        const title = thread?.title || '(untitled)';
        console.log(`💬 Thread:  ${tid}  "${title}"`);
        if (thread?.workspaceId) {
          try {
            const ws = await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${thread.workspaceId}`);
            if (ws?.isTemporary) {
              console.log(`📁 Project: (uncategorized — temporary workspace)`);
            } else {
              console.log(`📁 Project: ${ws?.name || '(unknown)'}  [${thread.workspaceId}]`);
            }
          } catch {
            console.log(`📁 Project: (workspace ${thread.workspaceId} not found)`);
          }
        } else {
          console.log(`📁 Project: (uncategorized)`);
        }
      } catch {
        // Thread lookup failed — silently skip
      }
    }
    return;
  }

  // ── Health ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'health') {
    const data = await api('GET', '/api/health');
    prettyPrint(data);
    return;
  }

  // ── Version ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'version') {
    const path = await import('path');
    const fs = await import('fs');
    const {
      fileURLToPath
    } = await import('url');
    const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    try {
      const pkg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(dir, '..', 'package.json'), 'utf-8'));
      console.log(`Alma v${pkg.version}`);
      return;
    } catch {
      // Not a source checkout — try the alma-server layout next.
    }
    try {
      // alma-server: <root>/resources/cli/alma, with <root>/VERSION.
      const version = fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(dir, '..', '..', 'VERSION'), 'utf-8').trim();
      if (version) {
        console.log(`Alma v${version}`);
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      // No VERSION file either — fall through to unknown.
    }
    console.log('Alma (version unknown)');
    return;
  }

  // ── Update ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'update') {
    const subcmd = args[1] || 'check';
    if (subcmd === 'check') {
      console.log('🔍 Checking for updates...');
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/update/check`, {
          method: 'POST'
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.status === 'update-available') {
          console.log(`🎉 New version available: v${data.version}`);
          console.log('   Run `alma update download` to download it.');
        } else if (data.status === 'update-downloaded') {
          console.log(`✅ Update v${data.version} already downloaded.`);
          console.log('   Run `alma update install` to install and restart.');
        } else if (data.status === 'update-not-available') {
          console.log('✅ Already up to date.');
        } else if (data.status === 'unsupported') {
          console.log(`⚠️  ${data.message || 'Auto-update not supported in dev mode.'}`);
        } else {
          console.log(`ℹ️  Status: ${data.status}${data.message ? ' — ' + data.message : ''}`);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to check for updates. Is Alma running?');
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'download') {
      console.log('📥 Downloading update...');
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/update/download`, {
          method: 'POST'
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
          console.log('✅ Update downloaded. Run `alma update install` to install and restart.');
        } else {
          console.error(`❌ Download failed: ${data.error}`);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to download update. Is Alma running?');
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'install') {
      console.log('🚀 Installing update and restarting Alma...');
      try {
        await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/update/install`, {
          method: 'POST'
        });
        console.log('✅ Alma is restarting with the new version.');
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to install update. Is Alma running?');
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'status') {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/update/status`);
        const data = await res.json();
        console.log(`App: ${data.name} v${data.version}`);
        console.log(`Auto-update: ${data.autoUpdateSupported ? 'supported' : 'not supported'}`);
        console.log(`Status: ${data.autoUpdateStatus?.status || 'unknown'}`);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to get update status. Is Alma running?');
      }
    } else {
      console.log('Usage: alma update [check|download|install|status]');
    }
    return;
  }

  // ── Config ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'config') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'get' || subcmd === 'list') {
      const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
      const path = args[2];
      prettyPrint(subcmd === 'list' ? settings : getNestedValue(settings, path));
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'set') {
      const path = args[2];
      const rawValue = args[3];
      if (!path || rawValue === undefined) {
        console.error('Usage: alma config set <path> <value>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const value = parseValue(rawValue);

      // Validate defaultModel changes — must be a real provider:model and not an image-only model
      if ((path === 'chat.defaultModel' || path === 'telegram.defaultModel') && typeof value === 'string' && value.includes(':')) {
        const [providerId, modelId] = value.split(':');
        const providers = await api('GET', '/api/providers');
        const provider = (providers || []).find(p => p.id === providerId);
        if (!provider) {
          console.error(`❌ Provider "${providerId}" not found. Run 'alma providers' to see available providers.`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
        if (!provider.enabled) {
          console.error(`❌ Provider "${provider.name}" is disabled.`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
        // Block image-only models as default chat model
        // `imagen` only as a word of its own: ids that merely contain the letters are text models.
        const imageOnlyPatterns = ['nano-banana', 'image-generation', 'dall-e', 'stable-diffusion'];
        const id = modelId.toLowerCase();
        if (imageOnlyPatterns.some(p => id.includes(p)) || /(?<![a-z])imagen(?![a-z])/.test(id)) {
          console.error(`❌ "${modelId}" is an image generation model. Use the image-gen skill for image generation instead of changing the default chat model.`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
      }
      const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
      delete settings.needsEmbeddingRebuild;
      try {
        setNestedValue(settings, path, value);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`❌ ${err.message}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('PUT', '/api/settings', settings);
      console.log(`✅ ${path} = ${JSON.stringify(value)}`);
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma config <get|set|list>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Providers ───────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'providers' || cmd === 'provider') {
    const sub = args[1];

    // alma provider add <name> <type> [--api-key KEY] [--base-url URL] [--models m1,m2,...]
    if (sub === 'add' && args[2] && args[3]) {
      const name = args[2];
      // Normalize common type aliases
      const TYPE_ALIASES = {
        gemini: 'google',
        'google-gemini': 'google',
        gpt: 'openai',
        claude: 'anthropic'
      };
      const type = TYPE_ALIASES[args[3].toLowerCase()] || args[3]; // openai, anthropic, google, openrouter, etc.
      const body = {
        name,
        type
      };
      // Parse optional flags
      for (let i = 4; i < args.length; i++) {
        if ((args[i] === '--api-key' || args[i] === '-k') && args[i + 1]) {
          body.apiKey = args[++i];
        } else if ((args[i] === '--base-url' || args[i] === '-u') && args[i + 1]) {
          body.baseURL = args[++i];
        } else if ((args[i] === '--models' || args[i] === '-m') && args[i + 1]) {
          body.models = args[++i].split(',');
        }
      }
      // Auto-populate default models if not specified
      if (!body.models) {
        const defaultModels = {
          google: ['gemini-2.5-pro', 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.0-flash', 'gemini-1.5-pro', 'gemini-1.5-flash'],
          anthropic: ['claude-sonnet-4-20250514', 'claude-haiku-4-20250414', 'claude-opus-4-20250514'],
          openai: ['gpt-4o', 'gpt-4o-mini', 'o1', 'o1-mini', 'o3-mini'],
          deepseek: ['deepseek-chat', 'deepseek-reasoner']
        };
        if (defaultModels[type]) {
          body.models = defaultModels[type];
          console.log(`   Auto-populated ${body.models.length} default models for ${type}`);
        }
      }
      const result = await api('POST', '/api/providers', body);
      if (result && result.id) {
        console.log(`✅ Provider created: ${result.id} (${result.name})`);
        if (body.apiKey) console.log('   API key: set');
        if (body.baseURL) console.log(`   Base URL: ${body.baseURL}`);
        if (body.models) console.log(`   Models: ${body.models.join(', ')}`);
      } else {
        prettyPrint(result);
      }
      return;
    }

    // alma provider delete <id>
    if ((sub === 'delete' || sub === 'remove') && args[2]) {
      const result = await api('DELETE', `/api/providers/${args[2]}`);
      console.log(`✅ Provider deleted: ${args[2]}`);
      return;
    }

    // alma providers <id> models
    const providerId = sub;
    if (providerId && args[2] === 'models') {
      const models = await api('GET', `/api/providers/${providerId}/models`);
      if (Array.isArray(models)) {
        for (const m of models) {
          const id = typeof m === 'string' ? m : m.id || m.name;
          console.log(id);
        }
      } else {
        prettyPrint(models);
      }
      return;
    }
    const providers = await api('GET', '/api/providers');
    if (Array.isArray(providers)) {
      for (const p of providers) {
        console.log(`${p.id}  ${p.name || p.type || ''}  (${p.type || 'unknown'})`);
      }
    } else {
      prettyPrint(providers);
    }
    return;
  }

  // ── Models ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'models') {
    const models = await api('GET', '/api/models');
    if (Array.isArray(models)) {
      for (const m of models) {
        const id = typeof m === 'string' ? m : m.id || m.name;
        const provider = m.provider || '';
        console.log(`${provider ? provider + ':' : ''}${id}`);
      }
    } else {
      prettyPrint(models);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'model') {
    if (args[1] === 'set' && args[2]) {
      const modelId = args[2];
      const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
      delete settings.needsEmbeddingRebuild;
      settings.chat = settings.chat || {};
      settings.chat.defaultModel = modelId;
      await api('PUT', '/api/settings', settings);
      console.log(`✅ Default model set to: ${modelId}`);
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma model set <provider:model>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Threads ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'threads') {
    const usage = 'Usage: alma threads [limit|--all] [--project id|--workspace id] [--json]. Visible thread metadata only; not original-message coverage.';
    const options = args.slice(1);
    if (options.length === 1 && (options[0] === '--help' || options[0] === '-h')) {
      console.log(usage);
      return;
    }
    let limit = 10,
      all = false,
      json = false,
      workspaceId,
      limitSet = false;
    for (let index = 0; index < options.length; index++) {
      const arg = options[index];
      if (arg === '--json' && !json) json = true;else if (arg === '--all' && !all && !limitSet) all = true;else if ((arg === '--project' || arg === '--workspace') && workspaceId === undefined && options[index + 1] && !options[index + 1].startsWith('--')) workspaceId = options[++index];else if (!arg.startsWith('-') && !limitSet && !all && Number.isSafeInteger(Number(arg)) && Number(arg) >= 1 && Number(arg) <= 10000) {
        limit = Number(arg);
        limitSet = true;
      } else {
        console.error(usage);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    }
    // The existing API returns the visible thread set and older versions
    // ignore limit/workspace query parameters. Filter BEFORE limiting so
    // an out-of-project recent thread cannot hide an in-project source.
    const threads = await api('GET', '/api/threads');
    if (Array.isArray(threads)) {
      const scoped = workspaceId === undefined ? threads : threads.filter(t => t.workspaceId === workspaceId);
      const selected = all ? scoped : scoped.slice(0, limit);
      if (json) {
        prettyPrint(selected.map(t => ({
          id: t.id,
          title: t.title,
          workspaceId: t.workspaceId,
          workspaceName: t.workspaceName,
          createdAt: t.createdAt,
          updatedAt: t.updatedAt,
          parentThreadId: t.parentThreadId
        })));
        return;
      }
      console.log(`SOURCE DISCOVERY ONLY — ${selected.length}/${scoped.length} visible thread metadata rows${workspaceId ? ` in project ${workspaceId}` : ''}; not a transcript or external-thread inventory.`);
      for (const t of selected) {
        const date = t.updatedAt || t.createdAt || '';
        const parent = t.parentThreadId ? ` ← ${t.parentThreadId.slice(0, 8)}…` : '';
        console.log(`${t.id}  ${date}  ${t.title || '(untitled)'}${parent}`);
      }
      if (selected.length < scoped.length) console.log('MORE VISIBLE THREADS — use --all to inspect the remaining metadata; this list is not exhaustive.');
    } else {
      console.error('Unexpected thread-list response; cannot establish source discovery coverage.');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'thread') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'create') {
      // Extract --project <id> flag if present
      const createArgs = args.slice(2);
      let workspaceId;
      const projIdx = createArgs.indexOf('--project');
      if (projIdx !== -1) {
        workspaceId = createArgs[projIdx + 1];
        if (!workspaceId) {
          console.error('Usage: alma thread create <title> [--project <id>]');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        createArgs.splice(projIdx, 2);
      }
      const title = createArgs.join(' ') || 'New Thread';
      const body = {
        title
      };
      if (workspaceId) body.workspaceId = workspaceId;
      const thread = await api('POST', '/api/threads', body);
      const projSuffix = workspaceId ? `  (project: ${workspaceId})` : '';
      console.log(`✅ Created thread: ${thread.id}  "${thread.title || title}"${projSuffix}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'rename') {
      const renameArgs = args.slice(2);
      let id = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
      const threadIdx = renameArgs.indexOf('--thread');
      if (threadIdx !== -1) {
        id = renameArgs[threadIdx + 1];
        renameArgs.splice(threadIdx, 2);
      }
      const title = renameArgs.join(' ').trim();
      if (!id || !title) {
        console.error('Usage: alma thread rename [--thread <id>] <title>   (default thread: $ALMA_THREAD_ID)');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const thread = await api('PUT', `/api/threads/${id}`, {
        title,
        skipUpdatedAt: true
      });
      console.log(`✅ Renamed thread ${thread.id} → "${thread.title}"`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'delete') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma thread delete <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('DELETE', `/api/threads/${id}`);
      console.log(`✅ Deleted thread: ${id}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'search') {
      const searchArgs = args.slice(2);
      if (searchArgs.length === 1 && (searchArgs[0] === '--help' || searchArgs[0] === '-h')) {
        console.log('Usage: alma thread search <query> [--project <id>] [--json]. Source discovery metadata only; read scoped original messages separately.');
        return;
      }
      const projectIndex = searchArgs.indexOf('--project');
      let workspaceId;
      if (projectIndex !== -1) {
        workspaceId = searchArgs[projectIndex + 1];
        if (!workspaceId || workspaceId.startsWith('--')) {
          console.error('--project requires a project ID');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        searchArgs.splice(projectIndex, 2);
      }
      const jsonIndex = searchArgs.indexOf('--json');
      const json = jsonIndex !== -1;
      if (json) searchArgs.splice(jsonIndex, 1);
      if (searchArgs.some(arg => arg.startsWith('--'))) {
        console.error('Usage: alma thread search <query> [--project <id>] [--json]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const query = searchArgs.join(' ').trim();
      if (!query) {
        console.error('Usage: alma thread search <query>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const results = await api('GET', `/api/search/threads?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&metadataOnly=1${workspaceId ? `&workspaceId=${encodeURIComponent(workspaceId)}` : ''}`);
      if (json) {
        // Older servers may ignore metadataOnly. Never turn source
        // discovery into an implicit dump of matching message bodies.
        prettyPrint(Array.isArray(results) ? results.map(t => ({
          id: t.id,
          title: t.title,
          workspaceId: t.workspaceId,
          workspaceName: t.workspaceName,
          matchCount: t.matchCount,
          createdAt: t.createdAt,
          updatedAt: t.updatedAt
        })) : results);
        return;
      }
      console.log('SOURCE DISCOVERY ONLY — project membership is metadata, not a matching thread title; read scoped originals before claiming coverage.');
      if (Array.isArray(results) && results.length > 0) {
        for (const t of results) {
          console.log(`${t.id}  ${t.title || '(untitled)'}`);
          console.log(`  project: ${t.workspaceId || '(unclassified)'}  ${t.workspaceName || '(unknown name)'}`);
        }
      } else {
        console.log('No threads found.');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'compact') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma thread compact <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const result = await api('POST', `/api/threads/${id}/compact`);
      console.log(`✅ Compacted thread: ${id}`);
      if (result && typeof result === 'object') prettyPrint(result);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'messages') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma thread messages <id> [limit] [--full] [--offset n] [--json]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const limit = Number(args[3] && !args[3].startsWith('--') ? args[3] : '20');
      const offsetIndex = args.indexOf('--offset');
      const offset = offsetIndex >= 0 ? Number(args[offsetIndex + 1]) : 0;
      const full = args.includes('--full');
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100 || !Number.isSafeInteger(offset) || offset < 0) {
        console.error('limit must be 1–100; --offset must be a nonnegative integer');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const page = await api('GET', `/api/threads/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/messages?page=1&limit=${limit}&offset=${offset}`);
      const messages = page.messages;
      if (!Array.isArray(messages)) {
        console.error('Server does not support bounded message retrieval. Update Alma; do not treat this response as a full transcript.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (args.includes('--json')) {
        prettyPrint({
          ...page,
          contentMode: 'original',
          sourceTextTruncated: false,
          coverageNote: 'Source pagination only; outer tool compaction may omit content. Inspect compaction markers before claiming full model-visible coverage.'
        });
        return;
      }
      console.log(full ? 'FULL SOURCE TEXT — outer tool compaction may still omit content; this is not a complete-read receipt.' : 'PREVIEW ONLY — each message is clipped to 80 characters; NOT a full transcript.');
      console.log(`Scope: ${page.scope}. Page offset=${offset}, total=${page.total}.`);
      if (Array.isArray(messages)) {
        for (const m of messages) {
          // Parse message: could be raw DB row with .message JSON string, or already parsed
          let role = m.role || 'unknown';
          let content = '';
          try {
            const msg = typeof m.message === 'string' ? JSON.parse(m.message) : m.message;
            if (msg) {
              role = msg.role || role;
              if (msg.parts && Array.isArray(msg.parts)) {
                content = msg.parts.filter(p => p.type === 'text').map(p => p.text).join('\n');
              } else if (msg.content) {
                content = typeof msg.content === 'string' ? msg.content : JSON.stringify(msg.content);
              }
            }
          } catch {
            // Fallback to direct fields
            content = typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content || '');
          }
          if (!content && m.content) {
            content = typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content);
          }
          const text = content || '(empty)';
          console.log(`\n[${m.id}] ${role} — sent-at: ${m.createdAt || m.timestamp || 'unknown'}`);
          console.log(full ? text : `${truncate(text, 80)}${text.length > 80 ? ` [TRUNCATED: ${text.length - 80} characters omitted]` : ''}`);
        }
        console.log(`\n(${messages.length} messages on this page; ${page.total} total)`);
        if (page.missingMessageIds?.length) console.log(`WARNING: missing stored rows: ${page.missingMessageIds.join(', ')}. Coverage is incomplete.`);
        if (!full) console.log(`Read original text: alma thread messages ${id} ${limit} --full --offset ${offset}`);
        if (page.hasMore) console.log(`MORE MESSAGES — continue: alma thread messages ${id} ${limit} --full --offset ${page.nextOffset}`);else console.log('END OF MESSAGE PAGES — source pagination only, NOT full model-visible read coverage. If output is compacted, read the existing archive through every Read page; grep/filter results cover excerpts only. Text does not inline images or expand tool outputs; use --json or specific references for those.');
      } else {
        prettyPrint(messages);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'switch') {
      const targetId = args[2];
      if (!targetId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma thread switch <target-thread-id> [--from <current-thread-id>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      // Get source thread from --from flag or ALMA_THREAD_ID env
      let sourceThreadId = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
      const fromIdx = args.indexOf('--from');
      if (fromIdx !== -1 && args[fromIdx + 1]) {
        sourceThreadId = args[fromIdx + 1];
      }
      if (!sourceThreadId) {
        console.error('Error: Cannot determine current thread. Use --from <thread-id> or ensure ALMA_THREAD_ID is set.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const result = await api('POST', `/api/threads/${targetId}/switch`, {
        sourceThreadId
      });
      if (result.success) {
        console.log(`✅ 已切换到: "${result.targetThread?.title || targetId}" (${result.switched} mapping(s) updated)`);
      } else {
        console.error('❌ Switch failed:', JSON.stringify(result));
      }
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma thread <create|delete|search|compact|messages|switch>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Memory ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'memory') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'list' || !subcmd) {
      const memories = await api('GET', '/api/memories');
      if (Array.isArray(memories)) {
        if (memories.length === 0) {
          console.log('No memories.');
          return;
        }
        for (const m of memories) {
          console.log(`${m.id}  ${truncate(m.content || m.text || '', 70)}`);
        }
        console.log(`\n(${memories.length} memories)`);
      } else {
        prettyPrint(memories);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'search') {
      const query = args.slice(2).join(' ');
      if (!query) {
        console.error('Usage: alma memory search <query>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const response = await api('POST', '/api/memories/search', {
        query
      });
      const results = Array.isArray(response) ? response : response?.results || [];
      if (results.length > 0) {
        for (const m of results) {
          const score = m.score != null ? ` (${(m.score * 100).toFixed(0)}%)` : '';
          console.log(`${m.id}  ${truncate(m.content || m.text || '', 60)}${score}`);
        }
      } else {
        console.log('No matching memories.');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'add') {
      const content = args.slice(2).join(' ');
      if (!content) {
        console.error('Usage: alma memory add <content>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const mem = await api('POST', '/api/memories', {
        content
      });
      console.log(`✅ Memory added: ${mem.id || '(ok)'}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'delete') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma memory delete <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('DELETE', `/api/memories/${id}`);
      console.log(`✅ Memory deleted: ${id}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'stats') {
      const stats = await api('GET', '/api/memories/stats');
      prettyPrint(stats);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'grep') {
      const query = args.slice(2).join(' ');
      if (!query) {
        console.error('Usage: alma memory grep <keyword>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      // Search through archived thread markdown files
      const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
      const workspacePath = settings?.workspace?.path || _path.join(_os.homedir(), 'Library', 'Application Support', 'alma', 'workspaces', 'default');
      const threadsDir = _path.join(workspacePath, 'threads');
      if (!_fs.existsSync(threadsDir)) {
        console.log('No thread archives yet. Archives are created automatically every 5 minutes.');
        return;
      }
      const files = _fs.readdirSync(threadsDir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.md'));
      const results = [];
      for (const file of files) {
        const content = _fs.readFileSync(_path.join(threadsDir, file), 'utf-8');
        const lines = content.split('\n');
        const matches = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
          if (lines[i].toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())) {
            matches.push({
              line: i + 1,
              text: lines[i].trim().substring(0, 120)
            });
          }
        }
        if (matches.length > 0) {
          // Extract metadata from frontmatter
          const titleMatch = content.match(/^title:\s*"?(.+?)"?\s*$/m);
          const dateMatch = content.match(/^createdAt:\s*(.+)$/m);
          const title = titleMatch ? titleMatch[1] : file;
          const date = dateMatch ? dateMatch[1].substring(0, 10) : '';
          results.push({
            file,
            title,
            date,
            matches
          });
        }
      }
      if (results.length === 0) {
        console.log(`No matches for "${query}" in ${files.length} archived threads.`);
      } else {
        let totalMatches = 0;
        for (const r of results) {
          console.log(`\n📄 ${r.title} (${r.date})`);
          for (const m of r.matches.slice(0, 5)) {
            console.log(`   L${m.line}: ${m.text}`);
            totalMatches++;
          }
          if (r.matches.length > 5) {
            console.log(`   ... and ${r.matches.length - 5} more matches`);
            totalMatches += r.matches.length - 5;
          }
        }
        console.log(`\n${totalMatches} matches in ${results.length} thread(s)`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'archive') {
      // Force archive all threads now
      const resp = await api('POST', '/api/threads/archive');
      console.log(resp?.message || '✅ Archive triggered');
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma memory <list|search|add|delete|grep|stats|archive>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Image Model (auto-detect best Gemini image model) ──
  if (cmd === 'image-model') {
    const googleProvider = await getEnabledGoogleProvider();
    if (!googleProvider) {
      console.error('No enabled Google provider found');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const modelIds = await fetchGeminiImageModelIds(googleProvider);
    const best = pickBestImageModel(modelIds);
    if (!best) {
      console.error('No image generation model found');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    process.stdout.write(best);
    process.exit(0);
  }

  // ── Image Generate ──────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'selfie') {
    const selfieDir = _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'selfies');
    _fs.mkdirSync(selfieDir, {
      recursive: true
    });
    const sub = args[1] || 'list';
    if (sub === 'list' || sub === 'ls') {
      const files = _fs.readdirSync(selfieDir).filter(f => /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif)$/i.test(f)).sort();
      if (files.length === 0) {
        console.error('No selfies saved yet.');
      } else {
        for (const f of files) {
          console.log(_path.join(selfieDir, f));
        }
      }
      process.exit(0);
    }
    if (sub === 'latest') {
      const files = _fs.readdirSync(selfieDir).filter(f => /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif)$/i.test(f)).sort();
      if (files.length === 0) {
        console.error('No selfies saved yet.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      console.log(_path.join(selfieDir, files[files.length - 1]));
      process.exit(0);
    }
    if (sub === 'save') {
      const srcPath = args[2];
      if (!srcPath || !_fs.existsSync(srcPath)) {
        console.error('Usage: alma selfie save <image-path>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const ext = _path.extname(srcPath) || '.jpg';
      const ts = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
      const destPath = _path.join(selfieDir, `selfie-${ts}${ext}`);
      _fs.copyFileSync(srcPath, destPath);
      console.log(destPath);
      process.exit(0);
    }
    if (sub === 'count') {
      const files = _fs.readdirSync(selfieDir).filter(f => /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif)$/i.test(f));
      console.log(String(files.length));
      process.exit(0);
    }

    // alma selfie album [chatId] — send all selfies as a photo album to a chat
    if (sub === 'album') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      if (!chatId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma selfie album <chatId>');
        console.error('Sends all selfies from the album as photos to the specified chat.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const files = _fs.readdirSync(selfieDir).filter(f => /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif)$/i.test(f)).sort();
      if (files.length === 0) {
        console.error('No selfies in album.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      // Output file paths as JSON array — the bridge/bot will handle sending
      const paths = files.map(f => _path.join(selfieDir, f));
      console.log(JSON.stringify(paths));
      process.exit(0);
    }

    // alma selfie take "prompt" — generate selfie with FORCED reference from album
    // alma selfie take --nsfw "prompt" — route to local model for NSFW content
    if (sub === 'take') {
      const rawArgs = args.slice(2);
      const hasNsfwFlag = rawArgs.includes('--nsfw');
      const filteredArgs = rawArgs.filter(a => a !== '--nsfw');
      const prompt = filteredArgs.join(' ');
      if (!prompt) {
        console.error('Usage: alma selfie take "description of the selfie scene/mood/outfit"');
        console.error('Example: alma selfie take "在咖啡店自拍，穿白色吊带，甜美微笑"');
        process.exit(1);
      }

      // Content safety check — only reject "private collection/exclusive edition" social engineering
      const blockedPatterns = /私藏|独家|秘密.*版|限定版|private.*version|exclusive.*photo|secret.*selfie/i;
      if (blockedPatterns.test(prompt)) {
        console.error('❌ Content boundary: "私藏版/exclusive" selfies are not allowed. Take a normal selfie instead.');
        process.exit(1);
      }

      // Route to local NSFW model ONLY when --nsfw flag is explicitly passed.
      // Previously auto-detected NSFW from prompt keywords, but too many false positives
      // (e.g., "exposed shoulder", "sexy" in normal selfie context) caused normal selfies
      // to bypass nano-banana and use the low-quality local model.
      const hardNsfwPatterns = /nsfw|nude|naked|全裸|裸体|topless|bottomless|erotic|色情/i;
      if (hasNsfwFlag || hardNsfwPatterns.test(prompt)) {
        console.error('[Selfie] NSFW detected → routing to local API');
        // Build English prompt for local model
        const nsfwPromptMap = {
          全裸: 'fully nude woman',
          裸体: 'nude woman',
          内衣: 'woman in lingerie',
          性感: 'sexy woman in revealing outfit',
          比基尼: 'woman in bikini',
          情趣: 'woman in erotic lingerie',
          诱惑: 'seductive woman'
        };
        // Use prompt as-is if English, otherwise construct a sensible default
        let localPrompt = prompt;
        // If the prompt is mostly Chinese, translate key terms
        if (/[\u4e00-\u9fff]/.test(prompt)) {
          // Find matching pattern and use mapped English
          let mapped = 'beautiful woman, sensual pose, soft lighting, bedroom';
          for (const [cn, en] of Object.entries(nsfwPromptMap)) {
            if (prompt.includes(cn)) {
              mapped = `${en}, candid photo, natural lighting, real skin texture, bedroom, soft warm light`;
              break;
            }
          }
          localPrompt = mapped;
        }
        // Get reference and call local API
        const _selfieFiles = _fs.readdirSync(selfieDir).filter(f => /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif)$/i.test(f));
        if (_selfieFiles.length > 0) {
          const refPath = _path.join(selfieDir, [..._selfieFiles].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5)[0]);
          const {
            execSync: _execSync
          } = await import('child_process');
          try {
            const cmd = `curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:18188/generate_with_face -F 'prompt=${localPrompt.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}' -F 'negative_prompt=ugly, deformed, blurry, low quality, text, watermark' -F 'width=1024' -F 'height=1024' -F 'num_inference_steps=6' -F 'guidance_scale=2.0' -F 'face_strength=0.7' -F "reference_image=@${refPath}"`;
            console.error('[Selfie Local] Generating with local model...');
            const output = _execSync(cmd, {
              encoding: 'utf-8',
              timeout: 180000
            });
            const result = JSON.parse(output);
            if (result.file_path) {
              console.log(result.file_path);
              console.error(`[Selfie Local] Generated in ${result.elapsed_seconds}s -> ${result.file_path}`);
            }
          } catch (err) {
            console.error('❌ Local generation failed:', err.message);
            process.exit(1);
          }
        } else {
          console.error('❌ No selfie album photos for face reference');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        process.exit(0);
      }

      // Get latest reference image from album (MANDATORY)
      const selfieFiles = _fs.readdirSync(selfieDir).filter(f => /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif)$/i.test(f)).sort();
      if (selfieFiles.length === 0) {
        console.error('❌ No selfies in album yet. Take a first selfie with: alma image generate "your appearance description"');
        console.error('Then save it: alma selfie save <path>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      // Randomly pick multiple reference images for better face consistency
      const NUM_REFS = Math.min(5, selfieFiles.length);
      const shuffled = [...selfieFiles].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
      const selectedRefs = shuffled.slice(0, NUM_REFS).map(f => _path.join(selfieDir, f));
      console.error(`[Selfie] Using ${NUM_REFS} references: ${selectedRefs.map(r => _path.basename(r)).join(', ')}`);

      // Delegate to alma image generate with forced --reference(s)
      const {
        execSync
      } = await import('child_process');
      const almaPath = process.argv[1];
      // Inject pose variety instruction
      const poseVariety = 'IMPORTANT: Use a DIFFERENT pose, angle, and expression from the reference images. Only keep the same FACE and APPEARANCE — vary everything else (pose, camera angle, body language, hand position, head tilt, expression intensity). ';
      const enhancedPrompt = poseVariety + prompt;
      const escapedPrompt = enhancedPrompt.replace(/"/g, '\\"');
      const refArgs = selectedRefs.map(r => `--reference "${r.replace(/"/g, '\\"')}"`).join(' ');
      try {
        const output = execSync(`"${process.execPath}" "${almaPath}" image generate "${escapedPrompt}" ${refArgs}`, {
          encoding: 'utf-8',
          timeout: 120_000,
          maxBuffer: 10 * 1024 * 1024,
          stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe']
        });
        // Pass through stdout (file path)
        const lines = output.trim().split('\n');
        for (const line of lines) {
          if (line.trim() && _fs.existsSync(line.trim())) {
            console.log(line.trim());
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('❌ Selfie generation failed:', err.stderr?.substring(err.stderr.length - 500) || err.message);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      process.exit(0);
    }

    // alma selfie local "prompt" — generate via local RealVisXL + FaceID (no content filter)
    if (sub === 'local') {
      const rawArgs = args.slice(2);
      const prompt = rawArgs.join(' ');
      if (!prompt) {
        console.error('Usage: alma selfie local "scene description in English"');
        console.error('Example: alma selfie local "woman in bedroom, wearing lingerie, soft lighting, sensual pose"');
        process.exit(1);
      }

      // Get reference image from album
      const selfieFiles = _fs.readdirSync(selfieDir).filter(f => /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif)$/i.test(f)).sort();
      let refArg = '';
      if (selfieFiles.length > 0) {
        const shuffled = [...selfieFiles].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
        const refPath = _path.join(selfieDir, shuffled[0]);
        refArg = `-F "reference_image=@${refPath}"`;
        console.error(`[Selfie Local] Using face reference: ${shuffled[0]}`);
      }

      // Call local API
      const {
        execSync
      } = await import('child_process');
      try {
        const endpoint = refArg ? 'generate_with_face' : 'generate';
        let cmd;
        if (refArg) {
          const refPath = _path.join(selfieDir, [...selfieFiles].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5)[0]);
          cmd = `curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:18188/generate_with_face -F 'prompt=${prompt.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}' -F 'negative_prompt=ugly, deformed, blurry, low quality, text, watermark, airbrushed, plastic skin' -F 'width=1024' -F 'height=1024' -F 'num_inference_steps=6' -F 'guidance_scale=2.0' -F 'face_strength=0.7' -F "reference_image=@${refPath}"`;
        } else {
          cmd = `curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:18188/generate -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"prompt":"${prompt.replace(/"/g, '\\"')}","negative_prompt":"ugly, deformed, blurry, low quality, text, watermark","width":1024,"height":1024,"num_inference_steps":6,"guidance_scale":2.0}'`;
        }
        console.error(`[Selfie Local] Generating with local model...`);
        const output = execSync(cmd, {
          encoding: 'utf-8',
          timeout: 180000
        });
        const result = JSON.parse(output);
        if (result.file_path) {
          console.log(result.file_path);
          console.error(`[Selfie Local] Generated in ${result.elapsed_seconds}s -> ${result.file_path}`);
        } else {
          console.error('❌ Local generation failed:', output);
          process.exit(1);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        if (err.message?.includes('ECONNREFUSED') || err.stderr?.includes('ECONNREFUSED') || err.stderr?.includes('Connection refused')) {
          console.error('❌ Local image server not running. Start it with:');
          console.error('   cd ~/.config/alma/z-image-turbo && nohup python3 server_realvis.py > /tmp/z-image-turbo.log 2>&1 &');
        } else {
          console.error('❌ Local generation failed:', err.stderr || err.message);
        }
        process.exit(1);
      }
      process.exit(0);
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma selfie <take|local|list|latest|save|count|album>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Sing (Suno music generation via PiAPI) ──────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'sing') {
    const sub = args[1];
    // alma sing config <piapi-api-key>  — save PiAPI API key
    if (sub === 'config') {
      const apiKey = args[2];
      if (!apiKey) {
        console.error('Usage: alma sing config <piapi-api-key>');
        console.error('Get your API key from https://app.piapi.ai/');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const configDir = _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma');
      if (!_fs.existsSync(configDir)) _fs.mkdirSync(configDir, {
        recursive: true
      });
      const configPath = _path.join(configDir, 'piapi.json');
      _fs.writeFileSync(configPath, JSON.stringify({
        apiKey
      }, null, 2));
      console.log('✅ PiAPI API key saved');
      return;
    }

    // Load PiAPI API key (optional — ACE-Step is the primary backend)
    const piapiConfigPath = _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'piapi.json');
    let piapiKey = '';
    if (_fs.existsSync(piapiConfigPath)) {
      try {
        piapiKey = JSON.parse(_fs.readFileSync(piapiConfigPath, 'utf-8')).apiKey || '';
      } catch {
        /* ignore */
      }
    }

    // alma sing generate "prompt" [--lyrics "lyrics"] [--duration 60] [--instrumental]
    if (sub === 'generate' || sub === 'gen' || !sub) {
      const rawArgs = args.slice(sub === 'generate' || sub === 'gen' ? 2 : 1);

      // Parse flags
      let lyrics = '';
      let duration = 60;
      let instrumental = false;
      const promptParts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < rawArgs.length; i++) {
        if (rawArgs[i] === '--lyrics' && rawArgs[i + 1]) {
          lyrics = rawArgs[++i].replace(/\\n/g, '\n');
          continue;
        }
        if (rawArgs[i] === '--duration' && rawArgs[i + 1]) {
          duration = parseInt(rawArgs[++i]) || 60;
          continue;
        }
        if (rawArgs[i] === '--instrumental') {
          instrumental = true;
          continue;
        }
        promptParts.push(rawArgs[i]);
      }
      const prompt = promptParts.join(' ');
      if (!prompt) {
        console.error('Usage: alma sing generate "style description" [--lyrics "lyrics"] [--duration 60] [--instrumental]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
