      // ACE-Step 1.5 on remote 3090 (10.0.0.207:8001)
      const ACESTEP_HOST = process.env.ACESTEP_HOST || '10.0.0.207';
      const ACESTEP_PORT = process.env.ACESTEP_PORT || '8001';
      const ACESTEP_URL = `http://${ACESTEP_HOST}:${ACESTEP_PORT}`;

      // SSH config for starting ACE-Step if not running
      const ACESTEP_SSH = process.env.ACESTEP_SSH || `yetone@${ACESTEP_HOST}`;
      console.error(`[Sing] Generating with ACE-Step 1.5 on 3090 (~${duration}s audio)...`);
      const {
        execSync
      } = await import('child_process');

      // Check if ACE-Step API is running, start if not
      let apiReady = false;
      try {
        const healthCheck = execSync(`curl -s --max-time 5 ${ACESTEP_URL}/health`, {
          encoding: 'utf-8',
          stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe']
        });
        if (healthCheck.includes('"status"')) apiReady = true;
      } catch {
        /* not running */
      }
      if (!apiReady) {
        console.error('[Sing] ACE-Step API not running, starting on 3090...');
        try {
          // Kill ComfyUI to free VRAM, then start ACE-Step
          execSync(`ssh -p 22 ${ACESTEP_SSH} 'pkill -f "python main.py.*8188" || true; sleep 2; export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"; export HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com; export ACESTEP_LM_BACKEND=pt; export ACESTEP_LM_MODEL_PATH=acestep-5Hz-lm-0.6B; cd ~/ACE-Step-1.5 && nohup uv run acestep-api --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8001 > /tmp/acestep.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &'`, {
            timeout: 30_000,
            stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe']
          });
          // Wait for startup (LM loading takes ~100s)
          console.error('[Sing] Waiting for ACE-Step to load models (~90s)...');
          for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
            execSync('sleep 5', {
              stdio: 'pipe'
            });
            try {
              const h = execSync(`curl -s --max-time 3 ${ACESTEP_URL}/health`, {
                encoding: 'utf-8',
                stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe']
              });
              if (h.includes('"status"')) {
                apiReady = true;
                break;
              }
            } catch {
              /* still loading */
            }
          }
          if (!apiReady) {
            console.error('❌ ACE-Step failed to start within 120s. Check /tmp/acestep.log on 3090.');
            process.exit(1);
          }
          console.error('[Sing] ACE-Step API ready!');
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('❌ Failed to start ACE-Step:', err.message);
          process.exit(1);
        }
      }

      // Submit generation task
      const taskPayload = {
        prompt,
        lyrics: instrumental ? '' : lyrics,
        thinking: true,
        audio_duration: duration,
        audio_format: 'mp3',
        inference_steps: 8
      };
      let taskId;
      try {
        const resp = execSync(`curl -s -X POST ${ACESTEP_URL}/release_task -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '${JSON.stringify(taskPayload).replace(/'/g, "'\\''")}'`, {
          encoding: 'utf-8',
          timeout: 30_000,
          stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe']
        });
        const parsed = JSON.parse(resp);
        taskId = parsed?.data?.task_id;
        if (!taskId) throw new Error('No task_id in response: ' + resp);
        console.error(`[Sing] Task submitted: ${taskId}`);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to submit task:', err.message);
        process.exit(1);
      }

      // Poll for result (max 5 min)
      console.error('[Sing] Generating...');
      let audioPath = null;
      const maxPolls = 60; // 60 * 5s = 5 min
      for (let i = 0; i < maxPolls; i++) {
        execSync('sleep 5', {
          stdio: 'pipe'
        });
        try {
          const resp = execSync(`curl -s -X POST ${ACESTEP_URL}/query_result -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"task_id_list": ["${taskId}"]}'`, {
            encoding: 'utf-8',
            timeout: 10_000,
            stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe']
          });
          const parsed = JSON.parse(resp);
          if (parsed?.data?.length > 0) {
            const item = parsed.data[0];
            if (item.status === 1) {
              // Succeeded — extract audio file URL
              const result = JSON.parse(item.result || '[]');
              if (result[0]?.file) {
                audioPath = result[0].file;
              }
              break;
            } else if (item.status === 2) {
              const result = JSON.parse(item.result || '[]');
              const errMsg = result[0]?.error || 'Unknown error';
              console.error('❌ Generation failed:', errMsg);
              process.exit(1);
            }
            // status 0 = still running
          }
        } catch {
          /* retry */
        }
      }
      if (!audioPath) {
        console.error('❌ Generation timed out (5 min)');
        process.exit(1);
      }

      // Download the audio file
      const outputDir = _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'music');
      if (!_fs.existsSync(outputDir)) _fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, {
        recursive: true
      });
      const outputFile = _path.join(outputDir, `song_${Date.now()}.mp3`);
      try {
        execSync(`curl -s -o "${outputFile}" "${ACESTEP_URL}${audioPath}"`, {
          timeout: 60_000,
          stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe']
        });
        if (!_fs.existsSync(outputFile) || _fs.statSync(outputFile).size < 1000) {
          throw new Error('Downloaded file is too small or missing');
        }
        console.log(outputFile);
        console.error(`[Sing] ✅ Saved to ${outputFile}`);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to download audio:', err.message);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      process.exit(0);
    }
    console.error('Usage:');
    console.error('  alma sing generate "style description" [--lyrics "lyrics"] [--duration 60] [--instrumental]');
    console.error('  alma sing config <piapi-api-key>  (for PiAPI/Suno fallback)');
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'image') {
    const sub = args[1];
    if (sub === 'models' || sub === 'list-models' || sub === 'ls-models') {
      let result;
      try {
        result = await api('GET', '/api/image/models');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`❌ Failed to list image models: ${err.message || err}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const models = Array.isArray(result?.models) ? result.models : [];
      if (models.length === 0) {
        console.error('❌ No image generation model found');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      // The `*` must mark what actually runs when `--model` is omitted — i.e. the
      // user's configured default (settings.imageGen.model), falling back to the
      // auto pick. Marking the auto pick unconditionally is what taught agents to
      // volunteer a `--model` that contradicted the user's setting
      // (alma-releases#65).
      let configuredDefault = '';
      try {
        const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
        configuredDefault = (settings && settings.imageGen && settings.imageGen.model || '').replace(/^models\//, '');
      } catch {
        /* settings unavailable — fall back to showing the auto pick */
      }
      const effectiveDefault = configuredDefault || result.best || '';
      console.log('Available image generation models:');
      for (const model of models) {
        const mark = model.value === effectiveDefault ? '*' : ' ';
        console.log(`${mark} ${model.label || model.value}`);
        if (model.label && model.label !== model.value) console.log(`    ${model.value}`);
      }
      if (effectiveDefault) {
        const origin = configuredDefault ? "the user's configured default" : 'auto-selected — no default configured';
        console.log(`\n* ${effectiveDefault} — used when you omit --model (${origin})`);
      }
      console.error('\nDo NOT pass --model unless the user named a model or asked to compare several: omitting it uses the default above.');
      process.exit(0);
    }
    if (sub === 'generate' || sub === 'gen' || sub === 'edit') {
      const rawArgs = args.slice(2);
      const positional = [];
      const referencePaths = [];
      let modelOverride = '';
      let aspectOverride = ''; // --aspect/--size: an aspect ratio like 1:1, 16:9, 9:16, 4:3, 3:4

      for (let i = 0; i < rawArgs.length; i++) {
        const token = rawArgs[i];
        if (token === '--model' && rawArgs[i + 1]) {
          modelOverride = rawArgs[++i].replace(/^models\//, '');
          continue;
        }
        if (token.startsWith('--model=')) {
          modelOverride = token.slice('--model='.length).replace(/^models\//, '');
          continue;
        }
        if (token === '--reference' && rawArgs[i + 1]) {
          referencePaths.push(rawArgs[++i]);
          continue;
        }
        if (token.startsWith('--reference=')) {
          referencePaths.push(token.slice('--reference='.length));
          continue;
        }
        // --aspect / --size (alias): an aspect ratio (1:1, 16:9, 9:16, 4:3, 3:4, …) or "auto".
        if ((token === '--aspect' || token === '--size') && rawArgs[i + 1]) {
          aspectOverride = rawArgs[++i];
          continue;
        }
        if (token.startsWith('--aspect=') || token.startsWith('--size=')) {
          aspectOverride = token.slice(token.indexOf('=') + 1);
          continue;
        }
        positional.push(token);
      }
      let prompt = '';
      let editPath = '';
      if (sub === 'edit') {
        editPath = positional[0] || '';
        prompt = positional.slice(1).join(' ').trim();
        if (!editPath || !prompt) {
          console.error('Usage: alma image edit [--model <model-id>] [--aspect <ratio>] <image-path> "<prompt>"');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        if (referencePaths.length > 0) {
          console.error('⚠️ `--reference` is ignored in edit mode.');
        }
        if (!_fs.existsSync(editPath)) {
          console.error(`❌ Edit source image not found: ${editPath}`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
      } else {
        prompt = positional.join(' ').trim();
        if (!prompt) {
          console.error('Usage: alma image generate [--model <model-id>] [--aspect <ratio>] "<prompt>" [--reference <image-path> ...]');
          process.exit(1);
        }
      }

      // Content safety check — reject NSFW/explicit requests
      const imgBlockedPatterns = /私藏|大尺度|裸|nude|naked|nsfw|explicit|porn|hentai/i;
      if (imgBlockedPatterns.test(prompt)) {
        console.error('❌ Content boundary: explicit/NSFW image generation is not allowed.');
        process.exit(1);
      }

      // When there's no explicit --model, fall back to the configured default
      // (settings.imageGen.model). It may be a plain Gemini id OR `providerId:modelId`
      // targeting any OpenAI-compatible provider (e.g. openai:gpt-image-1). It's then
      // treated like --model below, but with SOFT fallback to auto if unset/unavailable.
      let modelFromConfig = false;
      let modelFromAutoList = false;
      let configuredModel = '';
      // Effective aspect ratio: --aspect/--size flag wins, else the configured default
      // (settings.imageGen.aspectRatio). "auto"/empty → no constraint.
      let aspect = aspectOverride;
      try {
        const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
        configuredModel = (settings && settings.imageGen && settings.imageGen.model || '').replace(/^models\//, '');
        if (!modelOverride && configuredModel) {
          modelOverride = configuredModel;
          modelFromConfig = true;
        }
        if (!aspect && settings && settings.imageGen && settings.imageGen.aspectRatio) {
          aspect = settings.imageGen.aspectRatio;
        }
      } catch {
        /* settings unavailable — keep auto */
      }
      // An explicit --model winning over the user's configured default is exactly
      // the complaint in alma-releases#65. Telling the agent not to do it is not
      // enough — the configured model only actually wins if the CLI enforces it.
      // So: an override that the user's own words do not support is dropped, and
      // the substitution is never silent either way.
      if (!modelFromConfig && modelOverride && configuredModel && modelOverride !== configuredModel) {
        const userText = await latestUserMessageText();
        if (userText !== null && !textNamesModel(userText, modelOverride)) {
          console.error(`⚠️ Ignoring --model ${modelOverride}: this request never named a model, so the configured image model is used.`);
          console.error(`   Using ${configuredModel} (Settings → Image Generation). Pass --model only when the user names a model or asks to compare several.`);
          modelOverride = configuredModel;
          modelFromConfig = true;
        } else {
          console.error(`⚠️ --model ${modelOverride} overrides the configured image model (${configuredModel}).`);
          console.error('   Only pass --model when the user named a model; otherwise omit it and the configured default is used.');
        }
      }
      // No --model and no configured default: resolve "auto" through the same list
      // the settings picker shows, so auto isn't Gemini-or-bust for users whose only
      // image providers are OpenAI-compatible or plugin ones.
      if (!modelOverride) {
        try {
          const listed = await api('GET', '/api/image/models');
          const listedModels = Array.isArray(listed && listed.models) ? listed.models : [];
          const auto = (listed && listed.best || listedModels[0] && listedModels[0].value || '').replace(/^models\//, '');
          if (auto) {
            modelOverride = auto;
            modelFromConfig = true; // soft: an unusable auto pick falls back to the Gemini path
            modelFromAutoList = true;
          }
        } catch {
          /* list unavailable — fall through to the Gemini auto path below */
        }
      }
      if (aspect === 'auto') aspect = '';
      const openaiSize = aspectToOpenAISize(aspect);
      if (aspect) console.error(`[Image] Aspect ratio: ${aspect}`);

      // Provider routing: when --model is given as `provider:modelId`, resolve
      // that provider instead of assuming Google. OpenRouter uses its unified
      // /images JSON endpoint; other non-Google providers use OpenAI-compatible
      // image endpoints. An explicit Google provider falls through to the Gemini
      // path below with its prefix stripped so model matching works.
      // Plugin provider models are `plugin:<pluginId>:<providerId>:<modelId>`
      // (e.g. plugin:xai-grok-auth:xai-grok:grok-imagine-image) and must be
      // handled before the generic first-colon split below mangles them.
      if (modelOverride && modelOverride.startsWith('plugin:')) {
        if (modelOverride.split(':').length >= 4) {
          await generateImageViaPlugin(modelOverride, {
            sub,
            prompt,
            editPath,
            referencePaths,
            aspect
          });
          // generateImageViaPlugin always exits; the line below is unreachable.
        } else if (modelFromConfig) {
          // Falling back silently is the same class of surprise as overriding
          // silently (alma-releases#65): say which model did not survive.
          console.error(`⚠️ Configured image model ${modelOverride} is malformed (expected plugin:<pluginId>:<providerId>:<modelId>) — falling back to an automatic pick.`);
          modelOverride = '';
        } else {
          console.error(`❌ Invalid plugin model: ${modelOverride} (expected plugin:<pluginId>:<providerId>:<modelId>)`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
      }
      if (modelOverride && modelOverride.includes(':')) {
        const sepIdx = modelOverride.indexOf(':');
        const ovProviderId = modelOverride.slice(0, sepIdx);
        const ovModelId = modelOverride.slice(sepIdx + 1);
        const allProviders = (await api('GET', '/api/providers')) || [];
        const ovProvider = allProviders.find(p => p.id === ovProviderId && p.enabled !== false);
        if (ovProvider && ovProvider.type === 'google') {
          modelOverride = ovModelId; // strip prefix, let the Gemini path handle it
        } else if (ovProvider) {
          await generateImageViaOpenAI(ovProvider, ovModelId, {
            sub,
            prompt,
            editPath,
            referencePaths,
            size: openaiSize,
            aspect
          });
          // generateImageViaOpenAI always exits; the line below is unreachable.
        } else if (modelFromConfig) {
          console.error(`⚠️ ${modelFromAutoList ? 'Auto-selected' : 'Configured'} image model ${modelOverride} is unavailable (provider "${ovProviderId}" is missing or disabled) — falling back to an automatic pick.`);
          modelOverride = '';
        } else {
          console.error(`❌ Unknown provider in --model: ${ovProviderId}`);
          console.error('Run `alma providers` to list configured providers, then use `--model <providerId>:<modelId>`.');
          process.exit(1);
        }
      }
      const googleProvider = await getEnabledGoogleProvider();
      if (!googleProvider) {
        console.error('❌ No enabled Google provider with API key found');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let modelIds = [];
      try {
        modelIds = await fetchGeminiImageModelIds(googleProvider);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`❌ Failed to fetch image models: ${err.message || err}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (modelIds.length === 0) {
        console.error('❌ No image generation model found');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let model = pickBestImageModel(modelIds);
      let modelSource = 'auto';
      // modelOverride here is a plain Gemini id (from --model, or a configured default, or a
      // `google:<id>` that was prefix-stripped above). Resolve it against the fetched models.
      if (modelOverride) {
        const exact = modelIds.find(id => id === modelOverride);
        const contains = exact ? null : modelIds.find(id => id.includes(modelOverride));
        if (exact || contains) {
          model = exact || contains;
          modelSource = modelFromAutoList ? 'auto' : modelFromConfig ? 'configured' : 'manual';
        } else if (!modelFromConfig) {
          console.error(`❌ Unknown image model: ${modelOverride}`);
          console.error('Available models:');
          for (const m of modelIds) console.error(`  - ${m}`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
        // configured-but-unavailable → keep the auto pick (modelSource stays 'auto')
      }
      if (!model) {
        console.error('❌ No image generation model found');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      console.error(`[Image] Using model: ${model} (${modelSource})`);

      // Auto-inject photorealistic keywords for selfie/person prompts to prevent illustration style
      const lowerPrompt = prompt.toLowerCase();
      const isSelfieOrPerson = lowerPrompt.includes('selfie') || lowerPrompt.includes('自拍') || lowerPrompt.includes('girl') || lowerPrompt.includes('woman') || lowerPrompt.includes('person') || lowerPrompt.includes('portrait') || lowerPrompt.includes('photo of') || lowerPrompt.includes('美女') || lowerPrompt.includes('可爱') || lowerPrompt.includes('吊带');
      const alreadyHasRealism = lowerPrompt.includes('photorealistic') || lowerPrompt.includes('real photograph');
      const referenceImages = [];
      if (sub !== 'edit') {
        for (const refPath of referencePaths) {
          if (_fs.existsSync(refPath)) {
            try {
              const imgData = _fs.readFileSync(refPath);
              const ext = refPath.toLowerCase();
              const mime = ext.endsWith('.png') ? 'image/png' : ext.endsWith('.gif') ? 'image/gif' : 'image/jpeg';
              referenceImages.push({
                inlineData: {
                  mimeType: mime,
                  data: imgData.toString('base64')
                }
              });
            } catch {
              console.error(`⚠️ Failed to read reference: ${refPath}`);
            }
          } else {
            console.error(`⚠️ Reference image not found: ${refPath}`);
          }
        }
      }
      if (referenceImages.length > 0) {
        console.error(`Using ${referenceImages.length} reference image(s) for face consistency`);
      }

      // Build request
      const parts = [];
      if (sub === 'edit') {
        const imgData = _fs.readFileSync(editPath);
        const base64 = imgData.toString('base64');
        const ext = editPath.toLowerCase();
        const mime = ext.endsWith('.png') ? 'image/png' : ext.endsWith('.gif') ? 'image/gif' : 'image/jpeg';
        parts.push({
          inlineData: {
            mimeType: mime,
            data: base64
          }
        });
        parts.push({
          text: prompt
        });
      } else {
        const realismSuffix = isSelfieOrPerson && !alreadyHasRealism ? '\n\nIMPORTANT STYLE: This MUST be a photorealistic real photograph, NOT illustration, NOT anime, NOT cartoon, NOT drawing, NOT digital art. Real skin texture, natural lighting, shot on iPhone. Like a real photo from a smartphone camera.' : '';
        if (referenceImages.length > 0) {
          // Push ALL reference images for stronger face consistency
          for (const refImg of referenceImages) {
            parts.push(refImg);
          }
          const refCount = referenceImages.length;
          const refNote = refCount > 1 ? `I'm providing ${refCount} reference photos of the SAME person from different angles/settings.` : `I'm providing a reference photo.`;
          // Prepend face consistency instruction
          parts.push({
            text: `⚠️ CRITICAL REQUIREMENT — FACE CONSISTENCY IS THE #1 PRIORITY ⚠️\n\n${refNote} You MUST maintain the EXACT SAME face from the reference image(s). The face is NON-NEGOTIABLE:\n- SAME eye shape, eye size, eye color, eye spacing\n- SAME nose shape, nose bridge, nostril width\n- SAME lip shape, lip thickness, mouth width\n- SAME face shape, jawline, chin, cheekbones\n- SAME skin tone, skin texture, complexion\n- SAME eyebrow shape, thickness, arch\n- SAME facial proportions and features\n\nThe person in the generated image MUST be clearly recognizable as the SAME INDIVIDUAL in the reference photo(s). If the face changes even slightly, the output is WRONG. Think of it as the same person taking a different photo — the face NEVER changes, only the pose/setting/outfit/lighting can change.\n\nNow generate this scene with that EXACT same person:\n\n${prompt}${realismSuffix}`
          });
        } else {
          parts.push({
            text: `${prompt}${realismSuffix}`
          });
        }
      }
      console.error(`Generating with ${model}...`);
      const baseUrl = (googleProvider.baseURL || 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com').replace(/\/+$/, '');
      const apiPath = baseUrl.endsWith('/v1beta') ? '' : '/v1beta';
      const resp = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${apiPath}/models/${model}:generateContent?key=${googleProvider.apiKey}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          contents: [{
            parts
          }],
          generationConfig: {
            responseModalities: ['TEXT', 'IMAGE'],
            ...(aspect ? {
              imageConfig: {
                aspectRatio: aspect
              }
            } : {})
          }
        })
      });
      const data = await resp.json();
      if (data.error) {
        console.error(`❌ API error: ${data.error.message}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const respParts = data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts || [];
      const ts = Date.now();
      let saved = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < respParts.length; i++) {
        const part = respParts[i];
        if (part.inlineData) {
          const ext = part.inlineData.mimeType?.includes('png') ? 'png' : 'jpg';
          const outPath = _path.join(_os.tmpdir(), `alma-gen-${ts}-${i}.${ext}`);
          _fs.writeFileSync(outPath, Buffer.from(part.inlineData.data, 'base64'));
          console.log(outPath);
          saved = true;
        } else if (part.text) {
          console.error(part.text);
        }
      }
      if (!saved) {
        console.error('❌ No image generated');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      process.exit(0);
    }
    console.error('Usage:');
    console.error('  alma image models');
    console.error('  alma image generate [--model <model-id>] "<prompt>" [--reference <image-path> ...]');
    console.error('  alma image edit [--model <model-id>] <image-path> "<prompt>"');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Provider Key ──────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'provider-key') {
    const providerType = args[1]; // e.g., 'google', 'openai', 'anthropic'
    if (!providerType) {
      console.error('Usage: alma provider-key <type>  (e.g., google, openai, anthropic)');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const providers = await api('GET', '/api/providers');
    const match = (providers || []).find(p => p.type === providerType && p.apiKey && p.enabled !== false);
    if (match) {
      // Output ONLY the key (no newline decoration) for easy shell capture
      process.stdout.write(match.apiKey);
    } else {
      console.error(`No enabled ${providerType} provider with API key found`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    process.exit(0);
  }

  // ── Soul ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'soul') {
    // SOUL.md is global (in app data dir), not per-workspace
    const almaDataDir = _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma');
    const soulPath = _path.join(almaDataDir, 'SOUL.md');
    const sub = args[1];
    if (!sub || sub === 'show') {
      try {
        const content = _fs.readFileSync(soulPath, 'utf-8');
        console.log(content);
      } catch {
        console.log('No SOUL.md found. Create one with: alma soul set "<content>"');
      }
    } else if (sub === 'edit') {
      console.log(`SOUL.md path: ${soulPath}`);
      console.log('Edit this file directly or use: alma soul set "<content>"');
    } else if (sub === 'set') {
      const content = args.slice(2).join(' ');
      if (!content) {
        console.error('Usage: alma soul set <content>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      _fs.mkdirSync(_path.dirname(soulPath), {
        recursive: true
      });
      _fs.writeFileSync(soulPath, content, 'utf-8');
      console.log('✅ SOUL.md updated');
    } else if (sub === 'append-trait') {
      const trait = args.slice(2).join(' ');
      if (!trait) {
        console.error('Usage: alma soul append-trait "<trait description>"');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        let content = _fs.readFileSync(soulPath, 'utf-8');
        const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        const entry = `- [${today}] ${trait}`;
        // Find "## Evolved Traits" section
        const marker = '## Evolved Traits';
        const idx = content.indexOf(marker);
        if (idx === -1) {
          // Add section at end
          content += `\n\n${marker}\n${entry}\n`;
        } else {
          // Count existing entries to enforce max 15
          const after = content.slice(idx);
          const entries = after.split('\n').filter(l => l.startsWith('- ['));
          if (entries.length >= 15) {
            // Remove oldest entry
            const oldestLine = entries[0];
            content = content.replace(oldestLine + '\n', '');
          }
          // Append new entry at end of file (Evolved Traits is last section)
          content = content.trimEnd() + '\n' + entry + '\n';
        }
        _fs.writeFileSync(soulPath, content, 'utf-8');
        console.log(`✅ Trait added: ${entry}`);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to append trait:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else {
      console.error('Usage: alma soul [show|edit|set|append-trait <trait>]');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    process.exit(0);
  }

  // ── User Profile ──────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'user') {
    const almaDataDir = _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma');
    const userPath = _path.join(almaDataDir, 'USER.md');
    const sub = args[1];
    if (!sub || sub === 'show') {
      try {
        const content = _fs.readFileSync(userPath, 'utf-8');
        console.log(content);
      } catch {
        console.log('No USER.md found. Create one with: alma user set "<content>"');
        console.log('Or let Alma create it for you by telling her about yourself.');
      }
    } else if (sub === 'set') {
      const content = args.slice(2).join(' ');
      if (!content) {
        console.error('Usage: alma user set "<content>"');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      _fs.mkdirSync(_path.dirname(userPath), {
        recursive: true
      });
      _fs.writeFileSync(userPath, content, 'utf-8');
      console.log('✅ USER.md updated');
    } else if (sub === 'edit') {
      console.log(`USER.md path: ${userPath}`);
      console.log('Edit this file directly or use: alma user set "<content>"');
    } else {
      console.error('Usage: alma user [show|set|edit]');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    process.exit(0);
  }

  // ── Voices ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'voices') {
    const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
    const provider = settings?.tts?.provider || 'elevenlabs';
    const apiKey = settings?.tts?.apiKey;
    const currentVoiceId = settings?.tts?.voiceId;
    if (provider === 'elevenlabs') {
      if (!apiKey) {
        console.error('❌ No ElevenLabs API key configured. Set it with: alma config set tts.apiKey <key>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const resp = await fetch('https://api.elevenlabs.io/v1/voices', {
          headers: {
            'xi-api-key': apiKey
          }
        });
        const data = await resp.json();
        const voices = data.voices || [];
        if (voices.length === 0) {
          console.log('No voices found.');
          return;
        }
        console.log(`Available ElevenLabs voices (${voices.length} total):\n`);
        for (const v of voices) {
          const labels = v.labels || {};
          const lang = labels.language || '?';
          const gender = labels.gender || '?';
          const accent = labels.accent || '';
          const desc = labels.descriptive || '';
          const current = v.voice_id === currentVoiceId ? ' ← current' : '';
          console.log(`  ${v.voice_id}  ${v.name}  [${lang}/${gender}] ${accent} ${desc}${current}`);
        }
        console.log(`\nTo change voice: alma config set tts.voiceId <voice_id>`);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to fetch voices:', err.message);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (provider === 'openai') {
      console.log('OpenAI TTS voices: alloy, echo, fable, onyx, nova, shimmer');
      console.log(`Current: ${currentVoiceId || '(not set)'}`);
      console.log('\nTo change: alma config set tts.voiceId <voice_name>');
    } else if (provider === 'local' || provider === 'qwen') {
      console.log('Local Qwen3-TTS voices:\n');
      const localVoices = [{
        id: 'Chelsie',
        lang: 'en',
        gender: 'female',
        desc: 'warm, clear'
      }, {
        id: 'Aidan',
        lang: 'en',
        gender: 'male',
        desc: 'deep, steady'
      }, {
        id: 'Serena',
        lang: 'en',
        gender: 'female',
        desc: 'cute, lively'
      }, {
        id: 'Vivian',
        lang: 'zh',
        gender: 'female',
        desc: '温柔, 自然'
      }, {
        id: 'Ono_anna',
        lang: 'ja',
        gender: 'female',
        desc: 'Japanese'
      }, {
        id: 'Sohee',
        lang: 'ko',
        gender: 'female',
        desc: 'Korean'
      }, {
        id: 'Uncle_fu',
        lang: 'zh',
        gender: 'male',
        desc: '成熟, 稳重'
      }, {
        id: 'Ryan',
        lang: 'en',
        gender: 'male',
        desc: 'deep'
      }, {
        id: 'Aiden',
        lang: 'en',
        gender: 'male',
        desc: 'young'
      }, {
        id: 'Eric',
        lang: 'en',
        gender: 'male',
        desc: 'professional'
      }, {
        id: 'Dylan',
        lang: 'en',
        gender: 'male',
        desc: 'casual'
      }];
      for (const v of localVoices) {
        const current = v.id.toLowerCase() === (currentVoiceId || '').toLowerCase() ? ' ← current' : '';
        console.log(`  ${v.id.padEnd(12)} [${v.lang}/${v.gender}] ${v.desc}${current}`);
      }
      console.log(`\nTo change: alma config set tts.voiceId <voice_name>`);
      console.log('Note: Qwen3-TTS supports any voice name. These are the pre-tested ones.');
    } else {
      console.log(`Unknown TTS provider: ${provider}`);
      console.log('Supported providers: local, openai, elevenlabs');
    }
    return;
  }

  // ── Skills (powered by skills.sh ecosystem) ─────────────
  if (cmd === 'skill' && (args[1] === 'search' || args[1] === 'find') && args[2]) {
    const query = args.slice(2).join(' ');
    const {
      execSync
    } = await import('child_process');
    const runner = getPackageRunner();
    if (!runner) {
      console.error('Error: No package runner found. Install Node.js (npx) or Bun (bunx).');
      return;
    }
    try {
      execSync(`${runner} skills find ${JSON.stringify(query)}`, {
        stdio: 'inherit'
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Search failed:', e.message);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'skill' && (args[1] === 'list' || !args[1])) {
    const skills = await api('GET', '/api/skills');
    if (Array.isArray(skills)) {
      for (const s of skills) {
        console.log(`${s.name || s.id}  [${s.source || 'unknown'}]  ${s.description || ''}`);
      }
    } else {
      prettyPrint(skills);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'skill' && args[1] === 'create') {
    const name = args[2];
    const fileFlagIdx = args.indexOf('--file');
    const fileSrc = fileFlagIdx !== -1 ? args[fileFlagIdx + 1] : undefined;
    if (!name || name.startsWith('--') || !fileSrc) {
      console.error('Usage: alma skill create <name> --file <SKILL.md|-> (full SKILL.md with --- frontmatter: name, description)');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const fs = await import('fs');
    const path = await import('path');
    const os = await import('os');
    let content;
    if (fileSrc === '-') {
      content = await new Promise(resolve => {
        let buf = '';
        process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');
        process.stdin.on('data', c => buf += c);
        process.stdin.on('end', () => resolve(buf));
      });
    } else {
      content = fs.readFileSync(fileSrc, 'utf-8');
    }
    if (!/^---\n[\s\S]*?\n---/.test(content) || !/\bname:\s*\S/.test(content) || !/\bdescription:\s*\S/.test(content)) {
      console.error('Error: content must be a full SKILL.md with --- frontmatter containing name and description');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const dir = path.join(os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'skills', name);
    if (fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, 'SKILL.md'))) {
      console.error(`Error: skill "${name}" already exists at ${dir}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, {
      recursive: true
    });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'SKILL.md'), content);
    try {
      await api('POST', '/api/skills/refresh', {});
    } catch {
      // App not running — the skill is on disk and will load on next start.
    }
    console.log(`Installed personal skill "${name}" at ${dir}`);
    console.log(`Reference: alma://skill/${name}`);
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'skill' && args[1] === 'install' && args[2]) {
    const source = args[2];
    const {
      execSync
    } = await import('child_process');
    const os = await import('os');
    const path = await import('path');
    const skillsDir = path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'skills');

    // If source looks like owner/repo@skill (skills.sh format), use skills CLI
    if (source.includes('@') || source.match(/^[\w-]+\/[\w-]+$/)) {
      const runner = getPackageRunner();
      console.log(`Installing skill from skills.sh: ${source}...`);
      try {
        if (!runner) throw new Error('No package runner');
        execSync(`${runner} skills add ${JSON.stringify(source)} -g -y`, {
          stdio: 'inherit',
          cwd: skillsDir
        });
        console.log(`✅ Installed: ${source}`);
      } catch (e) {
        // Fallback to git clone for non-skills.sh repos
        console.log('skills.sh install failed, trying git clone...');
        try {
          const gitUrl = source.startsWith('http') ? source : `https://github.com/${source}`;
          const repoName = source.split('/').pop().split('@')[0].replace('.git', '');
          execSync(`git clone --depth 1 ${gitUrl} ${path.join(skillsDir, repoName)}`, {
            stdio: 'inherit'
          });
          console.log(`✅ Installed via git: ${repoName}`);
        } catch (e2) {
          console.error('Install failed:', e2.message);
        }
      }
    } else {
      // Direct git URL or other format
      console.log(`Installing skill from: ${source}...`);
      try {
        const gitUrl = source.startsWith('http') ? source : `https://github.com/${source}`;
        const repoName = source.split('/').pop().replace('.git', '');
        execSync(`git clone --depth 1 ${gitUrl} ${path.join(skillsDir, repoName)}`, {
          stdio: 'inherit'
        });
        console.log(`✅ Installed: ${repoName}`);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Install failed:', e.message);
      }
    }
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'skill' && args[1] === 'update') {
    const {
      execSync
    } = await import('child_process');
    const runner = getPackageRunner();
    if (!runner) {
      console.error('Error: No package runner found. Install Node.js (npx) or Bun (bunx).');
      return;
    }
    console.log('Checking for skill updates...');
    try {
      execSync(`${runner} skills check`, {
        stdio: 'inherit'
      });
      execSync(`${runner} skills update`, {
        stdio: 'inherit'
      });
      console.log('✅ Skills updated.');
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Update failed:', e.message);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'skill' && args[1] === 'uninstall' && args[2]) {
    const os = await import('os');
    const path = await import('path');
    const fs = await import('fs');
    const skillPath = path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'skills', args[2]);
    if (fs.existsSync(skillPath)) {
      fs.rmSync(skillPath, {
        recursive: true
      });
      console.log(`✅ Uninstalled: ${args[2]}`);
    } else {
      console.error(`Skill not found: ${args[2]}`);
    }
    return;
  }

  // ── Usage ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'usage') {
    const stats = await api('GET', '/api/usage/stats');
    prettyPrint(stats);
    return;
  }

  // ── Export / Import ─────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'export') {
    const fs = await import('fs');
    const resp = await apiRaw('GET', '/api/data/export');
    const data = await resp.text();
    const filename = `alma-export-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
    fs.writeFileSync(filename, data);
    console.log(`✅ Data exported to: ${filename}`);
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'import') {
    const file = args[1];
    if (!file) {
      console.error('Usage: alma import <file>');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const fs = await import('fs');
    if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
      console.error(`File not found: ${file}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const data = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf-8'));
    await api('POST', '/api/data/import', data);
    console.log(`✅ Data imported from: ${file}`);
    return;
  }

  // ── Project (agent-friendly alias over workspaces) ──────
  if (cmd === 'project') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'list' || !subcmd) {
      const workspaces = await api('GET', '/api/workspaces');
      const projects = Array.isArray(workspaces) ? workspaces.filter(w => !w.isTemporary && !w.isWorktree) : [];
      if (projects.length === 0) {
        console.log('No projects. Use "alma project create <name>" to make one.');
        return;
      }
      for (const w of projects) {
        const count = w.threadCount != null ? `  [${w.threadCount} threads]` : '';
        console.log(`${w.id}  ${w.name || '(unnamed)'}  ${w.path || ''}${count}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'create') {
      const createArgs = args.slice(2);
      let projectPath;
      const pathIdx = createArgs.indexOf('--path');
      if (pathIdx !== -1) {
        projectPath = createArgs[pathIdx + 1];
        if (!projectPath) {
          console.error('Usage: alma project create <name> [--path <dir>]');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        createArgs.splice(pathIdx, 2);
      }
      const name = createArgs.join(' ').trim();
      if (!name) {
        console.error('Usage: alma project create <name> [--path <dir>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (!projectPath) {
        const slug = name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '') || 'project';
        projectPath = _path.join(_os.homedir(), 'AlmaProjects', slug);
      }
      try {
        _fs.mkdirSync(projectPath, {
          recursive: true
        });
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(`⚠️  Failed to create directory ${projectPath}: ${e.message}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const project = await api('POST', '/api/workspaces', {
        name,
        path: projectPath
      });
      console.log(`✅ Created project: ${project.id}  "${project.name}"`);
      console.log(`   Path: ${project.path}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'show') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma project show <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const ws = await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${id}`);
      prettyPrint(ws);
      return;
    }

    // Resolve a project id argument, allowing "current" (from ALMA_THREAD_ID).
    const resolveProjectId = async raw => {
      if (raw && raw !== 'current') return raw;
      const tid = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
      if (!tid) {
        console.error('Error: pass a project id, or run inside a thread (ALMA_THREAD_ID) and use "current".');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const thread = await api('GET', `/api/threads/${tid}`);
      if (!thread?.workspaceId) {
        console.error('Error: the current thread has no project.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return thread.workspaceId;
    };
    if (subcmd === 'card') {
      const action = args[2] && !args[2].startsWith('alma://') && ['propose', 'set', 'draft'].includes(args[2]) ? args[2] : null;
      if (!action) {
        // alma project card <id|current> — show
        const id = await resolveProjectId(args[2]);
        const {
          card,
          config
        } = await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${id}/project-card`);
        const ws = await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${id}`);
        console.log(`Project: ${ws.name || '(unnamed)'}  (${ws.path})  [${id}]`);
        if (!card && !config) {
          console.log('No project card yet. The user can create one in the sidebar project card UI,');
          console.log('or you can suggest one: alma project card propose <id> --brief "..." --focus "..." --reason "..."');
          return;
        }
        if (card?.brief) console.log(`What it is: ${card.brief}`);
        if (card?.focus) console.log(`Current focus: ${card.focus}`);
        if (card?.resume?.length) {
          console.log('Resume from:');
          for (const r of card.resume) console.log(`  - ${r.label} → ${r.uri}${r.note ? `  (${r.note})` : ''}`);
        }
        if (card?.proposal) {
          console.log('⏳ Pending proposal (awaiting user confirmation):');
          if (card.proposal.brief) console.log(`  brief → ${card.proposal.brief}`);
          if (card.proposal.focus) console.log(`  focus → ${card.proposal.focus}`);
          if (card.proposal.resume) console.log(`  resume → ${card.proposal.resume.map(r => r.uri).join(', ')}`);
          console.log(`  reason: ${card.proposal.reason}`);
        }
        if (config) {
          if (config.preferredAgents?.length) console.log(`Resident collaborators: ${config.preferredAgents.join(', ')}`);
          if (config.preferredSkills?.length) console.log(`Preferred skills: ${config.preferredSkills.join(', ')}`);
          if (config.defaultModel) console.log(`Default model: ${config.defaultModel}${config.defaultReasoningEffort ? ` · ${config.defaultReasoningEffort}` : ''}`);
          const r = config.restricted || {};
          const bits = [];
          if (r.tools?.length) bits.push(`tools: ${r.tools.join(', ')}`);
          if (r.mcpServers?.length) bits.push(`MCP: ${r.mcpServers.join(', ')}`);
          if (r.models?.length) bits.push(`models: ${r.models.join(', ')}`);
          if (r.agents?.length) bits.push(`agents: ${r.agents.join(', ')}`);
          if (bits.length) console.log(`Boundary (only these are allowed): ${bits.join(' | ')}`);
          if (config.completionTemplates?.length) console.log(`Completion templates: ${config.completionTemplates.map(t => t.name).join(', ')}`);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'draft') {
        // Draft via the tool model → stored as a proposal (user confirms in UI).
        const id = await resolveProjectId(args[3]);
        const hintIdx = args.indexOf('--hint');
        const hint = hintIdx !== -1 && args[hintIdx + 1] ? args[hintIdx + 1] : '';
        const r = await api('POST', `/api/workspaces/${id}/project-card/draft`, {
          hint
        });
        console.log('✅ Draft recorded as a pending proposal — the user confirms it in the project card UI.');
        if (r.card?.proposal?.brief) console.log(`   brief: ${r.card.proposal.brief}`);
        if (r.card?.proposal?.focus) console.log(`   focus: ${r.card.proposal.focus}`);
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'propose') {
        // Agent-allowed: writes a PROPOSAL the user confirms in the UI.
        const id = await resolveProjectId(args[3]);
        const body = {
          source: process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID ? `thread:${process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID}` : 'cli'
        };
        const resume = [];
        for (let i = 4; i < args.length; i++) {
          if (args[i] === '--brief' && args[i + 1]) body.brief = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--focus' && args[i + 1]) body.focus = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--reason' && args[i + 1]) body.reason = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--resume' && args[i + 1]) {
            const uri = args[++i];
            const label = args[i + 1] && !args[i + 1].startsWith('--') && !args[i + 1].startsWith('alma://') ? args[++i] : uri;
            resume.push({
              uri,
              label
            });
          }
        }
        if (resume.length) body.resume = resume;
        if (!body.brief && !body.focus && !body.resume) {
          console.error('Usage: alma project card propose <id|current> [--brief "..."] [--focus "..."] [--resume alma://kind/id [label]]... --reason "..."');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        await api('POST', `/api/workspaces/${id}/project-card/propose`, body);
        console.log("✅ Proposal recorded — pending the USER's confirmation in the project card UI.");
        console.log('   Do not present it as applied; the confirmed card stays unchanged until the user accepts.');
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'set') {
        // Confirmed write — the user's act. Never from a model-driven shell.
        if (process.env.ALMA_TOOL_CALL_ID) {
          console.error("Refused: the confirmed project card is the USER's. Use `alma project card propose` — the user confirms it in the project card UI.");
          process.exit(1);
        }
        const id = await resolveProjectId(args[3]);
        const {
          card
        } = await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${id}/project-card`);
        const body = {
          brief: card?.brief || '',
          focus: card?.focus || '',
          resume: card?.resume || []
        };
        for (let i = 4; i < args.length; i++) {
          if (args[i] === '--brief' && args[i + 1]) body.brief = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--focus' && args[i + 1]) body.focus = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--resume' && args[i + 1]) {
            if (!Array.isArray(body._newResume)) body._newResume = [];
            const uri = args[++i];
            const label = args[i + 1] && !args[i + 1].startsWith('--') && !args[i + 1].startsWith('alma://') ? args[++i] : uri;
            body._newResume.push({
              uri,
              label
            });
          } else if (args[i] === '--clear-resume') body.resume = [];
        }
        if (Array.isArray(body._newResume)) {
          body.resume = body._newResume;
          delete body._newResume;
        }
        const r = await api('PUT', `/api/workspaces/${id}/project-card`, body);
        console.log('✅ Project card updated (confirmed).');
        if (r.card?.focus) console.log(`   Current focus: ${r.card.focus}`);
        return;
      }
    }
    if (subcmd === 'config') {
      const maybeAction = args[2] === 'set' ? 'set' : null;
      if (!maybeAction) {
        const id = await resolveProjectId(args[2]);
        const {
          config
        } = await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${id}/project-card`);
        if (!config) {
          console.log('No project run configuration. The user can set one: alma project config set <id> --json \'{"preferredAgents":["researcher"],"defaultModel":"provider:model"}\'');
          return;
        }
        prettyPrint(config);
        return;
      }
      // set — user-level: run defaults and boundaries are the user's decision.
      if (process.env.ALMA_TOOL_CALL_ID) {
        console.error('Refused: project run configuration (defaults & boundaries) is set by the user, not from inside a conversation.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const id = await resolveProjectId(args[3]);
      const jsonIdx = args.indexOf('--json');
      if (jsonIdx === -1 || !args[jsonIdx + 1]) {
        console.error("Usage: alma project config set <id|current> --json '{...}'");
        console.error('Keys: preferredAgents[], preferredSkills[], defaultModel, defaultReasoningEffort,');
        console.error('      restricted{tools[],mcpServers[],models[],agents[]}, completionTemplates[{name,condition}]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let cfg;
      try {
        cfg = JSON.parse(args[jsonIdx + 1]);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(`Invalid JSON: ${e.message}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('PUT', `/api/workspaces/${id}/project-config`, cfg);
      console.log('✅ Project run configuration updated.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'delete') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma project delete <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('DELETE', `/api/workspaces/${id}`);
      console.log(`✅ Deleted project: ${id}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'current') {
      const tid = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
      if (!tid) {
        console.error('Error: ALMA_THREAD_ID not set. Run this from within a thread context.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let thread;
      try {
        thread = await api('GET', `/api/threads/${tid}`);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(`Error: thread ${tid} not found (${e.message || e}).`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (!thread?.workspaceId) {
        console.log('(uncategorized — no workspaceId on current thread)');
        return;
      }
      let ws;
      try {
        ws = await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${thread.workspaceId}`);
      } catch {
        console.log(`(workspace ${thread.workspaceId} not found)`);
        return;
      }
      if (ws?.isTemporary) {
        console.log(`(uncategorized — temporary workspace ${ws.id})`);
        return;
      }
      console.log(`${ws.id}  ${ws.name || '(unnamed)'}  ${ws.path || ''}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'switch') {
      const projectId = args[2];
      if (!projectId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma project switch <projectId> [title]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const title = args.slice(3).join(' ') || 'New Thread';
      const sourceThreadId = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
      if (!sourceThreadId) {
        console.error('Error: ALMA_THREAD_ID not set. Cannot determine current thread to switch from.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const newThread = await api('POST', '/api/threads', {
        title,
        workspaceId: projectId
      });
      const result = await api('POST', `/api/threads/${newThread.id}/switch`, {
        sourceThreadId
      });
      if (result.success) {
        console.log(`✅ Created and switched to thread: "${newThread.title || title}"  [${newThread.id}]`);
        console.log(`   In project: ${projectId}`);
      } else {
        console.error('❌ Switch failed:', JSON.stringify(result));
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'assign') {
      const threadId = args[2];
      const projectId = args[3];
      if (!threadId || !projectId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma project assign <threadId> <projectId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('PUT', `/api/threads/${threadId}`, {
        workspaceId: projectId
      });
      console.log(`✅ Thread ${threadId} assigned to project ${projectId}`);
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma project <list|create|show|delete|current|switch|assign>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Workspace ───────────────────────────────────────────
  // ── Plan Weave (file-backed task-graph plans) ────────────
  if (cmd === 'loop') {
    const flag = name => {
      const i = args.indexOf(name);
      return i !== -1 ? args[i + 1] : undefined;
    };
    const threadId = flag('--thread') || process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
    if (!threadId) {
      console.error('No thread. Run this inside an Alma session, or pass --thread <id>.');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const rest = args.slice(1).filter(a => a !== '--thread' && a !== threadId);
    const sub = rest[0];
    if (!sub) {
      const r = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/threads/${threadId}/loop`);
      const {
        loop
      } = await r.json();
      if (!loop) return console.log('No loop running');
      const mins = Math.round(loop.intervalMs / 60000);
      console.log(`${loop.stoppedAt ? 'stopped' : 'running'}: ${loop.prompt}`);
      console.log(`  every ${mins}m, ${loop.iterations} iteration(s)`);
      if (loop.stoppedReason) console.log(`  ${loop.stoppedReason}`);
      return;
    }
    if (['stop', 'clear', 'off', 'cancel', 'none'].includes(sub)) {
      const r = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/threads/${threadId}/loop`, {
        method: 'DELETE'
      });
      const {
        stopped
      } = await r.json();
      console.log(stopped ? `Loop stopped: ${stopped}` : 'No loop running');
      return;
    }
    const r = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/threads/${threadId}/loop`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        prompt: rest.join(' ')
      })
    });
    if (!r.ok) {
      console.error(`Failed: ${(await r.json()).error || r.status}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const {
      loop,
      interval
    } = await r.json();
    console.log(`Loop set: ${loop.prompt}`);
    console.log(`Running every ${interval}. Stop it with: alma loop stop`);
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'goal') {
    // Goals belong to a thread, so the thread has to be named. Inside a
    // session Alma exports it; outside one, pass --thread.
    const flag = name => {
      const i = args.indexOf(name);
      return i !== -1 ? args[i + 1] : undefined;
    };
    const threadId = flag('--thread') || process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
    if (!threadId) {
      console.error('No thread. Run this inside an Alma session, or pass --thread <id>.');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const rest = args.slice(1).filter(a => a !== '--thread' && a !== threadId);
    const sub = rest[0];
    const show = g => {
      if (!g) return console.log('No goal set');
      const mins = Math.round((Date.now() - g.setAt) / 60000);
      const state = g.achievedAt ? 'finished' : 'active';
      console.log(`${state}: ${g.condition}`);
      console.log(`  ${g.turnsEvaluated} turn(s) evaluated, running ${mins}m`);
      if (g.lastReason) console.log(`  last check: ${g.lastReason}`);
    };
    if (!sub) {
      const r = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/threads/${threadId}/goal`);
      show((await r.json()).goal);
      return;
    }
    if (['clear', 'stop', 'off', 'reset', 'none', 'cancel'].includes(sub)) {
      const r = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/threads/${threadId}/goal`, {
        method: 'DELETE'
      });
      const {
        cleared
      } = await r.json();
      console.log(cleared ? `Goal cleared: ${cleared}` : 'No goal set');
      return;
    }
    const condition = rest.join(' ');
    const r = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/threads/${threadId}/goal`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        condition
      })
    });
    if (!r.ok) {
      console.error(`Failed: ${(await r.json()).error || r.status}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const {
      goal,
      started
    } = await r.json();
    console.log(`Goal set: ${goal.condition}`);
    console.log(started ? 'Working on it now.' : 'A turn is already running; it will be evaluated when that finishes.');
    return;
  }
  if (cmd === 'plan') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    const planCtx = () => {
      const ctx = {
        dir: process.cwd()
      };
      if (process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID) ctx.threadId = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
      return ctx;
    };
    const planQuery = () => {
      const params = new URLSearchParams(planCtx());
      return params.toString();
    };
    const wantsJson = args.includes('--json');
    const flagValue = name => {
      const idx = args.indexOf(name);
      return idx !== -1 ? args[idx + 1] : undefined;
    };
    /** Read content from "-" (stdin) or a file path. */
    const readContentArg = (value, label) => {
      if (!value) {
        console.error(`Error: ${label} is required`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (value === '-') return _fs.readFileSync(0, 'utf-8');
      if (_fs.existsSync(value)) return _fs.readFileSync(value, 'utf-8');
      return value; // literal text (e.g. short review notes)
    };
    const statusGlyph = block => {
      if (block.status === 'done') return block.outcome === 'max_cycles' ? '◐' : '✔';
      if (block.status === 'in_progress') return '▶';
      if (block.status === 'ready') return '○';
      if (block.status === 'blocked') return '✖';
      return '·';
    };
    const printSnapshot = snapshot => {
      if (!snapshot.exists) {
        console.log('No plan in this workspace. Create one with: alma plan set --file <plan.json>');
        console.log('See the format with: alma plan template');
        return;
      }
      console.log(`Plan: ${snapshot.title}  (${snapshot.progress.done}/${snapshot.progress.total} blocks done)`);
      console.log(`Goal: ${snapshot.goal}`);
      console.log('');
      for (const task of snapshot.tasks) {
        const deps = task.deps.length > 0 ? `  needs:${task.deps.join(',')}` : '';
        console.log(`[${task.status}] ${task.id}  ${task.title}${deps}`);
        for (const block of task.blocks) {
          const cycles = block.type === 'review' && block.cycles > 0 ? `  cycles:${block.cycles}/${block.maxReviewCycles}` : '';
          const runs = block.type === 'implementation' && block.runs > 1 ? `  runs:${block.runs}` : '';
          const blocked = block.status === 'blocked' ? `  (${block.blockedReason || 'blocked'})` : '';
          const outcome = block.outcome === 'max_cycles' ? '  ⚠ closed at cycle limit' : '';
          console.log(`  ${statusGlyph(block)} ${block.ref}  ${block.title}  [${block.type} · ${block.status}]${runs}${cycles}${outcome}${blocked}`);
        }
      }
      console.log('');
      if (snapshot.current) console.log(`Current: ${snapshot.current.kind} ${snapshot.current.id} (claimed ${formatDate(snapshot.current.claimedAt)})`);
      if (snapshot.openFeedback) console.log(`Open feedback: ${snapshot.openFeedback.id} from ${snapshot.openFeedback.sourceRef} — resolve with: alma plan claim`);
      if (!snapshot.current && !snapshot.openFeedback) {
        const allDone = snapshot.progress.total > 0 && snapshot.progress.done === snapshot.progress.total;
        console.log(allDone ? '🎉 Plan complete.' : 'Next: alma plan claim');
      }
    };
    if (subcmd === 'template') {
      console.log(JSON.stringify({
        title: 'Short plan name',
        goal: 'What "done" means for the whole plan',
        tasks: [{
          id: 'T1',
          title: 'First task',
          description: 'Optional context for every block in this task',
          acceptance: ['Concrete, verifiable criterion', 'Another criterion'],
          blocks: [{
            id: 'B1',
            type: 'implementation',
            title: 'Do the work',
            instructions: 'Exactly what to build/change, scoped and verifiable.'
          }, {
            id: 'R1',
            type: 'review',
            title: 'Review the work',
            instructions: 'Judge against the acceptance criteria. Do not fix anything yourself.',
            deps: ['B1'],
            maxReviewCycles: 3
          }]
        }, {
          id: 'T2',
          title: 'Second task (runs after T1)',
          deps: ['T1'],
          blocks: [{
            id: 'B1',
            type: 'implementation',
            title: 'Follow-up work',
            instructions: 'What to do once T1 is fully done.'
          }]
        }]
      }, null, 2));
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'set') {
      const file = flagValue('--file');
      const content = readContentArg(file, '--file <plan.json|->');
      let plan;
      try {
        plan = JSON.parse(content);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(`Error: plan file is not valid JSON — ${e.message}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        ...planCtx(),
        plan,
        force: args.includes('--force')
      };
      const snapshot = await api('POST', '/api/plan', body);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(snapshot);
      console.log(`✅ Plan "${snapshot.title}" created — ${snapshot.progress.total} blocks across ${snapshot.tasks.length} tasks.`);
      console.log('Start executing with: alma plan claim');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'status' || !subcmd) {
      const snapshot = await api('GET', `/api/plan?${planQuery()}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(snapshot);
      printSnapshot(snapshot);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'claim') {
      const claim = await api('POST', '/api/plan/claim', planCtx());
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(claim);
      if (claim.kind === 'none') {
        console.log(claim.reason);
        return;
      }
      if (claim.alreadyClaimed) console.log('(resuming the item you already claimed)\n');
      if (claim.agent) console.log(`Suggested agent: ${claim.agent}\n`);
      console.log(claim.packet);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'show') {
      const ref = args[2];
      if (!ref) {
        console.error('Usage: alma plan show <taskId:blockId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const detail = await api('GET', `/api/plan/block?${planQuery()}&ref=${encodeURIComponent(ref)}`);
      prettyPrint(detail);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'submit') {
      const ref = args[2];
      if (!ref || ref.startsWith('--')) {
        console.error('Usage: alma plan submit <taskId:blockId> --report <file|->');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const report = readContentArg(flagValue('--report'), '--report <file|->');
      const result = await api('POST', '/api/plan/submit', {
        ...planCtx(),
        ref,
        report
      });
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(result);
      console.log(`✅ ${result.ref} done (run ${result.runId}). Report: ${result.reportPath}`);
      console.log(`Next: ${result.next}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'review') {
      const ref = args[2];
      const verdict = flagValue('--verdict');
      if (!ref || ref.startsWith('--') || !verdict) {
        console.error('Usage: alma plan review <taskId:blockId> --verdict <passed|needs_changes> --notes <text|file|->');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const notes = readContentArg(flagValue('--notes') ?? '', '--notes') || '';
      const result = await api('POST', '/api/plan/review', {
        ...planCtx(),
        ref,
        verdict,
        notes
      });
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(result);
      if (result.verdict === 'passed') {
        console.log(`✅ ${result.ref} passed review.`);
      } else if (result.outcome === 'max_cycles') {
        console.log(`⚠️ ${result.ref} hit its review cycle limit (${result.cycles}) — gate closed as max_cycles.`);
      } else {
        console.log(`↩️ ${result.ref} needs changes — feedback ${result.feedbackId} opened (cycle ${result.cycles}).`);
      }
      console.log(`Next: ${result.next}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'resolve') {
      const report = readContentArg(flagValue('--report'), '--report <file|->');
      const result = await api('POST', '/api/plan/resolve', {
        ...planCtx(),
        report
      });
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(result);
      console.log(`✅ Feedback ${result.feedbackId} resolved — ${result.reviewRef} is ready for re-review.`);
      console.log(`Next: ${result.next}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'block' || subcmd === 'unblock') {
      const ref = args[2];
      if (!ref || ref.startsWith('--')) {
        console.error(`Usage: alma plan ${subcmd} <taskId:blockId>${subcmd === 'block' ? ' --reason <text>' : ''}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        ...planCtx(),
        ref,
        blocked: subcmd === 'block',
        reason: flagValue('--reason')
      };
      const snapshot = await api('POST', '/api/plan/blocked', body);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(snapshot);
      console.log(subcmd === 'block' ? `✖ ${ref} blocked.` : `✅ ${ref} unblocked.`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'reset') {
      const snapshot = await api('POST', '/api/plan/reset', planCtx());
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(snapshot);
      console.log('✅ Plan state reset — all results cleared, manifest kept.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'archive') {
      const result = await api('POST', '/api/plan/archive', planCtx());
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(result);
      console.log(result.archivedTo ? `✅ Plan archived to: ${result.archivedTo}` : 'No plan to archive.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'delete') {
      if (!args.includes('--force')) {
        console.error('This permanently deletes the plan, its state, and all results.');
        console.error('Use "alma plan archive" to keep a copy, or re-run with --force.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('DELETE', '/api/plan', planCtx());
      console.log('✅ Plan deleted.');
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma plan <template|set|status|claim|show|submit|review|resolve|block|unblock|reset|archive|delete>');
    process.exit(1);
  }
  if (cmd === 'ref' || cmd === 'refs' || cmd === 'reference') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    const wantsJson = args.includes('--json');
    const flagValue = name => {
      const idx = args.indexOf(name);
      return idx !== -1 ? args[idx + 1] : undefined;
    };
    const threadId = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
    const ctxQuery = () => threadId ? `&threadId=${encodeURIComponent(threadId)}` : '';
    const positional = args.slice(2).filter((a, i, arr) => !a.startsWith('--') && !(i > 0 && arr[i - 1].startsWith('--') && ['--kind', '--limit', '--title', '--depth', '--thread'].includes(arr[i - 1])));
    const printCard = r => {
      const flag = r.exists ? '' : '  (MISSING)';
      console.log(`${r.uri}${flag}`);
      console.log(`  kind:     ${r.kind}`);
      console.log(`  title:    ${r.title}`);
      if (r.subtitle) console.log(`  subtitle: ${r.subtitle}`);
      if (r.summary) console.log(`  summary:  ${r.summary}`);
      if (r.actions && r.actions.length) {
        console.log('  next:');
        for (const a of r.actions) console.log(`    - ${a}`);
      }
      if (r.body) {
        console.log('  body:');
        console.log(r.body.split('\n').map(l => `    ${l}`).join('\n'));
      }
    };
    if (!subcmd || subcmd === 'help') {
      console.log('Usage: alma ref <kinds|search|resolve|backlinks|outlinks|graph|snippet|open|token> …');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'time-index') {
      const target = args[2];
      const offset = Number(flagValue('--offset') ?? '0');
      const chunkOffset = Number(flagValue('--chunk-offset') ?? '0');
      if (!target || target.startsWith('--') || !Number.isSafeInteger(offset) || offset < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(chunkOffset) || chunkOffset < 0) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref time-index <thread-id> [--offset 0] [--chunk-offset 0] --json');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return prettyPrint(await api('POST', '/api/refs/time-index', {
        threadId: target,
        offset,
        chunkOffset
      }));
    }
    if (subcmd === 'kinds') {
      const {
        kinds
      } = await api('GET', '/api/refs/kinds');
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(kinds);
      for (const k of kinds) console.log(`${k.kind.padEnd(10)} ${k.available ? '✓' : '✗'}  @${k.prefixes[0]}:…   ${k.label}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'search' || subcmd === 'find') {
      const query = positional.join(' ');
      const kind = flagValue('--kind');
      const limit = flagValue('--limit');
      const qs = `q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}${kind ? `&kinds=${encodeURIComponent(kind)}` : ''}${limit ? `&limit=${encodeURIComponent(limit)}` : ''}${ctxQuery()}`;
      const {
        results
      } = await api('GET', `/api/refs/search?${qs}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(results);
      if (!results.length) {
        console.log('No matches.');
        return;
      }
      for (const r of results) {
        console.log(`${r.uri}`);
        console.log(`  [${r.kind}] ${r.title}${r.subtitle ? ` — ${r.subtitle}` : ''}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'resolve' || subcmd === 'show' || subcmd === 'get') {
      const uris = positional.filter(a => a.startsWith('alma://'));
      if (!uris.length) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref resolve <alma://kind/id> [--full] [--json]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const full = args.includes('--full');
      if (uris.length === 1) {
        const r = await api('GET', `/api/refs/resolve?uri=${encodeURIComponent(uris[0])}${full ? '&full=1' : ''}${ctxQuery()}`);
        if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(r);
        printCard(r);
        return;
      }
      const {
        results
      } = await api('POST', '/api/refs/resolve', {
        uris,
        full,
        threadId
      });
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(results);
      results.forEach((r, i) => {
        if (i) console.log('');
        printCard(r);
      });
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'backlinks' || subcmd === 'mentions') {
      const uri = positional[0];
      if (!uri) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref backlinks <alma://kind/id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const limit = flagValue('--limit');
      const {
        backlinks
      } = await api('GET', `/api/refs/backlinks?uri=${encodeURIComponent(uri)}${limit ? `&limit=${encodeURIComponent(limit)}` : ''}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(backlinks);
      if (!backlinks.length) {
        console.log('No chat messages mention this object yet.');
        console.log('(backlinks lists @-mentions from chat messages only — references written in file/skill bodies and links made with `alma ref link` show under `alma ref related <uri>`.)');
        return;
      }
      for (const b of backlinks) {
        console.log(`${b.fromUri}`);
        console.log(`  ${b.role} in "${b.threadTitle ?? b.threadId}" · ${b.createdAt}`);
        console.log(`  ${b.excerpt}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'outlinks') {
      const uri = positional[0];
      if (!uri) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref outlinks <alma://thread/<id>/message/<slot>>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const {
        outlinks
      } = await api('GET', `/api/refs/outlinks?uri=${encodeURIComponent(uri)}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(outlinks);
      if (!outlinks.length) {
        console.log('This message mentions nothing.');
        return;
      }
      for (const o of outlinks) console.log(`${o.uri}  [${o.kind}]${o.label ? `  @${o.label}` : ''}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'related') {
      const uri = positional[0];
      if (!uri) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref related <alma://kind/id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const {
        related
      } = await api('GET', `/api/refs/related?uri=${encodeURIComponent(uri)}${ctxQuery()}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(related);
      if (!related.length) {
        console.log('No manual/body relations yet. Create one with: alma ref link <from> <to>');
        return;
      }
      for (const r of related) {
        const arrow = r.direction === 'out' ? '→' : '←';
        console.log(`${arrow} ${r.uri}  [${r.kind}] ${r.title}${r.exists ? '' : ' (missing)'}  (${r.source})`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'link') {
      const fromUri = positional[0];
      const toUri = positional[1];
      if (!fromUri || !toUri) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref link <fromUri> <toUri> [label]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const label = positional.slice(2).join(' ') || undefined;
      await api('POST', '/api/refs/link', {
        fromUri,
        toUri,
        label
      });
      console.log(`Linked ${fromUri} → ${toUri}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'unlink') {
      const fromUri = positional[0];
      const toUri = positional[1];
      if (!fromUri || !toUri) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref unlink <fromUri> <toUri>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const {
        ok
      } = await api('POST', '/api/refs/unlink', {
        fromUri,
        toUri
      });
      console.log(ok ? `Unlinked ${fromUri} → ${toUri}` : 'No such manual link.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'graph') {
      const uri = positional[0];
      if (!uri) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref graph <alma://kind/id> [--depth n]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const depth = flagValue('--depth');
      const graph = await api('GET', `/api/refs/graph?uri=${encodeURIComponent(uri)}${depth ? `&depth=${encodeURIComponent(depth)}` : ''}${ctxQuery()}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(graph);
      console.log(`nodes (${graph.nodes.length}):`);
      for (const n of graph.nodes) console.log(`  ${n.uri}  [${n.kind}] ${n.title}${n.exists ? '' : ' (missing)'}`);
      console.log(`edges (${graph.edges.length}):`);
      for (const e of graph.edges) console.log(`  ${e.from}  →  ${e.to}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'snippet' || subcmd === 'quote') {
      const targetThread = flagValue('--thread') || threadId;
      if (!targetThread) {
        console.error('Error: no thread — run inside an Alma thread or pass --thread <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let text = positional.join(' ');
      if (text === '-' || !text) {
        text = await new Promise(resolve => {
          let buf = '';
          process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');
          process.stdin.on('data', c => buf += c);
          process.stdin.on('end', () => resolve(buf));
        });
      }
      const created = await api('POST', '/api/refs/snippets', {
        threadId: targetThread,
        messageId: process.env.ALMA_MESSAGE_ID || null,
        text,
        title: flagValue('--title') || null
      });
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(created);
      console.log(created.uri);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'open') {
      const uri = positional[0];
      if (!uri) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref open <alma://kind/id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('POST', '/api/refs/open', {
        uri,
        threadId
      });
      console.log(`Opened ${uri}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'context') {
      const targetThread = flagValue('--thread') || threadId;
      if (!targetThread) {
        console.error('Error: no thread — run inside an Alma thread or pass --thread <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const r = await api('GET', `/api/refs/context?threadId=${encodeURIComponent(targetThread)}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(r);
      console.log(r.context || '(no references in this thread)');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'token') {
      const uri = positional[0];
      if (!uri) {
        console.error('Usage: alma ref token <alma://kind/id> [label]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let label = positional.slice(1).join(' ');
      if (!label) {
        const r = await api('GET', `/api/refs/resolve?uri=${encodeURIComponent(uri)}${ctxQuery()}`);
        label = r.title || uri;
      }
      const safe = label.replace(/[\[\]()]/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      console.log(`@[${safe}](${uri})`);
      return;
    }
    console.error(`Unknown ref subcommand: ${subcmd}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  if (cmd === 'agent' || cmd === 'agents') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    const wantsJson = args.includes('--json');
    const flagValue = name => {
      const idx = args.indexOf(name);
      return idx !== -1 ? args[idx + 1] : undefined;
    };
    const readContent = async value => {
      if (value === '-') {
        return await new Promise(resolve => {
          let buf = '';
          process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');
          process.stdin.on('data', c => buf += c);
          process.stdin.on('end', () => resolve(buf));
        });
      }
      const fs = await import('fs');
      return fs.readFileSync(value, 'utf-8');
    };
    // Mirrors normalizeAgentId in src/lib/managedAgents.ts — the ONE
    // canonical agent-id rule (the server re-normalizes on every settings
    // write; this local copy serves the duplicate pre-check and forgiving
    // delete). If you change one, change both. A CJK-keeping slug here
    // once echoed ids the Task resolver rejected (0.0.990 bug report).
    const stableIdHash = input => {
      let h = 5381;
      for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) h = (h << 5) + h + input.charCodeAt(i) >>> 0;
      return h.toString(36);
    };
    const slugify = name => {
      const trimmed = String(name || '').trim();
      const ascii = trimmed.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
      if (ascii) return ascii;
      if (trimmed) return `agent-${stableIdHash(trimmed)}`;
      return `agent-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;
    };
    const getAgents = async () => {
      const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
      const agents = settings.agents || {
        enabled: true,
