        allowSubagentDelegation: true,
        profiles: []
      };
      if (!Array.isArray(agents.profiles)) agents.profiles = [];
      return {
        settings,
        agents
      };
    };
    if (subcmd === 'list' || !subcmd) {
      const {
        agents
      } = await getAgents();
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(agents.profiles);
      if (!agents.profiles.length) {
        console.log('No managed agent profiles.');
        return;
      }
      for (const p of agents.profiles) {
        console.log(`${p.id.padEnd(20)} ${p.enabled ? '✓' : '✗'}  ${p.name}${p.builtIn ? '  (built-in)' : ''}${p.model ? `  [${p.model}]` : ''}`);
        if (p.summary) console.log(`${' '.repeat(24)}${p.summary}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'create') {
      const name = args[2];
      if (!name || name.startsWith('--')) {
        console.error('Usage: alma agent create <name> --prompt <file|-> [--summary <t>] [--focus a,b] [--model <provider:model>] [--execution-mode <lane>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const promptSrc = flagValue('--prompt');
      if (!promptSrc) {
        console.error('Error: --prompt <file|-> is required (the agent system prompt)');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const prompt = (await readContent(promptSrc)).trim();
      const {
        settings,
        agents
      } = await getAgents();
      const id = flagValue('--id') || slugify(name);
      if (agents.profiles.some(p => p.id === id)) {
        console.error(`Error: agent profile "${id}" already exists (pass --id to pick another id)`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const profile = {
        id,
        name,
        category: 'custom',
        executionMode: flagValue('--execution-mode') || 'general-purpose',
        enabled: true,
        builtIn: false,
        color: flagValue('--color') || '#8b8b8b',
        summary: flagValue('--summary') || '',
        focus: (flagValue('--focus') || '').split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean),
        delegatesTo: [],
        prompt
      };
      const model = flagValue('--model');
      if (model) profile.model = model;
      const beforeIds = new Set(agents.profiles.map(p => p.id));
      agents.profiles.push(profile);
      settings.agents = agents;
      // The server normalizes agent ids on write — echo the id it
      // actually STORED, never the locally computed one, so the printed
      // Reference/Task examples are guaranteed to resolve.
      const saved = await api('PUT', '/api/settings', settings);
      const savedProfiles = saved && saved.agents && Array.isArray(saved.agents.profiles) ? saved.agents.profiles : [];
      const created = savedProfiles.find(p => p.name === name && !beforeIds.has(p.id)) || savedProfiles.find(p => p.id === id) || {
        id
      };
      if (created.id !== id) {
        console.log(`Note: requested id "${id}" was normalized to "${created.id}"`);
      }
      console.log(`Created managed agent "${name}" (id: ${created.id})`);
      console.log(`Reference: alma://agent/${created.id}`);
      console.log(`Delegate:  Task(agent_id: "${created.id}", ...)`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'delete') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma agent delete <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const {
        settings,
        agents
      } = await getAgents();
      // Forgiving: also match the canonical form of an un-normalized id
      // copied from older CLI output.
      const profile = agents.profiles.find(p => p.id === id) || agents.profiles.find(p => p.id === slugify(id));
      if (!profile) {
        console.error(`Error: no agent profile "${id}"`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (profile.builtIn) {
        console.error('Error: built-in profiles cannot be deleted (disable them in settings instead)');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      agents.profiles = agents.profiles.filter(p => p.id !== profile.id);
      settings.agents = agents;
      await api('PUT', '/api/settings', settings);
      console.log(`Deleted agent profile "${id}"`);
      return;
    }
    console.error(`Unknown agent subcommand: ${subcmd}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  if (cmd === 'workspace') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'list' || !subcmd) {
      const workspaces = await api('GET', '/api/workspaces');
      if (Array.isArray(workspaces)) {
        if (workspaces.length === 0) {
          console.log('No workspaces.');
          return;
        }
        for (const w of workspaces) {
          console.log(`${w.id}  ${w.path || w.name || '(unknown)'}`);
        }
      } else {
        prettyPrint(workspaces);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'set') {
      const id = args[2];
      const wsPath = args[3];
      if (!id || !wsPath) {
        console.error('Usage: alma workspace set <id> <path>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('PUT', `/api/workspaces/${id}`, {
        path: wsPath
      });
      console.log(`✅ Workspace ${id} path set to: ${wsPath}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'pins' || subcmd === 'pin' || subcmd === 'unpin') {
      // Project-level scope: pins are the user's decision. A model-driven
      // shell (ALMA_TOOL_CALL_ID set) may READ pins but never change them —
      // the whole point is that a conversation cannot widen or drop the
      // project's boundary.
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error(`Usage: alma workspace ${subcmd} <workspace-id>${subcmd === 'pins' ? '' : ' <alma://kind/id> [label]'}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (subcmd === 'pins') {
        const r = await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${id}/pinned-references`);
        if (args.includes('--json')) return prettyPrint(r);
        if (!r.effective?.length) {
          console.log('No pinned references.');
          return;
        }
        for (const p of r.effective) console.log(`${p.uri}  ${p.label !== p.uri ? p.label : ''}`.trimEnd());
        return;
      }
      if (process.env.ALMA_TOOL_CALL_ID) {
        console.error('Refused: pinned references are set at the project level by the user (settings → Projects), not from inside a conversation.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const uri = args[3];
      if (!uri) {
        console.error(`Usage: alma workspace ${subcmd} <workspace-id> <alma://kind/id>${subcmd === 'pin' ? ' [label]' : ''}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const current = await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${id}/pinned-references`);
      const own = Array.isArray(current.own) ? current.own : [];
      let pins;
      if (subcmd === 'pin') {
        const label = args.slice(4).filter(a => !a.startsWith('--')).join(' ');
        pins = [...own.filter(p => p.uri !== uri), {
          uri,
          label: label || undefined
        }];
      } else {
        pins = own.filter(p => p.uri !== uri);
        if (pins.length === own.length) {
          console.error(`Not pinned: ${uri}`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
      }
      const r = await api('PUT', `/api/workspaces/${id}/pinned-references`, {
        pins
      });
      console.log(`✅ ${subcmd === 'pin' ? 'Pinned' : 'Unpinned'} ${uri} (${r.own.length} pin${r.own.length === 1 ? '' : 's'})`);
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma workspace <list|set|pins|pin|unpin>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Email Channel (原料仓) ───────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'mail' || cmd === 'email') {
    const subcmd = args[1] || 'list';
    const wantsJson = args.includes('--json');
    const flagValue = name => {
      const idx = args.indexOf(name);
      return idx !== -1 ? args[idx + 1] : undefined;
    };
    if (subcmd === 'accounts') {
      const accounts = await api('GET', '/api/email/accounts');
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(accounts);
      if (!accounts.length) {
        console.log('No email accounts. Connect one in Settings → Channels → Email.');
        return;
      }
      for (const a of accounts) console.log(`${a.id}  ${a.name}  ${a.email}  [${a.status}]  pending:${a.pendingCount}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'list') {
      const qs = new URLSearchParams();
      const status = flagValue('--status');
      if (status) qs.set('status', status);
      const account = flagValue('--account');
      if (account) qs.set('accountId', account);
      qs.set('limit', flagValue('--limit') || '30');
      const items = await api('GET', `/api/email/inbox?${qs.toString()}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(items);
      if (!items.length) {
        console.log('Inbox empty.');
        return;
      }
      for (const m of items) {
        console.log(`${m.id}  [${m.status}${m.hasDraft ? '+draft' : ''}]  ${m.from || '?'}  ${m.subject || '(no subject)'}${m.attachmentCount ? `  📎${m.attachmentCount}` : ''}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'show') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma mail show <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const m = await api('GET', `/api/email/inbox/${id}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(m);
      console.log(`Subject: ${m.subject || '(no subject)'}`);
      console.log(`From:    ${m.fromAddr || '?'}`);
      console.log(`To:      ${(m.toAddrs || []).join(', ')}`);
      if (m.ccAddrs?.length) console.log(`Cc:      ${m.ccAddrs.join(', ')}`);
      console.log(`Date:    ${m.date || '?'}`);
      console.log(`Status:  ${m.status}${m.sentAt ? ` (sent ${m.sentAt})` : ''}`);
      if (m.attachments?.length) {
        console.log('Attachments:');
        for (const a of m.attachments) console.log(`  ${a.filename}  ${a.path}  (${a.size} bytes)`);
      }
      if (m.draft) {
        console.log(`Draft:   to ${m.draft.to.join(', ')} — "${m.draft.subject}" (awaiting user confirmation)`);
      }
      console.log('---');
      console.log(m.bodyText || '(empty body)');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'draft') {
      const id = args[2];
      const to = flagValue('--to');
      const subject = flagValue('--subject');
      if (!id || !to || !subject) {
        console.error('Usage: alma mail draft <id> --to <a,b> --subject <s> [--cc <c>] (--body <text> | --body-file <path|->)');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let body = flagValue('--body');
      const bodyFile = flagValue('--body-file');
      if (!body && bodyFile) {
        const fs = await import('fs');
        body = bodyFile === '-' ? fs.readFileSync(0, 'utf8') : fs.readFileSync(bodyFile, 'utf8');
      }
      if (!body || !body.trim()) {
        console.error('Draft body is empty (use --body or --body-file).');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const r = await api('PUT', `/api/email/inbox/${id}/draft`, {
        to,
        cc: flagValue('--cc') || [],
        subject,
        body
      });
      console.log(`✅ Draft saved on ${r.id} → to ${r.draft.to.join(', ')}.`);
      console.log('   The user confirms sending in Settings → Channels → Email inbox (or: alma mail send).');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'dismiss' || subcmd === 'done' || subcmd === 'reopen') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error(`Usage: alma mail ${subcmd} <id>`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const status = subcmd === 'dismiss' ? 'dismissed' : subcmd === 'done' ? 'done' : 'pending';
      await api('PUT', `/api/email/inbox/${id}/status`, {
        status
      });
      console.log(`✅ ${id} → ${status}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'send') {
      // Outward side effect: sending mail is the USER's call. A model
      // shell (ALMA_TOOL_CALL_ID) may draft, never send.
      if (process.env.ALMA_TOOL_CALL_ID) {
        console.error('Refused: sending email requires the user to confirm — the draft is saved; ask the user to review and send it (Settings → Channels → Email, or `alma mail send <id>` in their own terminal).');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma mail send <id>   (sends the stored draft)');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const r = await api('POST', `/api/email/inbox/${id}/send`);
      console.log(`✅ Sent (${r.messageId}).`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'check') {
      const accountId = args[2];
      if (!accountId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma mail check <account-id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const r = await api('POST', `/api/email/accounts/${accountId}/check`);
      console.log(`✅ Checked — ${r.ingested} new item(s).`);
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma mail <accounts|list|show|draft|dismiss|done|reopen|send|check> [--json]');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── 团队主机 (Team host / member) ─────────────
  if (cmd === 'team') {
    const subcmd = args[1] || 'status';
    const wantsJson = args.includes('--json');
    const flagValue = name => {
      const idx = args.indexOf(name);
      return idx !== -1 ? args[idx + 1] : undefined;
    };
    // Outward side effects (inviting, joining, revoking, posting to a
    // shared channel) are the USER's call. A model shell (ALMA_TOOL_CALL_ID)
    // may read the team freely but never change membership or post —
    // same gate as `alma mail send`.
    const userOnly = what => {
      if (process.env.ALMA_TOOL_CALL_ID) {
        console.error(`Refused: ${what} requires the user — ask them to do it from the Team page (or \`alma team ${subcmd}\` in their own terminal).`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    };
    const textOf = m => (m.parts || []).filter(p => p && p.type === 'text' && typeof p.text === 'string').map(p => p.text).join('\n').trim();
    if (subcmd === 'status') {
      const st = await api('GET', '/api/team/status');
      if (wantsJson) return console.log(JSON.stringify(st, null, 2));
      if (st.mode === 'host') {
        console.log(`hosting "${st.host.name}" on port ${st.host.port} — ${st.host.members} member(s), ${st.host.connectedMembers.length} online, ${st.host.channels} channel(s)`);
        const relay = st.host.relay || {};
        if (relay.url) console.log(`  relay: ${relay.url}${relay.accountEmail ? ` (${relay.accountEmail})` : ''}`);else if (!relay.enabled) console.log('  relay: off (alma team relay on)');else if (!relay.signedIn) console.log('  relay: not signed in — members outside your LAN cannot join (alma team relay sign-in)');else console.log(`  relay: ${relay.state}${relay.lastError ? ` (${relay.lastError})` : ''}`);
        if (st.host.lanAddresses.length) console.log(`  LAN: ${st.host.lanAddresses.map(ip => `http://${ip}:${st.host.port}`).join(', ')}`);
      } else if (st.mode === 'member') {
        console.log(`member "${st.client.name}" of "${st.client.teamName}" at ${st.client.hostUrl} — ${st.client.state}${st.client.lastError ? ` (${st.client.lastError})` : ''}`);
      } else {
        console.log('not in a team (alma team host | alma team join <invite-link>)');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'host') {
      userOnly('hosting a team');
      const port = flagValue('--port');
      const name = flagValue('--name');
      const body = {};
      if (port) body.port = Number(port);
      if (name) body.name = name;
      const h = await api('POST', '/api/team/host/enable', body);
      console.log(`✅ hosting on port ${h.port}${h.lanAddresses.length ? ` — ${h.lanAddresses.map(ip => `http://${ip}:${h.port}`).join(', ')}` : ''}`);
      if (h.relay && h.relay.enabled && !h.relay.signedIn) console.log('   relay not signed in: run `alma team relay sign-in` so people outside your LAN can join');
      return;
    }

    // relay on|off|sign-in — publish the host through relay.alma.now (no port forwarding)
    if (subcmd === 'relay') {
      userOnly('changing the team relay');
      const what = args[2];
      if (what === 'on' || what === 'off') {
        const h = await api('PUT', '/api/team/host/relay', {
          enabled: what === 'on'
        });
        console.log(`✅ relay ${h.relay.enabled ? 'on' : 'off'}${h.relay.url ? ` — ${h.relay.url}` : h.relay.enabled && !h.relay.signedIn ? ' (sign in first: alma team relay sign-in)' : ''}`);
        return;
      }
      if (what === 'sign-in') {
        const r = await api('POST', '/api/team/host/relay/sign-in');
        console.log('Open this in your browser to sign in (Alma community account):');
        console.log(r.url);
        return;
      }
      console.error('Usage: alma team relay <on|off|sign-in>');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    if (subcmd === 'unhost') {
      userOnly('stopping the team host');
      await api('POST', '/api/team/host/disable');
      console.log('✅ host stopped');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'invite') {
      userOnly('inviting a member');
      const r = await api('POST', '/api/team/host/invites', {
        name: flagValue('--name')
      });
      if (wantsJson) return console.log(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2));
      console.log(`code: ${r.code}   (single use, expires ${r.expiresAt})`);
      for (const u of r.hostUrls) console.log(`host: ${u}`);
      if (r.link) console.log(`invite link: ${r.link}`);
      console.log(`join with: alma team join ${r.link || `${r.hostUrls[0] || '<hostUrl>'} ${r.code}`} --name <you>`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'members') {
      const r = await api('GET', '/api/team/members');
      if (wantsJson) return console.log(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2));
      console.log(`${r.organizer.id}  ${r.organizer.name}  organizer`);
      for (const m of r.members) console.log(`${m.id}  ${m.name}  ${m.status === 'revoked' ? 'revoked' : m.online ? 'online' : 'offline'}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'revoke') {
      userOnly('revoking a member');
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma team revoke <memberId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const r = await api('POST', `/api/team/host/members/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/revoke`);
      console.log(`✅ ${r.member.name} (${r.member.id}) revoked`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'channels') {
      const r = await api('GET', '/api/team/channels');
      if (wantsJson) return console.log(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2));
      for (const c of r.channels) console.log(`${c.threadId}  ${c.kind === 'thread' ? '' : '#'}${c.name}  ${c.messageCount} msg${c.kind === 'thread' ? '  (shared thread)' : ''}${c.generating ? '  (generating)' : ''}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'policy') {
      // `alma team policy` reads (anyone); `alma team policy set …` is the organizer's.
      const printPolicy = policy => {
        if (wantsJson) return console.log(JSON.stringify({
          policy
        }, null, 2));
        const rate = [policy.rate.perMember === null ? null : `${policy.rate.perMember}/member/hour`, policy.rate.perChannel === null ? null : `${policy.rate.perChannel}/channel/hour`].filter(Boolean);
        console.log(`mode    ${policy.mode}`);
        console.log(`topics  ${policy.topics.length ? policy.topics.join(', ') : '(none)'}`);
        console.log(`rate    ${rate.length ? rate.join(', ') : 'unlimited'}`);
      };
      if (args[2] !== 'set') {
        const r = await api('GET', '/api/team/policy');
        return printPolicy(r.policy);
      }
      userOnly("changing the team Alma's policy");
      const body = {};
      if (flagValue('--mode') !== undefined) body.mode = flagValue('--mode');
      if (flagValue('--topics') !== undefined) body.topics = flagValue('--topics').split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      const rate = {};
      if (flagValue('--rate-member') !== undefined) rate.perMember = flagValue('--rate-member') === 'off' ? null : Number(flagValue('--rate-member'));
      if (flagValue('--rate-channel') !== undefined) rate.perChannel = flagValue('--rate-channel') === 'off' ? null : Number(flagValue('--rate-channel'));
      if (Object.keys(rate).length) body.rate = rate;
      if (!Object.keys(body).length) {
        console.error('Usage: alma team policy set [--mode silent|on_mention|topics] [--topics a,b,alma://…] [--rate-member N|off] [--rate-channel N|off]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const r = await api('PUT', '/api/team/policy', body);
      return printPolicy(r.policy);
    }
    if (subcmd === 'channel') {
      if (args[2] !== 'create' || !args[3]) {
        console.error('Usage: alma team channel create <name> [--workspace <id>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      userOnly('creating a channel');
      const r = await api('POST', '/api/team/host/channels', {
        name: args[3],
        workspaceId: flagValue('--workspace')
      });
      console.log(`✅ #${r.channel.name}  ${r.channel.threadId}`);
      return;
    }

    // Share one of my own threads with the team (history included); the
    // thread stays in my sidebar. `unshare` takes it back.
    if (subcmd === 'share' || subcmd === 'unshare') {
      if (!args[2]) {
        console.error(`Usage: alma team ${subcmd} <threadId>`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      userOnly(subcmd === 'share' ? 'sharing a thread with the team' : 'unsharing a thread');
      if (subcmd === 'share') {
        const r = await api('POST', '/api/team/host/channels/share', {
          threadId: args[2]
        });
        console.log(`✅ shared: ${r.channel.name}  ${r.channel.threadId}`);
      } else {
        await api('DELETE', `/api/team/host/channels/${encodeURIComponent(args[2])}`);
        console.log('✅ no longer shared');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'join') {
      userOnly('joining a team');
      const hostUrl = args[2];
      const code = args[3] && !args[3].startsWith('--') ? args[3] : undefined;
      if (!hostUrl || !code && !hostUrl.includes('#')) {
        console.error('Usage: alma team join <invite-link> [--name <you>]   or   alma team join <hostUrl> <code> [--name <you>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const r = await api('POST', '/api/team/join', code ? {
        hostUrl,
        code,
        name: flagValue('--name')
      } : {
        invite: hostUrl,
        name: flagValue('--name')
      });
      console.log(`✅ joined "${r.teamName}" as ${r.name} (${r.memberId}) — ${r.state}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'leave') {
      userOnly('leaving the team');
      await api('POST', '/api/team/leave');
      console.log('✅ left');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'messages') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma team messages <threadId> [-n 50]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const r = await api('GET', `/api/team/channels/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/messages`);
      if (wantsJson) return console.log(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2));
      const n = Number(flagValue('-n') || flagValue('--limit') || 50);
      for (const m of r.messages.slice(-n)) {
        const who = m.role === 'assistant' ? 'Alma' : m.metadata && m.metadata.teamAuthor && m.metadata.teamAuthor.name || m.role;
        const slot = m.metadata && m.metadata.slotId || m.id;
        console.log(`[${m.timestamp}] ${who} (${slot})${m.metadata && m.metadata.replyToSlotId ? ` ↩ ${m.metadata.replyToSlotId}` : ''}`);
        if (m.metadata && m.metadata.quote) console.log(String(m.metadata.quote).replace(/^/gm, '  > '));
        const t = textOf(m);
        if (t) console.log(t.replace(/^/gm, '  '));
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'submit') {
      const isDraft = args.includes('--draft');
      const id = args[2];
      const text = args.slice(3).filter((a, i, arr) => !['--reply', '--quote', '--ask', '--draft'].includes(a) && !['--reply', '--quote'].includes(arr[i - 1])).join(' ');
      if (!id || !text) {
        console.error('Usage: alma team submit <threadId> <text…> [--reply <slotId> [--quote <passage>]] [--ask] [--draft]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (isDraft) {
        // The only write a model shell may make: stage the text in the
        // person's Team page composer. They post it — or not.
        const r = await api('POST', '/api/team/drafts', {
          threadId: id,
          text,
          replyToSlotId: flagValue('--reply') || null
        });
        if (wantsJson) return console.log(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2));
        console.log(`📝 drafted for the Team page composer (${r.draft.text.length} chars) — the person decides whether to post it`);
        return;
      }
      if (process.env.ALMA_TOOL_CALL_ID) {
        console.error('Refused: posting to a team channel requires the user. Use `alma team submit <threadId> <text…> --draft` to put it in their composer instead.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const r = await api('POST', `/api/team/channels/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/submit`, {
        text,
        replyToSlotId: flagValue('--reply') || null,
        quote: flagValue('--quote') || null,
        askTeamAlma: args.includes('--ask')
      });
      const why = {
        silent: "team Alma is silent for members (organizer's policy)",
        rate_member: 'your hourly limit for team Alma is reached',
        rate_channel: "this channel's hourly limit for team Alma is reached"
      }[r.reason];
      console.log(r.asked ? r.steered ? '✅ posted, folded into the running answer' : '✅ posted, team Alma is answering' : why ? `✅ posted — ${why}` : '✅ posted');
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma team <status|host|unhost|relay|invite|members|revoke|channels|channel create|share|unshare|policy|join|leave|messages|submit> [--json]');
    console.error('  status                              host / member / none');
    console.error('  host [--port 23111] [--name me]     start hosting from this Alma');
    console.error('  relay <on|off|sign-in>              publish the host through relay.alma.now (no port forwarding)');
    console.error('  invite [--name who]                 one-shot invite link (relay URL first, then LAN)');
    console.error('  members | revoke <id>               who is in, kick someone out');
    console.error('  channels | channel create <name>    team channels');
    console.error('  share <threadId> | unshare <threadId>   share one of my own threads with the team (stays in my sidebar)');
    console.error("  policy [set --mode … --topics … --rate-member N --rate-channel N]   when the team's Alma answers (organizer sets)");
    console.error('  join <invite-link> [--name me]      become a member (or: join <hostUrl> <code>)');
    console.error('  messages <threadId> [-n 50]         read a channel');
    console.error('  submit <threadId> <text> [--reply <slotId> [--quote <passage>]] [--ask]   post (@alma or --ask to get an answer)');
    console.error('  submit … --draft                    stage it in the Team page composer for the person to post');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── 倒后镜 (Rearview system-waypoint ledger) ─────────────
  if (cmd === 'rearview' || cmd === 'rv' || cmd === '倒后镜') {
    const subcmd = args[1] || 'show';
    const wantsJson = args.includes('--json');
    const flagValue = name => {
      const idx = args.indexOf(name);
      return idx !== -1 ? args[idx + 1] : undefined;
    };
    const fmt = e => `${e.t}  ${e.i}  ${e.c} ${e.m}`;
    if (subcmd === 'show' || subcmd === 'list') {
      const limit = Number(flagValue('--limit') || flagValue('-n') || 20);
      const {
        entries
      } = await api('GET', `/api/rearview/entries?limit=${limit}`);
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(entries);
      if (!entries.length) {
        console.log('倒后镜为空 — 足迹会随每次运行自动累积。');
        return;
      }
      for (const e of entries) console.log(fmt(e));
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'last') {
      const {
        entries
      } = await api('GET', '/api/rearview/entries?limit=1');
      if (!entries.length) {
        console.log('(empty)');
        return;
      }
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(entries[0]);
      console.log(fmt(entries[0]));
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'stats') {
      const st = await api('GET', '/api/rearview/stats');
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(st);
      console.log(`足迹总数: ${st.total}  平均链长: ${st.avgChainLength}`);
      console.log(`回执分布: ✓ ${st.byMark['✓'] || 0}   ! ${st.byMark['!'] || 0}   ? ${st.byMark['?'] || 0}`);
      const sorted = Object.entries(st.byWaypoint).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
      console.log('地点热度:');
      for (const [w, n] of sorted) console.log(`  ${String(n).padStart(6)}  ${w}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'filter') {
      const only = flagValue('--only') || '?!';
      const marks = [...only].filter(c => '✓!?'.includes(c));
      const {
        entries
      } = await api('GET', '/api/rearview/entries?limit=100000');
      const hit = entries.filter(e => marks.includes(e.m)).reverse();
      const out = flagValue('--out');
      const text = hit.map(fmt).join('\n') + '\n';
      if (out) {
        const fs = await import('fs');
        fs.writeFileSync(out, text);
        console.log(`✅ ${hit.length} 条足迹已另存到 ${out}（仅公开列，可直接检查）`);
      } else {
        process.stdout.write(text);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'diff') {
      const a = args[2];
      const b = args[3];
      if (!a || !b) {
        console.error('Usage: alma rearview diff <idA> <idB>   (find last common waypoint + first divergence)');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const ea = await api('GET', `/api/rearview/entry/${a}`);
      const eb = await api('GET', `/api/rearview/entry/${b}`);
      const ca = ea.c.split('>');
      const cb = eb.c.split('>');
      let i = 0;
      while (i < ca.length && i < cb.length && ca[i] === cb[i]) i++;
      console.log(`A: ${ea.c} ${ea.m}`);
      console.log(`B: ${eb.c} ${eb.m}`);
      if (i === 0) console.log('无共同前缀。');else console.log(`最后共同地点: ${ca[i - 1]}`);
      console.log(`第一个分叉点: A→${ca[i] ?? '(结束)'}  B→${cb[i] ?? '(结束)'}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'freeze') {
      const only = flagValue('--only');
      const body = only ? {
        onlyMarks: [...only].filter(c => '✓!?'.includes(c))
      } : {};
      const r = await api('POST', '/api/rearview/freeze', body);
      console.log(`✅ 快照已冻结: ${r.path} (${r.lines} 行，仅公开列)`);
      console.log('   这份文件就是你可以检查、也可以选择分享的全部内容 — Alma 不会替你上传它。');
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma rearview <show|last|stats|filter|diff|freeze> [--json]');
    console.error('  show [-n 20]           最近足迹');
    console.error('  last                   最近一条（可直接复制进 Bug 反馈，无任何内容信息）');
    console.error('  stats                  回执分布 / 地点热度 / 平均链长');
    console.error("  filter --only '?!' [--out f]   只留异常足迹，另存待检查");
    console.error('  diff <idA> <idB>       最后共同地点 + 第一个分叉点');
    console.error("  freeze [--only '?!']   冻结不可变纯文本快照（仅公开列）");
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Themes (base46 color schemes) ───────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'theme' || cmd === 'themes') {
    const subcmd = args[1] || 'list';
    const wantsJson = args.includes('--json');
    const wantsLight = args.includes('--light');
    if (subcmd === 'list') {
      const r = await api('GET', '/api/themes');
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(r);
      for (const t of r.themes) console.log(`${t.id}  (${t.type})`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'current') {
      const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
      const tc = settings.themeConfig || {};
      console.log(`mode:  ${settings.general?.theme || 'system'}`);
      console.log(`dark:  ${tc.darkTheme || '(built-in default)'}`);
      console.log(`light: ${tc.lightTheme || '(built-in default)'}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'set' || subcmd === 'random') {
      const {
        themes
      } = await api('GET', '/api/themes');
      let id;
      if (subcmd === 'set') {
        id = args[2];
        if (!id || id.startsWith('--')) {
          console.error('Usage: alma theme set <theme-id> [--light]   (see: alma theme list)');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        if (!themes.some(t => t.id === id)) {
          console.error(`Unknown theme: ${id}. See: alma theme list`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
      } else {
        const pool = themes.filter(t => t.type === (wantsLight ? 'light' : 'dark'));
        id = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)].id;
      }
      const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
      settings.themeConfig = settings.themeConfig || {};
      if (wantsLight) settings.themeConfig.lightTheme = id;else settings.themeConfig.darkTheme = id;
      await api('PUT', '/api/settings', settings);
      console.log(`✅ ${wantsLight ? 'Light' : 'Dark'} theme set to: ${id}`);
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma theme <list|current|set <id>|random> [--light] [--json]');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Remote Hosts (SSH) ──────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'host' || cmd === 'hosts') {
    const subcmd = args[1] || 'list';
    const wantsJson = args.includes('--json');
    if (subcmd === 'list') {
      const hosts = await api('GET', '/api/remote-hosts');
      if (wantsJson) return prettyPrint(hosts);
      if (!Array.isArray(hosts) || hosts.length === 0) {
        console.log('No remote hosts.');
        return;
      }
      for (const h of hosts) console.log(`${h.id}  ${h.name}  ${h.sshTarget}  [${h.status?.state || 'unknown'}]`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'test') {
      const target = args[2];
      if (!target) {
        console.error('Usage: alma host test <id|name|all>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const hosts = await api('GET', '/api/remote-hosts');
      const list = Array.isArray(hosts) ? hosts : [];
      const picked = target === 'all' ? list : list.filter(h => h.id === target || h.name === target || h.sshTarget === target);
      if (picked.length === 0) {
        console.error(`No matching host: ${target}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let failed = 0;
      for (const h of picked) {
        try {
          const r = await api('POST', `/api/remote-hosts/${h.id}/test`);
          if (r.ok) {
            console.log(`✅ ${h.name} (${h.sshTarget})${typeof r.latencyMs === 'number' ? ` ${r.latencyMs}ms` : ''}`);
          } else {
            failed++;
            console.log(`❌ ${h.name} (${h.sshTarget}) — ${r.error || 'failed'}`);
          }
        } catch (err) {
          failed++;
          console.log(`❌ ${h.name} (${h.sshTarget}) — ${err.message}`);
        }
      }
      if (failed > 0) process.exit(1);
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma host <list|test <id|name|all>> [--json]');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Cron Jobs ────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'cron') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'list' || !subcmd) {
      const jobs = await api('GET', '/api/cron/jobs');
      if (Array.isArray(jobs) && jobs.length > 0) {
        for (const j of jobs) {
          const status = j.enabled ? '✅' : '⏸️';
          const lastRun = j.lastRunAt ? formatDate(j.lastRunAt) : 'never';
          const timezoneSuffix = j.scheduleType === 'cron' && j.payload?.timezone ? `  tz:${j.payload.timezone}` : '';
          console.log(`${status} ${j.id.slice(0, 8)}  ${j.name}  [${j.scheduleType}:${j.schedule}]${timezoneSuffix}  mode:${j.executionMode}  runs:${j.runCount}  last:${lastRun}`);
        }
      } else {
        console.log('No cron jobs. Use "alma cron add" to create one.');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'add') {
      // alma cron add <name> <type> <schedule> [--mode main|isolated] [--prompt "..."] [--thread-id ID] [--deliver-to CHAT_ID] [--model MODEL] [--timezone IANA_TZ]
      const name = args[2];
      const scheduleType = args[3]; // at, every, cron
      const schedule = args[4];
      if (!name || !scheduleType || !schedule) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cron add <name> <at|every|cron> <schedule> [--mode main|isolated] [--prompt "..."] [--thread-id ID] [--deliver-to CHAT_ID] [--model MODEL] [--timezone IANA_TZ]');
        console.error('Options:');
        console.error('  --mode main|isolated   main = run inside an existing Alma thread; isolated = fresh hidden thread per run (default)');
        console.error('  --thread-id ID         (main mode) the Alma thread to run in — ALMA_THREAD_ID of the current conversation');
        console.error('  --delivery-platform telegram|discord   external delivery channel (default: telegram)');
        console.error('  --deliver-to CHAT_ID   send to an explicitly authorized external target; Telegram chat/group id or Discord channel id,');
        console.error('                         i.e. ALMA_CHAT_ID when a Telegram message triggered this turn. NOT an Alma thread id.');
        console.error('Examples:');
        console.error('  alma cron add "morning-check" cron "0 9 * * *" --prompt "Check my emails"');
        console.error('  alma cron add "reminder" at "2024-12-25T09:00:00" --prompt "Merry Christmas!"');
        console.error('  alma cron add "status" every "2h" --mode main --thread-id abc123');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        name,
        scheduleType,
        schedule,
        executionMode: 'isolated',
        payload: {}
      };
      for (let i = 5; i < args.length; i++) {
        if (args[i] === '--mode' && args[i + 1]) body.executionMode = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--prompt' && args[i + 1]) body.payload.agentTurn = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--event' && args[i + 1]) body.payload.systemEvent = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--thread-id' && args[i + 1]) body.payload.threadId = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--deliver-to' && args[i + 1]) body.payload.deliverTo = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--delivery-platform') {
          const platform = args[++i];
          if (!['telegram', 'discord'].includes(platform)) {
            console.error('❌ --delivery-platform must be telegram or discord');
            process.exit(1);
          }
          body.payload.deliveryPlatform = platform;
        } else if (args[i] === '--model' && args[i + 1]) body.payload.model = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--timezone' && args[i + 1]) body.payload.timezone = args[++i];
      }
      if (body.payload.deliveryPlatform && !['telegram', 'discord'].includes(body.payload.deliveryPlatform)) {
        console.error('❌ --delivery-platform must be telegram or discord');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (body.payload.deliverTo) await validateDeliverTo(body.payload.deliverTo, body.payload.deliveryPlatform);
      // Remember which conversation asked for this, so the job can point
      // back to it (time clues). Not a dispatch target — that is --thread-id.
      if (process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID) body.payload.sourceThreadId = process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
      const job = await api('POST', '/api/cron/jobs', body);
      const timezoneSuffix = job.scheduleType === 'cron' && job.payload?.timezone ? ` tz:${job.payload.timezone}` : '';
      console.log(`✅ Job created: ${job.id} "${job.name}" [${job.scheduleType}:${job.schedule}]${timezoneSuffix}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'update' || subcmd === 'edit') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cron update <id> [--name "..."] [--prompt "..."] [--schedule "..."] [--deliver-to CHAT_ID] [--mode main|isolated] [--timezone IANA_TZ]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const jobs = await api('GET', '/api/cron/jobs');
      const match = resolveCronJob(jobs, id);
      const patch = {
        payload: {}
      };
      for (let i = 3; i < args.length; i++) {
        if (args[i] === '--name' && args[i + 1]) patch.name = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--prompt' && args[i + 1]) patch.payload.agentTurn = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--event' && args[i + 1]) patch.payload.systemEvent = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--schedule' && args[i + 1]) patch.schedule = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--deliver-to' && args[i + 1]) patch.payload.deliverTo = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--delivery-platform') {
          const platform = args[++i];
          if (!['telegram', 'discord'].includes(platform)) {
            console.error('❌ --delivery-platform must be telegram or discord');
            process.exit(1);
          }
          patch.payload.deliveryPlatform = platform;
        } else if (args[i] === '--mode' && args[i + 1]) patch.executionMode = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--model' && args[i + 1]) patch.payload.model = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--timezone' && args[i + 1]) patch.payload.timezone = args[++i];
      }
      if (patch.payload.deliveryPlatform && !['telegram', 'discord'].includes(patch.payload.deliveryPlatform)) {
        console.error('❌ --delivery-platform must be telegram or discord');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const deliveryTarget = patch.payload.deliverTo ?? match.payload?.deliverTo;
      const deliveryPlatform = patch.payload.deliveryPlatform ?? match.payload?.deliveryPlatform;
      if (deliveryTarget && (patch.payload.deliverTo !== undefined || patch.payload.deliveryPlatform !== undefined)) await validateDeliverTo(deliveryTarget, deliveryPlatform);
      if (Object.keys(patch.payload).length === 0) delete patch.payload;
      const updated = await api('PUT', `/api/cron/jobs/${match.id}`, patch);
      const timezoneSuffix = updated.scheduleType === 'cron' && updated.payload?.timezone ? ` tz:${updated.payload.timezone}` : '';
      console.log(`✅ Job updated: ${updated.id.slice(0, 8)} "${updated.name}"${timezoneSuffix}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'remove' || subcmd === 'delete') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cron remove <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      // Support partial ID matching
      const jobs = await api('GET', '/api/cron/jobs');
      const match = resolveCronJob(jobs, id);
      await api('DELETE', `/api/cron/jobs/${match.id}`);
      console.log(`✅ Removed job: ${match.name}`);
      console.log(`   Run history kept — alma cron history ${match.id}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'run') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cron run <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const jobs = await api('GET', '/api/cron/jobs');
      const match = resolveCronJob(jobs, id);
      await api('POST', `/api/cron/jobs/${match.id}/run`);
      console.log(`✅ Job triggered: ${match.name}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'enable' || subcmd === 'disable') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error(`Usage: alma cron ${subcmd} <id>`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const jobs = await api('GET', '/api/cron/jobs');
      const match = resolveCronJob(jobs, id);
      await api('POST', `/api/cron/jobs/${match.id}/toggle`, {
        enabled: subcmd === 'enable'
      });
      console.log(`✅ Job ${subcmd}d: ${match.name}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'history') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cron history <id>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const jobs = await api('GET', '/api/cron/jobs');
      // History survives `cron remove`: a full id that no longer matches
      // a job still resolves its runs (prefix matching needs the job).
      const existing = jobs.find(j => j.id === id) || jobs.filter(j => j.id.startsWith(id));
      const jobId = Array.isArray(existing) ? existing.length === 1 ? existing[0].id : existing.length > 1 ? resolveCronJob(jobs, id).id : id : existing.id;
      const runs = await api('GET', `/api/cron/jobs/${jobId}/runs?limit=10`);
      if (Array.isArray(runs) && runs.length > 0) {
        const fmtDuration = ms => ms < 1000 ? `${ms}ms` : ms < 60_000 ? `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(1)}s` : `${Math.round(ms / 60_000)}m`;
        for (const r of runs) {
          if (!r.completedAt) {
            const elapsed = Date.now() - new Date(r.startedAt).getTime();
            console.log(`⏳ ${formatDate(r.startedAt)}  running (${fmtDuration(elapsed)} so far)`);
            continue;
          }
          const status = r.error ? '❌' : '✅';
          const dur = fmtDuration(new Date(r.completedAt).getTime() - new Date(r.startedAt).getTime());
          console.log(`${status} ${formatDate(r.startedAt)}  ${dur.padStart(6)}  ${truncate(r.error || r.result || '', 60)}`);
        }
      } else if (!jobs.some(j => j.id === jobId)) {
        console.log(`No run history for ${id} (no such job; a removed job's history needs its full id).`);
      } else {
        console.log('No run history.');
      }
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma cron <list|add|update|remove|run|enable|disable|history>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Secrets ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'secret' || cmd === 'secrets') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'list' || !subcmd) {
      const {
        secrets
      } = await api('GET', '/api/secrets');
      if (!Array.isArray(secrets) || secrets.length === 0) {
        console.log('No secrets. Add one with: alma secret set <NAME>  (value is read from stdin)');
        return;
      }
      for (const sec of secrets) {
        const envTag = sec.env ? 'env:on ' : 'env:off';
        const encTag = sec.encrypted ? '' : '  ⚠️ stored WITHOUT OS encryption';
        console.log(`${sec.name}  ${envTag} ${sec.note ? `— ${sec.note}` : ''}${encTag}`);
      }
      console.log('');
      console.log('Values are write-only. Secrets with env:on are injected as environment variables into agent shells; output is redacted.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'set') {
      const name = args[2];
      if (!name) {
        console.error('Usage: alma secret set <NAME> [--note "..."] [--no-env]');
        console.error('The VALUE is read from stdin — pipe it or paste + Ctrl-D. Never pass secrets as arguments (argv leaks into process lists and logs).');
        console.error('  printf %s "$TOKEN" | alma secret set GH_TOKEN --note "GitHub PAT for gh CLI"');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      let note;
      let env = true;
      for (let i = 3; i < args.length; i++) {
        if (args[i] === '--note' && args[i + 1]) note = args[++i];else if (args[i] === '--no-env') env = false;
      }
      if (process.stdin.isTTY) {
        console.error(`Paste the value for ${name}, then press Enter and Ctrl-D:`);
      }
      const value = (await new Promise(resolve => {
        let buf = '';
        process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');
        process.stdin.on('data', c => buf += c);
        process.stdin.on('end', () => resolve(buf));
      })).replace(/\r?\n$/, '');
      if (!value) {
        console.error('❌ Empty value — nothing stored.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const {
        secret
      } = await api('POST', '/api/secrets', {
        name,
        value,
        note,
        env
      });
      console.log(`✅ Secret ${secret.name} stored${secret.encrypted ? ' (encrypted)' : ' ⚠️ WITHOUT OS encryption'}${secret.env ? ', will be injected into agent shells as $' + secret.name : ' (env injection off)'}.`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'env') {
      const name = args[2];
      const state = args[3];
      if (!name || !['on', 'off'].includes(state)) {
        console.error('Usage: alma secret env <NAME> on|off');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const {
        secret
      } = await api('POST', '/api/secrets', {
        name,
        env: state === 'on'
      });
      console.log(`✅ ${secret.name}: env injection ${secret.env ? 'on' : 'off'}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'rm' || subcmd === 'remove' || subcmd === 'delete') {
      const name = args[2];
      if (!name) {
        console.error('Usage: alma secret rm <NAME>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('DELETE', `/api/secrets/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`);
      console.log(`✅ Removed secret ${name}`);
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma secret <list|set|env|rm>');
    console.error('Secrets are write-only: there is no `get`. Enabled secrets are injected as env vars into agent shells, and their values are redacted from all shell output.');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Activity Recorder ───────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'activity') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'status' || !subcmd) {
      const s = await api('GET', '/api/activity-recorder/status');
      prettyPrint(s);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'start') {
      const s = await api('POST', '/api/activity-recorder/start');
      prettyPrint(s);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'stop') {
      const s = await api('POST', '/api/activity-recorder/stop');
      prettyPrint(s);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'config') {
      const act = args[2];
      if (!act || act === 'get') {
        const cfg = await api('GET', '/api/activity-recorder/config');
        prettyPrint(cfg);
        return;
      }
      if (act === 'set') {
        const p = args[3];
        const v = args[4];
        if (!p) {
          console.error('Usage: alma activity config set <key> <value>');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        const payload = {};
        payload[p] = parseValue(v);
        const cfg = await api('PUT', '/api/activity-recorder/config', payload);
        prettyPrint(cfg);
        return;
      }
      console.error('Usage: alma activity config <get|set>');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    if (subcmd === 'sessions') {
      const limit = args[2] ? parseInt(args[2], 10) : 20;
      const out = await api('GET', `/api/activity-recorder/sessions?limit=${limit}`);
      const sessions = out && out.sessions || [];
      if (sessions.length === 0) {
        console.log('No sessions recorded yet.');
        return;
      }
      for (const s of sessions) {
        const mins = s.durationMs ? Math.round(s.durationMs / 60000) : '?';
        const title = s.analysisTitle || s.appNames?.join(', ') || 'Session';
        // Show full ID so users can copy-paste directly into `alma activity show`.
        console.log(`${s.id}  ${formatDate(s.startedAt)}  ${mins}m  ${title}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'show') {
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma activity show <sessionId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const detail = await api('GET', `/api/activity-recorder/sessions/${id}`);
      prettyPrint(detail);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'search') {
      const mode = args[2] === 'semantic' ? 'semantic' : 'keyword';
      const q = args[2] === 'semantic' ? args.slice(3).join(' ') : args.slice(2).join(' ');
      if (!q) {
        console.error('Usage: alma activity search [semantic] <query>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const out = await api('GET', `/api/activity-recorder/search/${mode}?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&limit=20`);
      const results = out && out.results || [];
      if (results.length === 0) {
        console.log('No matches.');
        return;
      }
      for (const r of results) {
        const scoreTxt = r.score != null ? `[${r.score.toFixed(3)}] ` : '';
        console.log(`${scoreTxt}${formatDate(r.createdAt)}  ${r.sessionId.slice(0, 12)}  ${truncate(r.text.replace(/\s+/g, ' '), 100)}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'summary') {
      const kind = args[2]; // daily or weekly
      const dateKey = args[3] || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const narrative = args.includes('--narrative');
      if (kind !== 'daily' && kind !== 'weekly') {
        console.error('Usage: alma activity summary <daily|weekly> [YYYY-MM-DD] [--narrative]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const path = narrative ? `/api/activity-recorder/summary/${kind}/${dateKey}?narrative=true` : `/api/activity-recorder/summary/${kind}/${dateKey}`;
      const s = await api('POST', path);
      prettyPrint(s);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'analyze' || subcmd === 'crystallize') {
      // 'crystallize' kept as an alias for anyone who scripted against the old name.
      const id = args[2];
      if (!id) {
        console.error('Usage: alma activity analyze <sessionId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const s = await api('POST', `/api/activity-recorder/sessions/${id}/analyze`);
      prettyPrint(s);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'digest') {
      // Rich markdown digest for on-demand use by the AI via Bash.
      let lookbackMin = 120;
      let maxAnalyzed = 8;
      for (let i = 2; i < args.length; i++) {
        if (args[i] === '--lookback' && args[i + 1]) lookbackMin = parseInt(args[++i], 10) || 120;else if ((args[i] === '--max-analyzed' || args[i] === '--max-crystals') && args[i + 1]) maxAnalyzed = parseInt(args[++i], 10) || 8;
      }
      const resp = await apiRaw('GET', `/api/activity-recorder/digest?lookbackMin=${lookbackMin}&maxAnalyzed=${maxAnalyzed}`);
      const md = await resp.text();
      process.stdout.write(md);
      if (!md.endsWith('\n')) process.stdout.write('\n');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'report') {
      // LittleBird-style thematic work journal for an entire day.
      // `alma activity report`            → today
      // `alma activity report yesterday`  → yesterday
      // `alma activity report 2026-04-17` → explicit date
      // Flags: --force (bypass cache), --skeleton (no LLM narrative), --json
      let dateKey = 'today';
      let force = false;
      let skeleton = false;
      let asJson = false;
      for (let i = 2; i < args.length; i++) {
        const a = args[i];
        if (a === '--force') force = true;else if (a === '--skeleton' || a === '--skeleton-only') skeleton = true;else if (a === '--json') asJson = true;else if (!a.startsWith('--')) dateKey = a;
      }
      const qs = [];
      if (force) qs.push('force=1');
      if (skeleton) qs.push('skeletonOnly=1');
      if (asJson) qs.push('format=json');
      const q = qs.length ? `?${qs.join('&')}` : '';
      const resp = await apiRaw('GET', `/api/activity-recorder/report/${encodeURIComponent(dateKey)}${q}`);
      const text = await resp.text();
      if (asJson) {
        try {
          prettyPrint(JSON.parse(text));
        } catch {
          process.stdout.write(text);
          if (!text.endsWith('\n')) process.stdout.write('\n');
        }
      } else {
        process.stdout.write(text);
        if (!text.endsWith('\n')) process.stdout.write('\n');
      }
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma activity <status|start|stop|config|sessions|show|search|summary|analyze|digest|report>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Heartbeat ───────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'heartbeat') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'status' || !subcmd) {
      const status = await api('GET', '/api/heartbeat/status');
      prettyPrint(status);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'config') {
      const config = await api('GET', '/api/heartbeat/config');
      prettyPrint(config);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'enable') {
      await api('PUT', '/api/heartbeat/config', {
        enabled: true
      });
      console.log('✅ Heartbeat enabled');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'disable') {
      await api('PUT', '/api/heartbeat/config', {
        enabled: false
      });
      console.log('✅ Heartbeat disabled');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'interval') {
      const minutes = parseInt(args[2], 10);
      if (!minutes || minutes < 1) {
        console.error('Usage: alma heartbeat interval <minutes>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('PUT', '/api/heartbeat/config', {
        intervalMinutes: minutes
      });
      console.log(`✅ Heartbeat interval set to ${minutes} minutes`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'patrol') {
      const action = args[2];
      if (action === 'enable') {
        await api('PUT', '/api/heartbeat/config', {
          groupPatrol: {
            enabled: true
          }
        });
        console.log('✅ Group patrol enabled');
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'disable') {
        await api('PUT', '/api/heartbeat/config', {
          groupPatrol: {
            enabled: false
          }
        });
        console.log('✅ Group patrol disabled');
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'config') {
        const config = await api('GET', '/api/heartbeat/config');
        prettyPrint(config.groupPatrol || {
          enabled: true,
          cooldownMinutes: 15,
          quietMinutes: 5,
          maxGroupsPerTick: 2
        });
        return;
      }
      console.error('Usage: alma heartbeat patrol <enable|disable|config>');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma heartbeat <status|config|enable|disable|interval|patrol>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Private Chat History ──────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'chat') {
    const fs = await import('fs');
    const pathMod = await import('path');
    const chatLogDir = pathMod.default.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'chats');
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'list') {
      if (!fs.existsSync(chatLogDir)) {
        console.log('No private chat logs.');
        return;
      }
      const files = fs.readdirSync(chatLogDir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.log')).sort().reverse();
      const chatIds = [...new Set(files.map(f => f.split('_')[0]))];
      for (const id of chatIds) {
        const latest = files.find(f => f.startsWith(id + '_'));
        const date = latest ? latest.replace(id + '_', '').replace('.log', '') : '';
        console.log(`${id}  (latest: ${date})`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'history') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const limit = parseInt(args[3]) || 50;
      if (!chatId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma chat history <chatId> [limit]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (!fs.existsSync(chatLogDir)) {
        console.log('No private chat logs.');
        return;
      }
      const files = fs.readdirSync(chatLogDir).filter(f => f.startsWith(chatId + '_') && f.endsWith('.log')).sort().reverse();
      const lines = [];
      for (const f of files) {
        const content = fs.readFileSync(pathMod.default.join(chatLogDir, f), 'utf-8');
        const fileLines = content.split('\n').filter(l => l.trim());
        lines.push(...fileLines.reverse());
        if (lines.length >= limit) break;
      }
      lines.reverse().slice(-limit).forEach(l => console.log(l));
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'search') {
      const query = args.slice(2).join(' ');
      if (!query) {
        console.error('Usage: alma chat search <keyword>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (!fs.existsSync(chatLogDir)) {
        console.log('No private chat logs.');
        return;
      }
      const files = fs.readdirSync(chatLogDir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.log')).sort();
      let found = 0;
      for (const f of files) {
        const content = fs.readFileSync(pathMod.default.join(chatLogDir, f), 'utf-8');
        const matching = content.split('\n').filter(l => l.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()));
        for (const line of matching) {
          console.log(`[${f}] ${line}`);
          found++;
        }
      }
      if (!found) console.log('No matches.');
      return;
    }
    console.error('Usage: alma chat <list|history|search> [args]');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Discord send ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'discord') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'list' || subcmd === 'servers') {
      const result = await api('GET', '/api/discord/servers');
      const servers = result.servers || [];
      if (servers.length === 0) {
        console.log('No Discord servers connected.');
      } else {
        for (const s of servers) {
          console.log(`\n🏠 ${s.name} (ID: ${s.id}, ${s.memberCount} members)`);
          for (const ch of s.channels) {
            console.log(`  #${ch.name} — ${ch.id} (${ch.type})`);
          }
        }
      }
      process.exit(0);
    }
    if (subcmd === 'send') {
      const channelId = args[2];
      // Parse --reply-to flag
      let replyTo;
      const replyIdx = args.indexOf('--reply-to');
      let msgArgs = args.slice(3);
      if (replyIdx >= 0) {
        replyTo = args[replyIdx + 1];
        msgArgs = [...args.slice(3, replyIdx), ...args.slice(replyIdx + 2)];
      }
      const message = msgArgs.join(' ').replace(/\\n/g, '\n');
      if (!channelId || !message) {
        console.error('Usage: alma discord send <channelId> "<message>" [--reply-to <messageId>]  (over 2000 chars is split into several messages)');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const result = await api('POST', `/api/discord/channels/${channelId}/send`, {
          message,
          replyTo
        });
        if (result?.ok || result?.messageId) {
          // Over 2000 chars Discord gets several messages, never a truncated one
          const parts = result.parts > 1 ? ` as ${result.parts} messages (Discord's 2000-char limit)` : '';
          console.log(`✅ Message sent to Discord channel ${channelId}${parts}`);
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result?.error || result?.description || 'unknown error');
          process.exit(1);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error sending Discord message:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'send-photo' || subcmd === 'send-file') {
      const channelId = args[2];
      const filePath = args[3];
      const caption = args.slice(4).join(' ') || undefined;
      if (!channelId || !filePath) {
        console.error(`Usage: alma discord ${subcmd} <channelId> <filePath> [caption]`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const endpoint = subcmd === 'send-photo' ? 'send-photo' : 'send-file';
        const result = await api('POST', `/api/discord/channels/${channelId}/${endpoint}`, {
          filePath,
          caption
        });
        if (result?.ok || result?.messageId) {
          console.log(`✅ ${subcmd === 'send-photo' ? 'Photo' : 'File'} sent to Discord channel ${channelId}`);
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result?.error || 'unknown error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`Error sending Discord ${subcmd}:`, err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'sticker') {
      const channelId = args[2];
      const stickerId = args[3];
      if (!channelId || !stickerId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma discord sticker <channelId> <stickerId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const result = await api('POST', `/api/discord/channels/${channelId}/sticker`, {
          stickerId
        });
        if (result?.ok || result?.messageId) {
          console.log(`✅ Sticker sent to Discord channel ${channelId}`);
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result?.error || 'unknown error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error sending Discord sticker:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'sticker-list') {
      const guildId = args[2] || '';
      try {
        const qs = guildId ? `?guildId=${guildId}` : '';
        const result = await api('GET', `/api/discord/stickers${qs}`);
        if (result?.stickers) {
          for (const s of result.stickers) {
            console.log(`${s.id}\t${s.name}\t[${s.guildName}]`);
          }
          if (result.stickers.length === 0) console.log('No stickers found.');
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result?.error || 'unknown error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error listing stickers:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'sticker-find') {
      const query = args.slice(2).join(' ').toLowerCase();
      if (!query) {
        console.error('Usage: alma discord sticker-find <query>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const result = await api('GET', `/api/discord/stickers`);
        if (result?.stickers) {
          const matches = result.stickers.filter(s => s.name.toLowerCase().includes(query));
          for (const s of matches) {
            console.log(`${s.id}\t${s.name}\t[${s.guildName}]`);
          }
          if (matches.length === 0) console.log('No stickers matching query.');
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result?.error || 'unknown error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error searching stickers:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'dm') {
      const userId = args[2];
      const message = args.slice(3).join(' ').replace(/\\n/g, '\n');
      if (!userId || !message) {
        console.error('Usage: alma discord dm <userId> "<message>"');
        console.error('  userId: Discord numeric user ID');
        console.error('  Note: The user must share a server with the bot or have DMs enabled.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const result = await api('POST', '/api/discord/dm', {
          userId,
          message
        });
        if (result?.ok || result?.messageId) {
          console.log(`✅ DM sent to Discord user ${userId}`);
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result?.error || result?.description || 'unknown error');
          process.exit(1);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error sending Discord DM:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'delete') {
      const channelId = args[2];
      const messageId = args[3];
      if (!channelId || !messageId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma discord delete <channelId> <messageId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        await api('DELETE', `/api/discord/channels/${channelId}/messages/${messageId}`);
        console.log('✅ Message deleted.');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error deleting Discord message:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'messages' || subcmd === 'history') {
      // alma discord messages <channelId> [--limit N] [--around messageId]
      const channelId = args[2];
      if (!channelId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma discord messages <channelId> [--limit N] [--around <messageId>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const limitIdx = args.indexOf('--limit');
      const limit = limitIdx >= 0 ? parseInt(args[limitIdx + 1]) || 20 : 20;
      const aroundIdx = args.indexOf('--around');
      const around = aroundIdx >= 0 ? args[aroundIdx + 1] : undefined;
      try {
        let url = `/api/discord/channels/${channelId}/messages?limit=${limit}`;
        if (around) url += `&around=${around}`;
        const result = await api('GET', url);
        if (result.messages) {
          for (const m of result.messages) {
            const ts = new Date(m.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', {
              hour12: false,
              hour: '2-digit',
              minute: '2-digit'
            });
            const attachHint = m.attachments?.length > 0 ? ` [${m.attachments.length} attachment(s)]` : '';
            console.log(`[${ts}] [${m.id}] ${m.author} (${m.authorId}): ${m.content}${attachHint}`);
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error fetching messages:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'fetch') {
      // alma discord fetch <channelId> <messageId> — fetch a single message
      const channelId = args[2];
      const messageId = args[3];
      if (!channelId || !messageId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma discord fetch <channelId> <messageId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const result = await api('GET', `/api/discord/channels/${channelId}/messages?limit=1&around=${messageId}`);
        if (result.messages) {
          for (const m of result.messages) {
            console.log(`Author: ${m.author} (${m.authorId})`);
            console.log(`Time: ${m.timestamp}`);
            console.log(`Content: ${m.content}`);
            if (m.attachments?.length > 0) {
              console.log(`Attachments: ${m.attachments.join(', ')}`);
            }
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error fetching message:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else {
      console.error('Usage: alma discord <list|send|send-photo|send-file|dm|delete|messages|fetch|sticker|sticker-list|sticker-find> [args]');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    process.exit(0);
  }

  // ── Feishu send ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'feishu') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'send') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const message = args.slice(3).join(' ').replace(/\\n/g, '\n');
      if (!chatId || !message) {
        console.error('Usage: alma feishu send <chatId> "<message>"');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const result = await api('POST', `/api/feishu/chats/${chatId}/send`, {
          message
        });
        if (result?.ok || result?.messageId) {
          console.log(`✅ Message sent to Feishu chat ${chatId}`);
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result?.error || result?.description || 'unknown error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error sending Feishu message:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (subcmd === 'send-photo') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const filePath = args[3];
      const caption = args.slice(4).join(' ') || undefined;
      if (!chatId || !filePath) {
        console.error('Usage: alma feishu send-photo <chatId> <filePath> [caption]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const result = await api('POST', '/api/feishu/send-photo', {
          chatId,
          filePath,
          caption
        });
        if (result?.ok) {
          console.log(`✅ Photo sent to Feishu chat ${chatId}`);
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result?.error || 'unknown error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error:', err.message || err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else {
      console.error('Usage: alma feishu <send|send-photo> [args]');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    process.exit(0);
  }

  // ── Group Chat History ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'group') {
    const fs = await import('fs');
    const os = await import('os');
    const pathMod = await import('path');
    const logDir = pathMod.default.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'groups');
    const subcmd = args[1]; // list | history | search

    if (!subcmd || subcmd === 'list') {
      if (!fs.existsSync(logDir)) {
        console.log('No group chat logs yet.');
        return;
      }
      const files = fs.readdirSync(logDir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.log'));
      const groups = new Set(files.map(f => f.split('_')[0]));
      for (const g of groups) {
        const groupFiles = files.filter(f => f.startsWith(g + '_'));
        const latest = groupFiles.sort().pop();
        console.log(`  Group ${g} (${groupFiles.length} day(s), latest: ${latest})`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'history') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const limit = parseInt(args[3]) || 50;
      if (!chatId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma group history <chatId> [limit]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (!fs.existsSync(logDir)) {
        console.log('No group chat logs.');
        return;
      }
      // Read all log files for this group, most recent first
      const files = fs.readdirSync(logDir).filter(f => f.startsWith(chatId + '_') && f.endsWith('.log')).sort().reverse();
      const lines = [];
      for (const f of files) {
        const content = fs.readFileSync(pathMod.default.join(logDir, f), 'utf-8');
        const fileLines = content.split('\n').filter(l => l.trim());
        lines.push(...fileLines.reverse());
        if (lines.length >= limit) break;
      }
      lines.reverse().slice(-limit).forEach(l => console.log(l));
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'search') {
      const query = args.slice(2).join(' ');
      if (!query) {
        console.error('Usage: alma group search <keyword>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (!fs.existsSync(logDir)) {
        console.log('No group chat logs.');
        return;
      }
      const files = fs.readdirSync(logDir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.log')).sort();
      let found = 0;
      for (const f of files) {
        const content = fs.readFileSync(pathMod.default.join(logDir, f), 'utf-8');
        const matching = content.split('\n').filter(l => l.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()));
        for (const line of matching) {
          console.log(`[${f}] ${line}`);
          found++;
        }
      }
      if (found === 0) console.log('No matches found.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'send') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const message = args.slice(3).join(' ').replace(/\\n/g, '\n');
      if (!chatId || !message) {
        console.error('Usage: alma group send <chatId> <message>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      // Send via Alma API so that lastBotReplyTime and groupHistory are updated
      try {
        const result = await api('POST', `/api/groups/${chatId}/send`, {
          message
        });
        if (result?.ok || result?.messageId) {
          console.log(`✅ Message sent to group ${chatId}`);
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result?.error || result?.description || 'unknown error');
        }
      } catch {
        // Fallback: direct Telegram API (in case /api/groups/:chatId/send doesn't exist yet)
        const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
        const botToken = settings?.telegram?.botToken;
        if (!botToken) {
          console.error('Telegram bot not configured.');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        const resp = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${botToken}/sendMessage`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            chat_id: chatId,
            text: message
          })
        });
        const result = await resp.json();
        if (result.ok) {
          console.log(`✅ Message sent to group ${chatId} (direct)`);
        } else {
          console.error('Failed:', result.description);
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'send-photo') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const filePath = args[3];
      const caption = args.slice(4).join(' ') || undefined;
      if (!chatId || !filePath) {
        console.error('Usage: alma group send-photo <chatId> <filePath> [caption]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const result = await api('POST', `/api/groups/${chatId}/send-photo`, {
        filePath,
        caption
      });
      if (result?.ok) console.log(`✅ Photo sent to group ${chatId}`);else console.error('Failed:', result?.error || 'unknown');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'send-document') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const filePath = args[3];
      const caption = args.slice(4).join(' ') || undefined;
      if (!chatId || !filePath) {
        console.error('Usage: alma group send-document <chatId> <filePath> [caption]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const result = await api('POST', `/api/groups/${chatId}/send-document`, {
        filePath,
        caption
      });
      if (result?.ok) console.log(`✅ Document sent to group ${chatId}`);else console.error('Failed:', result?.error || 'unknown');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'send-video') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const filePath = args[3];
      const caption = args.slice(4).join(' ') || undefined;
      if (!chatId || !filePath) {
        console.error('Usage: alma group send-video <chatId> <filePath> [caption]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const result = await api('POST', `/api/groups/${chatId}/send-video`, {
        filePath,
        caption
      });
      if (result?.ok) console.log(`✅ Video sent to group ${chatId}`);else console.error('Failed:', result?.error || 'unknown');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'pin') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const messageId = args[3];
      if (!chatId || !messageId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma group pin <chatId> <messageId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const res = await api('POST', `/api/groups/${chatId}/pin`, {
          messageId: Number(messageId)
        });
        console.log(res?.success ? `✅ Message ${messageId} pinned` : `❌ Failed to pin: ${res?.error || 'unknown'}`);
      } catch {
        console.error('❌ Failed. Is Alma running?');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'unpin') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const messageId = args[3]; // optional
      if (!chatId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma group unpin <chatId> [messageId]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      try {
        const body = messageId ? {
          messageId: Number(messageId)
        } : {};
        const res = await api('POST', `/api/groups/${chatId}/unpin`, body);
        console.log(res?.success ? `✅ Unpinned${messageId ? ` message ${messageId}` : ' all'}` : `❌ Failed: ${res?.error || 'unknown'}`);
      } catch {
        console.error('❌ Failed. Is Alma running?');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'context') {
      const chatId = args[2];
      const limit = parseInt(args[3]) || 100;
      if (!chatId) {
        console.error('Usage: alma group context <chatId> [limit]');
        process.exit(1);
      }

      // 1. Group info from Telegram API
      const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
      const botToken = settings?.telegram?.botToken;
      if (botToken) {
        try {
          const chatResp = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${botToken}/getChat`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json'
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              chat_id: chatId
            })
          });
          const chatData = await chatResp.json();
          if (chatData.ok) {
            const c = chatData.result;
            console.log(`=== Group Info ===`);
            console.log(`Title: ${c.title || 'N/A'}`);
            console.log(`Type: ${c.type}`);
            console.log(`Description: ${c.description || 'N/A'}`);
            if (c.pinned_message) {
              const pin = c.pinned_message;
              const pinFrom = pin.from?.first_name || 'Unknown';
              console.log(`Pinned: [${pinFrom}] ${pin.text || pin.caption || '[media]'}`);
            }
            console.log('');
          }
        } catch {
          /* ignore */
        }

        // 2. Member count + admin list
        try {
          const countResp = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${botToken}/getChatMemberCount`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json'
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              chat_id: chatId
            })
          });
          const countData = await countResp.json();
          if (countData.ok) console.log(`Members: ${countData.result}`);
          const adminsResp = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${botToken}/getChatAdministrators`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json'
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              chat_id: chatId
            })
          });
          const adminsData = await adminsResp.json();
          if (adminsData.ok) {
