      _fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, Buffer.from(data.image_base64, 'base64'));
      console.log(outputPath);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'click') {
      const selector = pos[0];
      if (!selector) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab click <selector>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/click', withWs({
        selector
      }));
      console.log(data.success ? 'Clicked.' : `Click failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
      if (!data.success) process.exit(1);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'type') {
      const selector = pos[0];
      const text = pos[1];
      const pressEnter = args.includes('--enter');
      if (!selector || text === undefined) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab type <selector> <text> [--enter]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/type', withWs({
        selector,
        text,
        pressEnter
      }));
      console.log(data.success ? 'Typed.' : `Type failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
      if (!data.success) process.exit(1);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'read') {
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/read', withWs());
      if (data.value) {
        if (data.value.title) console.log(`# ${data.value.title}\n`);
        if (data.value.url) console.log(`URL: ${data.value.url}\n`);
        if (data.value.text) console.log(data.value.text);
      } else console.error(data.error || 'read failed');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'read-dom') {
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/read-dom', withWs());
      if (data.value) prettyPrint(data.value);else console.error(data.error || 'read-dom failed');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'eval') {
      const code = pos.join(' ');
      if (!code) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab eval <code>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/eval', withWs({
        code
      }));
      if (data.success) prettyPrint(data.value);else {
        console.error('Error:', data.error);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'scroll') {
      const direction = pos[0];
      const amount = pos[1] ? parseInt(pos[1], 10) : undefined;
      if (!['up', 'down'].includes(direction)) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab scroll <up|down> [amount]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/scroll', withWs({
        direction,
        amount
      }));
      if (data.success) console.log(`Scrolled ${direction}.`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'viewport') {
      if (pos[0] === 'reset') {
        await api('POST', '/api/iab/viewport', withWs());
        console.log('Viewport emulation cleared.');
        return;
      }
      const width = parseInt(pos[0], 10);
      const height = parseInt(pos[1], 10);
      const mobile = args.includes('--mobile');
      if (!width || !height) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab viewport <w> <h> [--mobile] | reset');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/viewport', withWs({
        width,
        height,
        mobile
      }));
      console.log(data.success ? `Viewport set to ${width}x${height}${mobile ? ' (mobile)' : ''}.` : `Failed: ${data.error}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'locator') {
      let payload;
      try {
        payload = JSON.parse(pos[0] || '{}');
      } catch {
        console.error("Usage: alma iab locator '<json: {steps,action}>'");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/locator', withWs(payload));
      if (data.success) {
        if (data.value !== undefined) prettyPrint(data.value);else console.log('OK.');
      } else {
        console.error('Error:', data.error);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'cdp') {
      const method = pos[0];
      let params = {};
      if (pos[1]) {
        try {
          params = JSON.parse(pos[1]);
        } catch {
          console.error('cdp params must be JSON');
          process.exit(1);
        }
      }
      if (!method) {
        console.error("Usage: alma iab cdp <method> '<jsonParams>'");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/cdp', withWs({
        method,
        params
      }));
      prettyPrint(data.result);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'upload') {
      const selector = posClean[0];
      const files = posClean.slice(1);
      if (!selector || files.length === 0) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab upload <selector> <file> [<file2>...]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/upload', withWs({
        selector,
        files
      }));
      console.log(data.success ? `Uploaded ${files.length} file(s).` : `Upload failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
      if (!data.success) process.exit(1);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'download') {
      const url = posClean[0];
      if (!url) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab download <url> [--out <path>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/download', withWs({
        url,
        out: flagVal('--out')
      }));
      if (data.success) console.log(`Downloaded -> ${data.value?.path} (${data.value?.bytes ?? '?'} bytes)`);else {
        console.error(`Download failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'download-media') {
      const selector = posClean[0];
      if (!selector) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab download-media <selector> [--out <path>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/download-media', withWs({
        selector,
        out: flagVal('--out')
      }));
      if (data.success) console.log(`Downloaded -> ${data.value?.path} (${data.value?.bytes ?? '?'} bytes)`);else {
        console.error(`Download failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'export') {
      const format = posClean[0] || 'pdf';
      if (!['pdf', 'html', 'mhtml', 'md'].includes(format)) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab export <pdf|html|mhtml|md> [--out <path>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/export', withWs({
        format,
        out: flagVal('--out')
      }));
      if (data.success) console.log(`Exported -> ${data.value?.path}`);else {
        console.error(`Export failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'export-gsuite') {
      const type = posClean[0];
      if (!type) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab export-gsuite <pdf|docx|xlsx|csv|pptx|...> [--out <path>]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/export-gsuite', withWs({
        type,
        out: flagVal('--out')
      }));
      if (data.success) console.log(`Exported -> ${data.value?.path}`);else {
        console.error(`Export failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'fetch') {
      const urls = posClean;
      if (urls.length === 0) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab fetch <url> [<url2>...] [--html]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/fetch', withWs({
        urls,
        html: args.includes('--html'),
        timeoutMs: flagVal('--timeout') ? parseInt(flagVal('--timeout'), 10) : undefined,
        env: flagVal('--env')
      }));
      if (data.success) prettyPrint(data.value);else {
        console.error(`Fetch failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'dialog') {
      const action = posClean[0];
      if (!['watch', 'get', 'accept', 'dismiss', 'stop'].includes(action)) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab dialog <watch|get|accept [text]|dismiss|stop> [--auto accept|dismiss]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/dialog', withWs({
        action,
        auto: flagVal('--auto'),
        text: posClean[1]
      }));
      if (action === 'get') prettyPrint(data.value ?? null);else console.log(data.success ? `Dialog ${action} ok.` : `Dialog ${action} failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
      if (!data.success && action !== 'get') process.exit(1);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'clipboard') {
      const action = posClean[0] || 'read';
      if (action === 'write') {
        const text = posClean.slice(1).join(' ');
        const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/clipboard', {
          action: 'write',
          text
        });
        console.log(data.success ? 'Clipboard written.' : `Failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
        if (!data.success) process.exit(1);
      } else {
        const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/clipboard', {
          action: 'read'
        });
        if (data.success) prettyPrint(data.value);else {
          console.error(data.error || 'clipboard read failed');
          process.exit(1);
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'cua') {
      const action = (posClean[0] || '').replace('-', '_'); // double-click -> double_click
      const num = v => v === undefined ? undefined : Number(v);
      let body;
      if (action === 'click' || action === 'double_click' || action === 'move') body = {
        action,
        x: num(posClean[1]),
        y: num(posClean[2]),
        button: flagVal('--button')
      };else if (action === 'scroll') body = {
        action,
        x: num(posClean[1]),
        y: num(posClean[2]),
        deltaX: num(posClean[3]) || 0,
        deltaY: num(posClean[4]) || 0
      };else if (action === 'type') body = {
        action,
        text: posClean.slice(1).join(' ')
      };else if (action === 'keypress') body = {
        action,
        keys: (posClean[1] || '').split('+')
      };else {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab cua <click|double-click|move <x> <y> | scroll <x> <y> <dx> <dy> | type <text> | keypress <key[+key]>>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/cua', withWs(body));
      console.log(data.success ? `cua ${action} ok.` : `cua ${action} failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
      if (!data.success) process.exit(1);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'get-visible-dom') {
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/get-visible-dom', withWs());
      if (data.success) prettyPrint(data.value);else {
        console.error(data.error || 'get-visible-dom failed');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'dom-click') {
      const ref = posClean[0];
      if (!ref) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab dom-click <ref>  (get refs from get-visible-dom)');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/dom-click', withWs({
        ref
      }));
      console.log(data.success ? 'Clicked.' : `Click failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
      if (!data.success) process.exit(1);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'dom-type') {
      const ref = posClean[0];
      const text = posClean.slice(1).join(' ');
      if (!ref || text === '') {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab dom-type <ref> <text>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/dom-type', withWs({
        ref,
        text
      }));
      console.log(data.success ? 'Typed.' : `Type failed: ${data.error || 'unknown'}`);
      if (!data.success) process.exit(1);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'profiles') {
      const data = await api('GET', '/api/iab/profiles');
      const profiles = data.profiles || [];
      if (profiles.length === 0) console.log('No Chromium-based browser profiles found.');else for (const p of profiles) console.log(`  ${p.source}\t${p.appName}${p.profileName ? ' · ' + p.profileName : ''}${p.userName ? ' (' + p.userName + ')' : ''}\n    ${p.profilePath}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'history') {
      let source = flagVal('--source');
      let profilePath = flagVal('--profile');
      // Auto-pick the first (or requested) profile when not fully specified.
      if (!profilePath) {
        const pd = await api('GET', '/api/iab/profiles');
        const profiles = pd.profiles || [];
        const pick = source ? profiles.find(p => p.source === source) : profiles[0];
        if (!pick) {
          console.error('No browser profile found; pass --source and --profile.');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        source = pick.source;
        profilePath = pick.profilePath;
      }
      const limit = flagVal('--limit') ? parseInt(flagVal('--limit'), 10) : undefined;
      const query = flagVal('--query');
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/history', {
        source,
        profilePath,
        limit,
        query
      });
      if (data.success) prettyPrint(data.entries);else {
        console.error(data.error || 'history failed');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'pip') {
      const action = posClean[0] || 'toggle';
      if (!['show', 'hide', 'toggle', 'status'].includes(action)) {
        console.error('Usage: alma iab pip <show|hide|toggle|status>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (action === 'status') {
        const data = await api('POST', '/api/iab/pip/status', withWs());
        console.log(data.open ? `PIP open${data.workspace ? ' (workspace ' + data.workspace + ')' : ''}.` : 'PIP closed.');
        return;
      }
      const data = await api('POST', `/api/iab/pip/${action}`, withWs());
      console.log(data.open ? 'PIP is floating (always-on-top mirror of the preview).' : 'PIP closed.');
      return;
    }
    console.error(`Unknown iab subcommand: ${subcmd}. Run 'alma iab help' for usage.`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Artifact Sidebar: Terminal ──────────────────────────
  // Drive / read the shared terminal in the artifact sidebar. Routes to the current
  // thread's workspace via $ALMA_THREAD_ID unless --session/--workspace/--thread given.
  if (cmd === 'terminal' || cmd === 'term') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    const flag = name => {
      const i = args.indexOf(name);
      return i !== -1 && args[i + 1] !== undefined ? args[i + 1] : undefined;
    };
    const sessionFlag = flag('--session');
    const workspaceFlag = flag('--workspace');
    const threadFlag = flag('--thread') || process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
    const raw = args.includes('--raw');
    const FLAG_NAMES = ['--session', '--workspace', '--thread', '--name', '--timeout'];
    const pos = [];
    for (let i = 2; i < args.length; i++) {
      if (FLAG_NAMES.includes(args[i])) {
        i++;
        continue;
      }
      if (args[i] === '--raw') continue;
      pos.push(args[i]);
    }
    const resolveSession = async () => {
      if (sessionFlag) return sessionFlag;
      const q = threadFlag ? `?threadId=${threadFlag}` : workspaceFlag ? `?workspaceId=${workspaceFlag}` : '';
      const list = await api('GET', `/api/terminal/sessions${q}`);
      return list.sessions && list.sessions[0] ? list.sessions[0].id : null;
    };
    if (!subcmd || subcmd === 'help') {
      console.log(`alma terminal — run/read the artifact sidebar's shared terminal

  alma terminal exec "<command>"        Run a command, stream it into the sidebar terminal,
                                        wait, print its output; exits with the command's code
  alma terminal read [--raw]            Print the terminal scrollback (what the user sees)
  alma terminal write "<text>"          Send raw keystrokes (e.g. answer a prompt; use $'\\n' for Enter)
  alma terminal list                    List terminal sessions
  alma terminal create [--name <n>]     Create a new terminal session
  alma terminal kill <sessionId>        Kill a session

  Targets the current thread's workspace via $ALMA_THREAD_ID. Override with
  --thread <id>, --workspace <id>, or --session <id>. --timeout <ms> for exec.`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'exec') {
      const command = pos.join(' ');
      if (!command) {
        console.error('Usage: alma terminal exec "<command>"');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        command
      };
      if (sessionFlag) body.sessionId = sessionFlag;else if (workspaceFlag) body.workspaceId = workspaceFlag;else if (threadFlag) body.threadId = threadFlag;else {
        console.error('No target: set $ALMA_THREAD_ID or pass --workspace/--session.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const t = flag('--timeout');
      if (t) body.timeout = parseInt(t, 10);
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/terminal/exec', body);
      if (data.output) process.stdout.write(data.output.endsWith('\n') ? data.output : data.output + '\n');
      if (data.timedOut) console.error('(command timed out)');
      process.exit(typeof data.exitCode === 'number' ? data.exitCode : 0);
    }
    if (subcmd === 'read') {
      const sid = await resolveSession();
      if (!sid) {
        console.error('No terminal session. Run `alma terminal create` or open the Terminal tab.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('GET', `/api/terminal/${sid}/output${raw ? '?raw=1' : ''}`);
      process.stdout.write((data.output || '') + '\n');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'write') {
      const text = pos.join(' ');
      const sid = await resolveSession();
      if (!sid) {
        console.error('No terminal session.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('POST', `/api/terminal/${sid}/input`, {
        data: text
      });
      console.log('sent. (typed into the sidebar Terminal the user sees — dock → Terminal tab)');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'list') {
      const q = threadFlag ? `?threadId=${threadFlag}` : workspaceFlag ? `?workspaceId=${workspaceFlag}` : '';
      const data = await api('GET', `/api/terminal/sessions${q}`);
      if (!data.sessions || !data.sessions.length) console.log('No terminal sessions.');else for (const s of data.sessions) console.log(`  ${s.id}  ${s.name || '(unnamed)'}  ws=${s.workspaceId}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'create') {
      const workspaceId = workspaceFlag || (threadFlag ? (await api('GET', `/api/threads/${threadFlag}`)).workspaceId : undefined);
      if (!workspaceId) {
        console.error('Cannot resolve a workspace. Pass --workspace <id> or set $ALMA_THREAD_ID.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/terminal/create', {
        workspaceId,
        threadId: threadFlag,
        name: flag('--name')
      });
      console.log(data.sessionId);
      console.log('(created in the dock → Terminal tab; the dock switches to it automatically. This is NOT Settings → Remote Hosts — say which surface you used when you report back.)');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'kill') {
      const sid = pos[0] || sessionFlag;
      if (!sid) {
        console.error('Usage: alma terminal kill <sessionId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('DELETE', `/api/terminal/${sid}`);
      console.log('killed.');
      return;
    }
    console.error(`Unknown terminal subcommand: ${subcmd}. Run 'alma terminal help'.`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Artifact Sidebar: Preview server lifecycle ──────────
  // Bring the sidebar Preview up/down; drive the browser itself with `alma iab`.
  if (cmd === 'preview') {
    const subcmd = args[1];
    const flag = name => {
      const i = args.indexOf(name);
      return i !== -1 && args[i + 1] !== undefined ? args[i + 1] : undefined;
    };
    const threadFlag = flag('--thread') || process.env.ALMA_THREAD_ID;
    const resolveWorkspace = async () => {
      const wf = flag('--workspace');
      if (wf) return wf;
      if (threadFlag) return (await api('GET', `/api/threads/${threadFlag}`)).workspaceId;
      return undefined;
    };
    if (!subcmd || subcmd === 'help') {
      console.log(`alma preview — control the artifact sidebar Preview server

  alma preview start [--entry <file>]   Start the dev/static server for the workspace
  alma preview stop                     Stop it
  alma preview status                   Show status + URL
  alma preview detect                   Detect project type (vite/static/…)

  Targets the current thread's workspace via $ALMA_THREAD_ID (or --workspace <id>).
  Once running, drive the page with: alma iab read-dom | eval | screenshot | click …`);
      return;
    }
    const wsId = await resolveWorkspace();
    if (!wsId) {
      console.error('Cannot resolve a workspace. Pass --workspace <id> or set $ALMA_THREAD_ID.');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    if (subcmd === 'start') {
      const data = await api('POST', `/api/workspaces/${wsId}/preview/start`, {
        entryFile: flag('--entry')
      });
      console.log(`Preview ${data.status || ''}${data.url ? ': ' + data.url : ''}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'stop') {
      await api('POST', `/api/workspaces/${wsId}/preview/stop`, {});
      console.log('Preview stopped.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'status') {
      prettyPrint(await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${wsId}/preview/status`));
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'detect') {
      prettyPrint(await api('GET', `/api/workspaces/${wsId}/preview/detect`));
      return;
    }
    console.error(`Unknown preview subcommand: ${subcmd}. Run 'alma preview help'.`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── Computer Use (macOS desktop automation via AX) ──────
  if (cmd === 'cu') {
    const subcmd = args[1];

    // Parse "<bundle-or-pid>" — a bundle id if it looks like one, else a PID.
    const parseTarget = raw => {
      if (!raw) return {};
      if (/^\d+$/.test(raw)) return {
        pid: parseInt(raw, 10)
      };
      return {
        bundle: raw
      };
    };

    // Flag lookup: `--foo=bar` or `--foo bar`. Returns undefined if absent.
    const flag = name => {
      for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
        const a = args[i];
        if (a === `--${name}`) return args[i + 1];
        if (a && a.startsWith(`--${name}=`)) return a.slice(name.length + 3);
      }
      return undefined;
    };
    const hasFlag = name => args.includes(`--${name}`);
    const asJson = hasFlag('json');
    if (!subcmd || subcmd === 'help') {
      console.log(`alma cu — macOS desktop automation (Codex-compatible verb set)

DISCOVERY
  alma cu status                      Helper bundle + daemon status
  alma cu doctor                      Ping daemon + report permissions
  alma cu grant                       Trigger AX permission dialog
  alma cu list_apps [--days=14]       Running + recently-used apps (w/ freq)
  alma cu apps [--json]               Running apps only (legacy)
  alma cu windows <bundle|pid>        List AX windows of an app

STATE (call once per turn)
  alma cu get_app_state <bundle|pid> [--window=ID] [--depth=N] [--no-shot]
                                      AX tree + window screenshot combined
  alma cu snap <bundle|pid> [--window=ID] [--depth=N] [--all]
                                      AX tree only (legacy)

ACTIONS
  alma cu click <ref> [--strategy=auto|physical|ax] [--button=left|right|middle]
                [--clicks=N]
  alma cu click --pixel <x> <y> [--pid=N] [--button=left|right|middle] [--clicks=N]
  alma cu perform_secondary_action <ref>
  alma cu perform_secondary_action --pixel <x> <y> [--pid=N]
  alma cu drag <x1> <y1> <x2> <y2> [--pid=N] [--button=left|right|middle]
  alma cu type <ref> <text> [--append]          AX-scoped text replacement
  alma cu type_text <text> [--pid=N]            CGEvent keystroke input
  alma cu press <ref> <key>                     Enter|Escape|Space|Increment|Decrement|ShowMenu
  alma cu press_key <combo> [--pid=N]           xdotool syntax: cmd+s, ctrl+shift+t, Return…
  alma cu set_value <ref> <value>               Direct AXValue set (sliders, inputs)
  alma cu scroll <ref> <up|down|left|right> [--pages=N]

OTHER
  alma cu raise <bundle|pid> [--window=ID]
  alma cu shot <bundle|pid> [--window=ID] [--out=PATH] [--max-width=N]
  alma cu lens <on|off|toggle>
  alma cu shutdown

Action flags: --no-cursor hides the lens for one action.

Hint: start every turn with \`alma cu get_app_state <bundle>\` — refs become
stale across turns, so the screenshot is your visual anchor.
`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'status') {
      const data = await api('GET', '/api/computer-use/status');
      prettyPrint(data);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'doctor') {
      const status = await api('GET', '/api/computer-use/status');
      if (!status.available) {
        console.log('helper:    NOT FOUND');
        console.log('platform: ', status.platform);
        console.log('\nBuild the helper with: pnpm build:computer-use');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const ping = await api('GET', '/api/computer-use/ping');
      const perms = await api('GET', '/api/computer-use/permissions');
      console.log(`helper:              ${status.helper_path}`);
      console.log(`daemon:              v${ping.version} (uptime ${ping.uptime_s}s)`);
      console.log(`accessibility:       ${perms.accessibility ? 'granted' : 'NOT granted'}`);
      console.log(`screen recording:    ${perms.screen_recording ? 'granted' : 'NOT granted'}`);
      if (!perms.accessibility) {
        console.log('\nRun `alma cu grant`, then grant "Alma Computer Use" in');
        console.log('System Settings → Privacy & Security → Accessibility.');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'grant') {
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/grant');
      if (data.accessibility) console.log('Accessibility already granted.');else console.log('Requested Accessibility permission. Approve the dialog, then `alma cu doctor`.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'apps') {
      const data = await api('GET', '/api/computer-use/apps');
      if (asJson) {
        console.log(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
        return;
      }
      for (const a of data.apps) {
        const mark = a.active ? '*' : ' ';
        console.log(`${mark} [${String(a.pid).padStart(6)}] ${a.bundle || '?'.padEnd(30)}  ${a.name}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'launch_app' || subcmd === 'launch') {
      const bundle = args[2];
      if (!bundle) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu launch_app <bundle> [--activates]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        bundle,
        activates: hasFlag('activates')
      };
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/launch_app', body);
      console.log(`${data.app.name || bundle} pid=${data.app.pid}${data.activates ? ' (foreground)' : ' (background)'}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'list_apps') {
      const body = {};
      const d = flag('days');
      if (d) body.days = parseInt(d, 10);
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/list_apps', body);
      if (asJson) {
        console.log(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
        return;
      }
      for (const a of data.apps) {
        const mark = a.active ? '*' : a.running ? 'R' : ' ';
        const pid = a.pid != null ? String(a.pid).padStart(6) : '     -';
        const freq = a.use_count != null ? `(${a.use_count}×)` : '';
        console.log(`${mark} [${pid}] ${(a.bundle || '?').padEnd(32)}  ${a.name}  ${freq}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'windows') {
      const target = parseTarget(args[2]);
      if (!target.bundle && !target.pid) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu windows <bundle|pid>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/windows', target);
      if (asJson) {
        console.log(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
        return;
      }
      for (const w of data.windows) {
        const f = w.focused ? '*' : ' ';
        const m = w.minimized ? ' (minimized)' : '';
        console.log(`${f} [${w.id}] ${w.title || '(untitled)'} ${Math.round(w.bounds.w)}×${Math.round(w.bounds.h)}${m}`);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'get_app_state') {
      const target = parseTarget(args[2]);
      if (!target.bundle && !target.pid) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu get_app_state <bundle|pid> [--window=ID] [--depth=N] [--no-shot] [--shot-out=PATH] [--shot-max-width=N]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        ...target
      };
      const w = flag('window');
      if (w) body.window_id = parseInt(w, 10);
      const d = flag('depth');
      if (d) body.depth = parseInt(d, 10);
      if (hasFlag('all')) body.interactive_only = false;
      if (hasFlag('no-shot')) body.include_screenshot = false;
      const sp = flag('shot-out');
      if (sp) body.screenshot_path = sp;
      const smw = flag('shot-max-width');
      if (smw) body.screenshot_max_width = parseInt(smw, 10);
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/get_app_state', body);
      if (asJson) {
        console.log(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
        return;
      }
      console.log(`${data.app.name} (${data.app.bundle || 'pid=' + data.app.pid})`);
      console.log(`window: ${data.window.title || ''} [${data.window.id ?? '?'}]`);
      if (data.screenshot) {
        console.log(`screenshot: ${data.screenshot.path} ${data.screenshot.width}×${data.screenshot.height}`);
      } else if (data.screenshot_error) {
        console.log(`screenshot: FAILED (${data.screenshot_error})`);
      }
      console.log(`snapshot: ${data.snapshot_id}  elements: ${data.elements.length}`);
      console.log('');
      for (const el of data.elements) {
        const parts = [`${el.ref.padEnd(5)}`, el.role];
        if (el.subrole) parts.push(`(${el.subrole})`);
        if (el.title) parts.push(`"${el.title}"`);else if (el.value) parts.push(`= ${JSON.stringify(String(el.value).slice(0, 60))}`);else if (el.description) parts.push(`~ ${JSON.stringify(String(el.description).slice(0, 60))}`);
        if (!el.enabled) parts.push('[disabled]');
        if (el.focused) parts.push('[focused]');
        console.log(parts.join(' '));
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'snap') {
      const target = parseTarget(args[2]);
      if (!target.bundle && !target.pid) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu snap <bundle|pid> [--window=ID] [--depth=N] [--all]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        ...target
      };
      const w = flag('window');
      if (w) body.window_id = parseInt(w, 10);
      const d = flag('depth');
      if (d) body.depth = parseInt(d, 10);
      if (hasFlag('all')) body.interactive_only = false;
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/snap', body);
      if (asJson) {
        console.log(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
        return;
      }
      console.log(`${data.app.name} (${data.app.bundle || 'pid=' + data.app.pid})`);
      console.log(`window: ${data.window.title || ''} [${data.window.id}]`);
      console.log(`snapshot: ${data.snapshot_id}  elements: ${data.elements.length}`);
      console.log('');
      for (const el of data.elements) {
        const parts = [`${el.ref.padEnd(5)}`, el.role];
        if (el.subrole) parts.push(`(${el.subrole})`);
        if (el.title) parts.push(`"${el.title}"`);else if (el.value) parts.push(`= ${JSON.stringify(String(el.value).slice(0, 60))}`);else if (el.description) parts.push(`~ ${JSON.stringify(String(el.description).slice(0, 60))}`);
        if (!el.enabled) parts.push('[disabled]');
        if (el.focused) parts.push('[focused]');
        console.log(parts.join(' '));
      }
      return;
    }
    const showCursor = !hasFlag('no-cursor');
    const buttonArg = (() => {
      const b = flag('button');
      if (!b) return 'left';
      if (!['left', 'right', 'middle'].includes(b)) {
        console.error(`--button must be left|right|middle`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return b;
    })();
    const clickCountArg = parseInt(flag('clicks') || '1', 10);
    if (subcmd === 'click') {
      // Two forms:
      //   alma cu click <ref> [--strategy=...] [--button=...] [--clicks=N]
      //   alma cu click --pixel <x> <y> [--pid=N] [--button=...] [--clicks=N]
      if (hasFlag('pixel')) {
        const xi = args.indexOf('--pixel');
        const x = parseFloat(args[xi + 1]);
        const y = parseFloat(args[xi + 2]);
        if (!Number.isFinite(x) || !Number.isFinite(y)) {
          console.error('Usage: alma cu click --pixel <x> <y> [--pid=N] [--button=...] [--clicks=N]');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        const body = {
          x,
          y,
          button: buttonArg,
          click_count: clickCountArg,
          show_cursor: showCursor
        };
        const p = flag('pid');
        if (p) body.pid = parseInt(p, 10);
        const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/click', body);
        console.log(`clicked @${x},${y} (${data.button}×${data.clicks})`);
        return;
      }
      const ref = args[2];
      if (!ref) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu click <ref> | --pixel <x> <y>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const strategy = flag('strategy') || 'auto';
      const body = {
        ref,
        strategy,
        button: buttonArg,
        click_count: clickCountArg,
        show_cursor: showCursor
      };
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/click', body);
      console.log(`clicked ${data.clicked} (${data.strategy}, ${data.button}×${data.clicks})`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'perform_secondary_action') {
      if (hasFlag('pixel')) {
        const xi = args.indexOf('--pixel');
        const x = parseFloat(args[xi + 1]);
        const y = parseFloat(args[xi + 2]);
        if (!Number.isFinite(x) || !Number.isFinite(y)) {
          console.error('Usage: alma cu perform_secondary_action --pixel <x> <y> [--pid=N]');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        const body = {
          x,
          y,
          show_cursor: showCursor
        };
        const p = flag('pid');
        if (p) body.pid = parseInt(p, 10);
        await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/perform_secondary_action', body);
        console.log(`secondary click @${x},${y}`);
        return;
      }
      const ref = args[2];
      if (!ref) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu perform_secondary_action <ref> | --pixel <x> <y>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/perform_secondary_action', {
        ref,
        show_cursor: showCursor
      });
      console.log(`secondary click on ${ref}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'drag') {
      const x1 = parseFloat(args[2]);
      const y1 = parseFloat(args[3]);
      const x2 = parseFloat(args[4]);
      const y2 = parseFloat(args[5]);
      if (!Number.isFinite(x1) || !Number.isFinite(y1) || !Number.isFinite(x2) || !Number.isFinite(y2)) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu drag <x1> <y1> <x2> <y2> [--pid=N] [--button=...]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        start_x: x1,
        start_y: y1,
        end_x: x2,
        end_y: y2,
        button: buttonArg,
        show_cursor: showCursor
      };
      const p = flag('pid');
      if (p) body.pid = parseInt(p, 10);
      await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/drag', body);
      console.log(`dragged ${x1},${y1} → ${x2},${y2}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'type_text') {
      const text = args[2];
      if (text === undefined) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu type_text <text> [--pid=N] [--bundle=ID] [--ref=eN] [--input-method=auto|physical|unicode|ax]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        text
      };
      const p = flag('pid');
      if (p) body.pid = parseInt(p, 10);
      const b = flag('bundle');
      if (b) body.bundle = b;
      const r = flag('ref');
      if (r) body.ref = r;
      const m = flag('input-method') || flag('input_method');
      if (m) body.input_method = m;
      await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/type_text', body);
      console.log(`typed ${text.length} chars`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'press_key') {
      const combo = args[2];
      if (!combo) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu press_key <combo> [--pid=N]  e.g. cmd+s, ctrl+shift+t, Return');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        key: combo
      };
      const p = flag('pid');
      if (p) body.pid = parseInt(p, 10);
      await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/press_key', body);
      console.log(`pressed ${combo}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'set_value') {
      const ref = args[2];
      const raw = args[3];
      if (!ref || raw === undefined) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu set_value <ref> <value>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      // Coerce number / bool / fallback to string.
      let value;
      if (raw === 'true') value = true;else if (raw === 'false') value = false;else if (!Number.isNaN(Number(raw)) && raw.trim() !== '') value = Number(raw);else value = raw;
      await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/set_value', {
        ref,
        value
      });
      console.log(`set ${ref} = ${JSON.stringify(value)}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'type') {
      const ref = args[2];
      const text = args[3];
      if (!ref || text === undefined) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu type <ref> <text> [--append] [--no-cursor]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const replace = !hasFlag('append');
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/type', {
        ref,
        text,
        replace,
        show_cursor: showCursor
      });
      console.log(`typed ${data.length} chars into ${data.typed}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'press') {
      const ref = args[2];
      const key = args[3];
      if (!ref || !key) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu press <ref> <key> [--no-cursor]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/press', {
        ref,
        key,
        show_cursor: showCursor
      });
      console.log(`pressed ${data.pressed}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'scroll') {
      const ref = args[2];
      const direction = args[3];
      if (!ref || !direction) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu scroll <ref> <up|down|left|right> [--pages=N] [--no-cursor]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const pages = parseInt(flag('pages') || '1', 10);
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/scroll', {
        ref,
        direction,
        pages,
        show_cursor: showCursor
      });
      console.log(`scrolled ${data.scrolled} x${data.pages}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'lens') {
      const mode = args[2];
      let enabled;
      if (mode === 'on' || mode === 'true' || mode === '1') enabled = true;else if (mode === 'off' || mode === 'false' || mode === '0') enabled = false;else if (mode === 'toggle' || mode === undefined) enabled = undefined;else {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu lens <on|off|toggle>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      // Toggle: read current state, flip.
      if (enabled === undefined) {
        const cur = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/lens', {});
        enabled = !cur.enabled;
      }
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/lens', {
        enabled
      });
      console.log(`lens: ${data.enabled ? 'on' : 'off'}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'raise') {
      const target = parseTarget(args[2]);
      if (!target.bundle && !target.pid) {
        console.error('Usage: alma cu raise <bundle|pid> [--window=ID]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const body = {
        ...target
      };
      const w = flag('window');
      if (w) body.window_id = parseInt(w, 10);
      await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/raise', body);
      console.log('raised');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'shot') {
      const target = parseTarget(args[2]);
      const out = flag('out') || `/tmp/alma-cu-${Date.now()}.jpg`;
      const body = {
        ...target,
        out
      };
      const w = flag('window');
      if (w) body.window_id = parseInt(w, 10);
      const mw = flag('max-width');
      if (mw) body.max_width = parseInt(mw, 10);
      const data = await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/shot', body);
      console.log(`${data.path}  ${data.width}×${data.height}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'shutdown') {
      await api('POST', '/api/computer-use/shutdown');
      console.log('daemon stopped.');
      return;
    }
    console.error(`Unknown cu subcommand: ${subcmd}. Run 'alma cu help' for usage.`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ───────────────────── mission ─────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'mission') {
    const missionsDir = _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'missions');
    const missionsFile = _path.join(missionsDir, 'missions.json');
    if (!_fs.existsSync(missionsDir)) _fs.mkdirSync(missionsDir, {
      recursive: true
    });
    const loadMissions = () => {
      try {
        return JSON.parse(_fs.readFileSync(missionsFile, 'utf-8'));
      } catch {
        return [];
      }
    };
    const saveMissions = missions => {
      _fs.writeFileSync(missionsFile, JSON.stringify(missions, null, 2) + '\n');
    };
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'create') {
      const desc = args[2];
      if (!desc) {
        console.error('Usage: alma mission create "description" [--goals "g1" "g2" ...]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const goalsIdx = args.indexOf('--goals');
      const goals = goalsIdx >= 0 ? args.slice(goalsIdx + 1).map((g, i) => ({
        id: i + 1,
        text: g,
        status: 'pending'
      })) : [];
      const id = 'm-' + Date.now().toString(36);
      const mission = {
        id,
        description: desc,
        status: 'pending',
        goals,
        agents: [],
        logs: [],
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        completedAt: null,
        summary: null
      };
      const missions = loadMissions();
      missions.push(mission);
      saveMissions(missions);
      console.log(JSON.stringify({
        id,
        description: desc,
        goals: goals.length
      }));
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'list') {
      const all = args.includes('--all');
      const missions = loadMissions().filter(m => all || m.status === 'active');
      for (const m of missions) {
        const done = m.goals.filter(g => g.status === 'done').length;
        const total = m.goals.length;
        const icon = m.status === 'active' ? '🟢' : m.status === 'completed' ? '✅' : '❌';
        console.log(`${icon} ${m.id} — "${m.description}" [${done}/${total} goals] ${m.agents.length} agents`);
      }
      if (missions.length === 0) console.log('No missions.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'status') {
      const mid = args[2];
      if (!mid) {
        console.error('Usage: alma mission status <missionId>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const mission = loadMissions().find(m => m.id === mid);
      if (!mission) {
        console.error(`Mission ${mid} not found`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      console.log(JSON.stringify(mission, null, 2));
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'progress') {
      const mid = args[2];
      const goalIdx = args.indexOf('--goal');
      const statusIdx = args.indexOf('--status');
      const noteIdx = args.indexOf('--note');
      if (!mid || goalIdx < 0 || statusIdx < 0) {
        console.error('Usage: alma mission progress <missionId> --goal <num> --status <pending|in-progress|done|blocked> [--note "..."]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const missions = loadMissions();
      const mission = missions.find(m => m.id === mid);
      if (!mission) {
        console.error(`Mission ${mid} not found`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const goalNum = parseInt(args[goalIdx + 1]);
      const goal = mission.goals.find(g => g.id === goalNum);
      if (!goal) {
        console.error(`Goal ${goalNum} not found`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      goal.status = args[statusIdx + 1];
      if (noteIdx >= 0) goal.note = args[noteIdx + 1];
      mission.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      saveMissions(missions);
      console.log(`Goal ${goalNum} → ${goal.status}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'assign') {
      const mid = args[2];
      const agentIdx = args.indexOf('--agent');
      const roleIdx = args.indexOf('--role');
      if (!mid || agentIdx < 0) {
        console.error('Usage: alma mission assign <missionId> --agent <taskId> [--role "..."]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const missions = loadMissions();
      const mission = missions.find(m => m.id === mid);
      if (!mission) {
        console.error(`Mission ${mid} not found`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      mission.agents.push({
        taskId: args[agentIdx + 1],
        role: roleIdx >= 0 ? args[roleIdx + 1] : 'general',
        assignedAt: new Date().toISOString()
      });
      mission.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      saveMissions(missions);
      console.log(`Agent ${args[agentIdx + 1]} assigned to ${mid}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'activate' || subcmd === 'start') {
      const mid = args[2];
      if (!mid) {
        console.error(`Usage: alma mission activate <missionId>`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const missions = loadMissions();
      const mission = missions.find(m => m.id === mid);
      if (!mission) {
        console.error(`Mission ${mid} not found`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      mission.status = 'active';
      mission.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      saveMissions(missions);
      console.log(`Mission ${mid} → active`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'complete' || subcmd === 'cancel') {
      const mid = args[2];
      if (!mid) {
        console.error(`Usage: alma mission ${subcmd} <missionId> [--summary/--reason "..."]`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const missions = loadMissions();
      const mission = missions.find(m => m.id === mid);
      if (!mission) {
        console.error(`Mission ${mid} not found`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      mission.status = subcmd === 'complete' ? 'completed' : 'cancelled';
      mission.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      mission.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      const summaryIdx = args.indexOf('--summary');
      const reasonIdx = args.indexOf('--reason');
      if (summaryIdx >= 0) mission.summary = args[summaryIdx + 1];
      if (reasonIdx >= 0) mission.summary = args[reasonIdx + 1];
      saveMissions(missions);
      console.log(`Mission ${mid} → ${mission.status}`);
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'log') {
      const mid = args[2];
      const msg = args[3];
      if (!mid || !msg) {
        console.error('Usage: alma mission log <missionId> "message"');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const missions = loadMissions();
      const mission = missions.find(m => m.id === mid);
      if (!mission) {
        console.error(`Mission ${mid} not found`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      mission.logs.push({
        text: msg,
        at: new Date().toISOString()
      });
      mission.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      saveMissions(missions);
      console.log('Logged.');
      return;
    }
    console.error(`Unknown mission subcommand: ${subcmd}. Commands: create, list, status, progress, assign, complete, cancel, log`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ───────────────────── comms ─────────────────────
  if (cmd === 'comms') {
    const commsDir = _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'missions', 'comms');
    const dmDir = _path.join(commsDir, 'dm');
    if (!_fs.existsSync(commsDir)) _fs.mkdirSync(commsDir, {
      recursive: true
    });
    if (!_fs.existsSync(dmDir)) _fs.mkdirSync(dmDir, {
      recursive: true
    });
    const subcmd = args[1];
    if (subcmd === 'send') {
      const target = args[2]; // missionId
      const msg = args[3];
      if (!target || !msg) {
        console.error('Usage: alma comms send <missionId> "message"');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const channelFile = _path.join(commsDir, `${target}.jsonl`);
      const entry = JSON.stringify({
        from: process.env.ALMA_AGENT_ID || 'alma',
        text: msg,
        at: new Date().toISOString()
      });
      _fs.appendFileSync(channelFile, entry + '\n');
      console.log('Sent.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'dm') {
      const targetAgent = args[2];
      const msg = args[3];
      if (!targetAgent || !msg) {
        console.error('Usage: alma comms dm <agentTaskId> "message"');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const dmFile = _path.join(dmDir, `${targetAgent}.jsonl`);
      const entry = JSON.stringify({
        from: process.env.ALMA_AGENT_ID || 'alma',
        text: msg,
        at: new Date().toISOString()
      });
      _fs.appendFileSync(dmFile, entry + '\n');
      console.log('DM sent.');
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'read') {
      const target = args[2];
      if (!target) {
        console.error('Usage: alma comms read <missionId> [--limit N]');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const channelFile = _path.join(commsDir, `${target}.jsonl`);
      if (!_fs.existsSync(channelFile)) {
        console.log('No messages.');
        return;
      }
      const limitIdx = args.indexOf('--limit');
      const limit = limitIdx >= 0 ? parseInt(args[limitIdx + 1]) : 20;
      const lines = _fs.readFileSync(channelFile, 'utf-8').trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean);
      const msgs = lines.slice(-limit);
      for (const line of msgs) {
        try {
          const m = JSON.parse(line);
          console.log(`[${m.at}] ${m.from}: ${m.text}`);
        } catch {
          console.log(line);
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'inbox') {
      const agentId = args[2] || process.env.ALMA_AGENT_ID || 'alma';
      const dmFile = _path.join(dmDir, `${agentId}.jsonl`);
      if (!_fs.existsSync(dmFile)) {
        console.log('No DMs.');
        return;
      }
      const lines = _fs.readFileSync(dmFile, 'utf-8').trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean);
      for (const line of lines.slice(-20)) {
        try {
          const m = JSON.parse(line);
          console.log(`[${m.at}] ${m.from}: ${m.text}`);
        } catch {
          console.log(line);
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if (subcmd === 'broadcast') {
      const msg = args[2];
      if (!msg) {
        console.error('Usage: alma comms broadcast "message"');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      const missionsFile = _path.join(_os.homedir(), '.config', 'alma', 'missions', 'missions.json');
      let missions = [];
      try {
        missions = JSON.parse(_fs.readFileSync(missionsFile, 'utf-8'));
      } catch {}
      const active = missions.filter(m => m.status === 'active');
      for (const m of active) {
        const channelFile = _path.join(commsDir, `${m.id}.jsonl`);
        const entry = JSON.stringify({
          from: 'alma-broadcast',
          text: msg,
          at: new Date().toISOString()
        });
        _fs.appendFileSync(channelFile, entry + '\n');
      }
      console.log(`Broadcast to ${active.length} missions.`);
      return;
    }
    console.error(`Unknown comms subcommand: ${subcmd}. Commands: send, dm, read, inbox, broadcast`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // ── TUI (Terminal User Interface) ────
  if (cmd === 'tui') {
    const {
      execFileSync
    } = await import('child_process');
    const _fs = await import('fs');
    const tuiEnv = {
      ...process.env,
      ALMA_API_URL: BASE_URL
    };

    // Try bundled single-file version first (production)
    const tuiBundled = _path.join(__alma_dirname, 'tui.mjs');
    if (_fs.existsSync(tuiBundled)) {
      try {
        execFileSync(process.execPath, [tuiBundled], {
          stdio: 'inherit',
          env: tuiEnv
        });
      } catch (e) {
        if (e.status) process.exit(e.status);
      }
      process.exit(0);
    }

    // Fall back to dev mode (source with tsx)
    const tuiDev = _path.join(__alma_dirname, 'tui', 'index.tsx');
    try {
      execFileSync('node', ['--import', 'tsx', tuiDev], {
        stdio: 'inherit',
        env: tuiEnv
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (e.status) process.exit(e.status);
    }
    process.exit(0);
  }

  // ── Run (pipe-friendly AI completion via full chat pipeline) ────
  if (cmd === 'run') {
    // Handle SIGPIPE gracefully (downstream pipe closed early, e.g. `alma run '...' | head -5`)
    process.on('SIGPIPE', () => process.exit(0));
    process.stdout.on('error', err => {
      if (err.code === 'EPIPE') process.exit(0);
      throw err;
    });

    // Parse options
    let model = null;
    let systemPrompt = null;
    let temperature = null;
    let raw = false;
    let noStream = false;
    let listModels = false;
    let verbose = false;
    let timeoutSeconds = 300; // inactivity timeout; 0 disables
    // Tool selection sent with generate_response. Omitting it is NOT "all
    // tools": the server treats a missing selection as empty and the model
    // only gets the skill baseline (Bash + Skill, ToolSearch dormant without
    // a tool model) — no Read/Write/Edit/Grep/Glob. The composer always sends
    // either an explicit list or ['__auto__']; the CLI now does the same.
    const DEFAULT_RUN_TOOLS = ['Bash', 'BashOutput', 'KillShell', 'Read', 'Write', 'Edit', 'Glob', 'Grep'];
    let toolKeys = DEFAULT_RUN_TOOLS;
    const promptParts = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < args.length; i++) {
      if ((args[i] === '-m' || args[i] === '--model') && args[i + 1]) {
        model = args[++i];
      } else if ((args[i] === '-s' || args[i] === '--system') && args[i + 1]) {
        systemPrompt = args[++i];
      } else if (args[i] === '--timeout' && args[i + 1]) {
        timeoutSeconds = parseInt(args[++i], 10);
        if (isNaN(timeoutSeconds) || timeoutSeconds < 0) {
          console.error('Error: --timeout must be a non-negative number of seconds (0 disables)');
          process.exit(1);
        }
      } else if ((args[i] === '-t' || args[i] === '--temperature') && args[i + 1]) {
        temperature = parseFloat(args[++i]);
        if (isNaN(temperature) || temperature < 0 || temperature > 2) {
          console.error('Error: temperature must be between 0 and 2');
          process.exit(1);
        }
      } else if (args[i] === '--raw') {
        raw = true;
      } else if (args[i] === '--no-stream') {
        noStream = true;
      } else if (args[i] === '-l' || args[i] === '--list-models') {
        listModels = true;
      } else if (args[i] === '-v' || args[i] === '--verbose') {
        verbose = true;
      } else if (args[i] === '--tools' && args[i + 1]) {
        const spec = args[++i].trim();
        if (spec === 'auto') toolKeys = ['__auto__'];else if (spec === 'none') toolKeys = undefined;else {
          toolKeys = spec.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);
          if (!toolKeys.length) {
            console.error('Error: --tools expects a comma-separated tool list, "auto" or "none"');
            process.exit(1);
          }
        }
      } else if (!args[i].startsWith('-')) {
        promptParts.push(args[i]);
      }
    }

    // Handle --list-models: fetch and display available models, then exit
    if (listModels) {
      try {
        const modelsResp = await api('GET', '/api/models');
        const models = modelsResp?.data || modelsResp || [];
        if (!models.length) {
          console.error('No models available. Check your provider configuration.');
          process.exit(1);
        }
        // Group models by provider
        const byProvider = {};
        for (const m of models) {
          const key = m.providerId || 'unknown';
          if (!byProvider[key]) {
            byProvider[key] = {
              name: m.providerName || key,
              models: []
            };
          }
          byProvider[key].models.push(m);
        }
        for (const [providerId, group] of Object.entries(byProvider)) {
          console.log(`\nProvider: ${group.name} (${providerId})`);
          for (const m of group.models) {
            const displayId = m.id || m.name;
            console.log(`  ${providerId}:${displayId}`);
          }
        }
        console.log('');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error: Failed to fetch models.');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    const promptArg = promptParts.join(' ');

    // Read stdin if piped (non-TTY), with timeout to avoid hanging
    let stdinContent = '';
    if (!process.stdin.isTTY) {
      stdinContent = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const chunks = [];
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
          process.stdin.destroy();
          resolve(chunks.join(''));
        }, 10000); // 10s timeout
        process.stdin.setEncoding('utf-8');
        process.stdin.on('data', chunk => chunks.push(chunk));
        process.stdin.on('end', () => {
          clearTimeout(timer);
          resolve(chunks.join(''));
        });
        process.stdin.on('error', err => {
          clearTimeout(timer);
          reject(err);
        });
      });
    }

    // Must have at least one of stdin or prompt
    if (!stdinContent.trim() && !promptArg.trim()) {
      console.error(`Usage: alma run [options] [prompt]

Pipe-friendly AI completion. Uses the full Alma chat pipeline (memory, tools, SOUL, plugins).

Examples:
  cat log.txt | alma run '分析这个日志中的错误'
  alma run '写一个 hello world Python 脚本'
  git diff | alma run '写一个 commit message' > commit_msg.txt
  cat code.py | alma run -s '你是代码审查专家' '审查这段代码'

Options:
  -m, --model <provider:model>   Override default model
  -s, --system <prompt>          Prepend a system instruction to message
  -t, --temperature <n>          Set temperature (0-2, via settings)
  --timeout <seconds>            Inactivity timeout (default 300; 0 = wait forever)
  --raw                          Strip markdown from output
  --no-stream                    Buffer output, print at end
  -l, --list-models              List available models and exit
  -v, --verbose                  Show thinking and tool calls (auto-enabled in TTY)`);
      process.exit(1);
    }

    // Build user message text
    let userText;
    if (stdinContent.trim() && promptArg.trim()) {
      userText = `<context>\n${stdinContent.trim()}\n</context>\n\n${promptArg}`;
    } else if (stdinContent.trim()) {
      userText = stdinContent.trim();
    } else {
      userText = promptArg;
    }

    // If system prompt provided via -s, prepend it to user message
    // (the backend's generateChatResponse builds its own system prompt from SOUL.md etc.)
    if (systemPrompt) {
      userText = `[System instruction: ${systemPrompt}]\n\n${userText}`;
    }

    // Get default model from settings if not specified
    if (!model) {
      try {
        const settings = await api('GET', '/api/settings');
        model = settings?.chat?.defaultModel;
      } catch {
        // api() already exits on failure
      }
    }
    if (!model) {
      console.error('Error: No model specified and no default model configured.');
      console.error('Use -m <provider:model> or set a default: alma model set <provider:model>');
      process.exit(1);
    }

    // ── Resolve human-readable provider name to provider ID ──
    // Supports: 'anthropic:claude-sonnet-4-20250514', 'Ollama:qwen3:8b', 'Antigravity:gpt-4o'
    // Instead of requiring: 'mk496vlh6capo8m9exa:gpt-4o'
    {
      const colonIdx = model.indexOf(':');
      if (colonIdx > 0) {
        const providerPart = model.substring(0, colonIdx);
        const modelPart = model.substring(colonIdx + 1);
        try {
          const providers = await api('GET', '/api/providers');
          const providerList = Array.isArray(providers) ? providers : providers?.data || [];

          // Check if providerPart already matches a provider ID directly
          const directMatch = providerList.find(p => p.id === providerPart);
          if (!directMatch) {
            // Try to resolve by name or type (case-insensitive)
            const lowerPart = providerPart.toLowerCase();
            const candidates = providerList.filter(p => p.enabled !== false && (p.type?.toLowerCase() === lowerPart || p.id?.toLowerCase() === lowerPart || p.name?.toLowerCase() === lowerPart || p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(lowerPart)));
            if (candidates.length === 1) {
              model = `${candidates[0].id}:${modelPart}`;
            } else if (candidates.length > 1) {
              console.error(`Multiple providers match "${providerPart}":`);
              for (const c of candidates) {
                console.error(`  ${c.id}  ${c.name} (${c.type})`);
              }
              console.error(`\nUse a more specific name, or the full ID: alma run -m '${candidates[0].id}:${modelPart}'`);
              process.exit(1);
            }
            // If no candidates found, proceed with original string (backend will error)
          }
        } catch {
          // If provider fetch fails, proceed with original model string
        }
      }
    }

    // Strip basic markdown formatting from text
    function stripMarkdown(text) {
      return text.replace(/^#{1,6}\s+/gm, '') // headings
      .replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, '$1') // bold
      .replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g, '$1') // italic
      .replace(/__(.+?)__/g, '$1') // bold alt
      .replace(/_(.+?)_/g, '$1') // italic alt
      .replace(/~~(.+?)~~/g, '$1') // strikethrough
      .replace(/`{3}[\s\S]*?`{3}/g, m => m.replace(/^```\w*\n?/, '').replace(/\n?```$/, '')) // code blocks
      .replace(/`(.+?)`/g, '$1') // inline code
      .replace(/^\s*[-*+]\s+/gm, '  - ') // list items (normalize)
      .replace(/^\s*>\s+/gm, ''); // blockquotes
    }

    // Step 1: Create a temporary thread for the pipeline
    let threadId;
    try {
      const title = (promptArg || stdinContent).slice(0, 50).replace(/\n/g, ' ') || 'alma run';
      const thread = await api('POST', '/api/threads', {
        title: `[run] ${title}`
      });
      threadId = thread.id;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`Error: Failed to create thread: ${err.message}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }

    // Step 2: Connect to WebSocket and run generation via the full chat pipeline
    let WS;
    try {
      WS = (await import('ws')).default;
    } catch {
      if (typeof WebSocket !== 'undefined') {
        WS = WebSocket; // Node 22+ built-in
      } else {
        console.error('Error: WebSocket support not available');
        process.exit(1);
      }
    }
    const wsUrl = BASE_URL.replace(/^http/, 'ws') + '/ws/threads';
    let ws;
    try {
      ws = new WS(wsUrl);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`Error: Cannot connect to Alma WebSocket at ${wsUrl}`);
      console.error('Is Alma running? Start it with: npm run dev (in the alma directory)');
      // Clean up thread
      try {
        await api('DELETE', `/api/threads/${threadId}`);
      } catch {}
      process.exit(1);
    }

    // Track state
    let fullText = '';
    let hadError = false;
    let timedOut = false;
    const timeoutMs = timeoutSeconds * 1000;
    let inThinking = false; // tracks if we're inside a <think> block
    let inNativeReasoning = false; // tracks if we're in a reasoning part
    const showVerbose = verbose || process.stderr.isTTY;
    const useColor = process.stderr.isTTY;
    const dim = useColor ? '\x1b[2m' : '';
    const cyan = useColor ? '\x1b[36m' : '';
    const red = useColor ? '\x1b[31m' : '';
    const green = useColor ? '\x1b[32m' : '';
    const yellow = useColor ? '\x1b[33m' : '';
    const reset = useColor ? '\x1b[0m' : '';
    const seenProgress = new Set(); // deduplicate analysis progress events
    let lastOutputWasText = false; // track if we need newline before tool indicator
    const toolNames = {}; // partIndex → tool name
    const toolInputs = {}; // partIndex → accumulated input

    const done = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      // INACTIVITY timeout, not wall-clock: long agentic runs (tool loops, sprint
      // harness missions) legitimately exceed any fixed total budget while still
      // streaming progress. Only give up when the stream goes silent. A timeout is
      // a FAILURE (exit 1) and must not delete the thread — the server-side
      // generation may well still be running and complete on its own.
      let timeout = null;
      const armTimeout = () => {
        if (timeoutMs <= 0) return;
        if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
        timeout = setTimeout(() => {
          console.error(`Error: No generation activity for ${Math.round(timeoutMs / 60000)} min — giving up. ` + `The server may still finish this run; thread ${threadId} is kept for inspection.`);
          hadError = true;
          timedOut = true;
          ws.close();
        }, timeoutMs);
      };
      armTimeout();
      ws.on('error', err => {
        console.error(`Error: WebSocket connection failed: ${err.message}`);
        if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
        reject(err);
      });
      ws.on('close', () => {
        if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
        resolve();
      });
      ws.on('open', () => {
        // Send generate_response — this triggers the same pipeline as the GUI
        ws.send(JSON.stringify({
          type: 'generate_response',
          data: {
            threadId,
            model,
            ...(toolKeys ? {
              tools: toolKeys
            } : {}),
            userMessage: {
              role: 'user',
              parts: [{
                type: 'text',
                text: userText
              }]
            }
          }
        }));
      });
      ws.on('message', data => {
        try {
          const msg = JSON.parse(data.toString());
          const {
            type,
            data: payload
          } = msg;

          // Any event for our thread counts as activity — re-arm the inactivity timer.
          if (payload?.threadId === threadId || payload?.id === threadId) {
            armTimeout();
          }
          if (type === 'message_delta' && payload?.threadId === threadId) {
            // Streaming text deltas
            if (Array.isArray(payload.deltas)) {
              for (const delta of payload.deltas) {
                // ── Regular text (may contain <think> tags) ──
                if (delta.type === 'text_append' && delta.partType === 'text') {
                  let text = delta.text;

                  // Parse <think>...</think> within text stream
                  while (text.length > 0) {
                    if (inThinking) {
                      // Look for </think> end tag
                      const endIdx = text.indexOf('</think>');
                      if (endIdx >= 0) {
                        // Output thinking portion
                        const thinkPart = text.slice(0, endIdx);
                        if (showVerbose && thinkPart) {
                          process.stderr.write(`${dim}${thinkPart}${reset}`);
                        }
                        process.stderr.write(`${reset}\n`);
                        inThinking = false;
                        text = text.slice(endIdx + '</think>'.length);
                        // Skip leading whitespace/newlines after </think>
                        text = text.replace(/^\n+/, '');
                      } else {
                        // Still inside thinking, output all remaining to stderr
                        if (showVerbose && text) {
                          process.stderr.write(`${dim}${text}${reset}`);
                        }
                        text = '';
                      }
                    } else {
                      // Look for <think> start tag
                      const startIdx = text.indexOf('<think>');
                      if (startIdx >= 0) {
                        // Output normal text before <think>
                        const normalPart = text.slice(0, startIdx);
                        if (normalPart) {
                          if (noStream) {
                            fullText += normalPart;
                            lastOutputWasText = true;
                          } else {
                            try {
                              process.stdout.write(raw ? stripMarkdown(normalPart) : normalPart);
                              lastOutputWasText = true;
                            } catch (e) {
                              if (e.code === 'EPIPE') {
                                ws.close();
                                return;
                              }
                              throw e;
                            }
                          }
                        }
                        // Enter thinking mode
                        inThinking = true;
                        if (showVerbose) {
                          process.stderr.write(`${dim}▸ `);
                        }
                        text = text.slice(startIdx + '<think>'.length);
                        // Skip leading newline after <think>
                        text = text.replace(/^\n/, '');
                      } else {
                        // No <think> tag, output as normal text
                        if (noStream) {
                          fullText += text;
                          lastOutputWasText = true;
                        } else {
                          try {
                            process.stdout.write(raw ? stripMarkdown(text) : text);
                            lastOutputWasText = true;
                          } catch (e) {
                            if (e.code === 'EPIPE') {
                              ws.close();
                              return;
                            }
                            throw e;
                          }
                        }
                        text = '';
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }

                // ── Native reasoning (Claude, etc.) ──
                if (delta.type === 'text_append' && delta.partType === 'reasoning' && showVerbose) {
                  if (!inNativeReasoning) {
                    process.stderr.write(`${dim}▸ `);
                    inNativeReasoning = true;
                  }
                  process.stderr.write(`${dim}${delta.text}${reset}`);
                }
                if (delta.type === 'text_done' && delta.partType === 'reasoning' && inNativeReasoning) {
                  process.stderr.write(`${reset}\n`);
                  inNativeReasoning = false;
                }

                // ── Tool invocation ──
                if (delta.type === 'part_add' && showVerbose) {
                  const part = delta.part;
                  if (part?.type?.startsWith('tool-')) {
                    const toolName = part.type.slice(5); // Remove "tool-" prefix
                    if (typeof delta.partIndex !== 'undefined') {
                      toolNames[delta.partIndex] = toolName;
                    }
                    if (lastOutputWasText) {
                      process.stderr.write(`\n`);
                      lastOutputWasText = false;
                    }
                    process.stderr.write(`${cyan}▸ ${toolName}${reset}\n`);
                  } else if (part?.type === 'tool-invocation') {
                    const toolName = part.toolName || 'unknown';
                    if (typeof delta.partIndex !== 'undefined') {
                      toolNames[delta.partIndex] = toolName;
                    }
                    if (lastOutputWasText) {
                      process.stderr.write(`\n`);
                      lastOutputWasText = false;
                    }
                    process.stderr.write(`${cyan}▸ ${toolName}${reset}\n`);
                  }
                }

                // ── Tool output ──
                if (delta.type === 'tool_output_set' && showVerbose) {
                  const output = delta.output;
                  if (delta.state === 'output-available' && output) {
                    if (output.file_path) {
                      // Write tool
                      const filePath = output.file_path.replace(/^(\.\.\/)+/, '/');
                      process.stderr.write(`${dim}  ✓ Wrote ${filePath} (${output.bytes_written} bytes)${reset}\n`);
                    } else if ('exitCode' in output) {
                      // Bash tool
                      const status = output.exitCode === 0 ? `${green}✓ exit 0` : `${red}✗ exit ${output.exitCode}`;
                      const dur = output.durationMs ? ` (${output.durationMs}ms)` : '';
                      process.stderr.write(`${dim}  ${status}${dur}${reset}\n`);
                    } else if (output.result !== undefined) {
                      // AttemptCompletion — output result text to STDOUT
                      process.stderr.write(`${dim}  ✓ Done${reset}\n`);
                      if (output.result) {
                        try {
                          process.stdout.write('\n' + output.result + '\n');
                        } catch (e) {
                          if (e.code === 'EPIPE') {
                            ws.close();
                            return;
                          }
                        }
                      }
                    } else {
                      // Generic tool output
                      const brief = JSON.stringify(output).slice(0, 100);
                      process.stderr.write(`${dim}  ✓ ${brief}${reset}\n`);
                    }
                  } else if (delta.state === 'output-error') {
                    process.stderr.write(`${red}  ✗ ${delta.errorText || 'Tool error'}${reset}\n`);
                  }
                }

                // ── Tool streaming output (bash stdout/stderr) ──
                if (delta.type === 'tool_output_streaming' && showVerbose) {
                  if (delta.stream?.stdout) {
                    const lines = delta.stream.stdout;
                    process.stderr.write(`${dim}${lines}${reset}`);
                  }
                  if (delta.stream?.stderr) {
                    process.stderr.write(`${red}${delta.stream.stderr}${reset}`);
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }

          // Show analysis progress
          if (showVerbose) {
            if (type === 'tool_analysis_progress' && payload?.threadId === threadId) {
              if (!seenProgress.has('tools')) {
                seenProgress.add('tools');
                process.stderr.write(`${dim}• Analyzing tools...${reset}\n`);
              }
            }
            if (type === 'memory_retrieval_progress' && payload?.threadId === threadId) {
              if (!seenProgress.has('memory')) {
                seenProgress.add('memory');
                process.stderr.write(`${dim}• Retrieving memories...${reset}\n`);
              }
            }
            if (type === 'skill_analysis_progress' && payload?.threadId === threadId) {
              if (!seenProgress.has('skills')) {
                seenProgress.add('skills');
                process.stderr.write(`${dim}• Analyzing skills...${reset}\n`);
              }
            }
          }
          if (type === 'generation_error' && payload?.threadId === threadId) {
            const errMsg = payload?.error || 'Unknown generation error';
            console.error(`Error: ${errMsg}`);
            hadError = true;
          }
          if (type === 'thread_generating' && payload?.id === threadId && payload?.isGenerating === false) {
            // Generation is fully done
            if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
            ws.close();
          }
        } catch {}
      });
    });
    try {
      await done;
    } catch {
      // WebSocket error already logged
    }

    // Output buffered text if --no-stream
    if (noStream && fullText) {
      let output = raw ? stripMarkdown(fullText) : fullText;
      process.stdout.write(output);
    }

    // Print trailing newline for clean terminal output
    if (process.stdout.isTTY || noStream) {
      process.stdout.write('\n');
    }

    // Clean up: delete temporary thread (fire and forget). On timeout the server-side
    // generation may still be running — deleting the thread could kill it mid-flight,
    // so keep it (the id was printed in the timeout message).
    if (!timedOut) {
      try {
        await api('DELETE', `/api/threads/${threadId}`);
      } catch {}
    }
    process.exit(hadError ? 1 : 0);
  }
  console.error(`Unknown command: ${cmd}. Run 'alma help' for usage.`);
  process.exit(1);
}
main();
